================================================================================
UI CONTENT AND WRITING STANDARD (PERMANENT PROJECT CONSTRAINT, added 2026-10-04)
================================================================================
This is a HARD constraint on every future implementation, not a style preference.
Every task that introduces or modifies user-facing text (new features, bug fixes,
redesigns, refactors, empty states, errors, notifications, forms, dashboards,
customer-facing pages, admin pages, emails, toasts, any rendered UI text) must
follow it, and new copy must be reviewed against it before the implementation is
considered complete.

1. All user-facing UI copy must read as professional, natural, human-written
   content: specific to DeChimexPrints, the screen, the audience (customer vs
   staff vs developer), and the action the user is taking.
2. Do NOT use em dashes (—) or en dashes (–) in UI copy. If a sentence was
   structured around the dash, rewrite the sentence; do not just swap in a comma.
3. Do NOT use recognizable AI-writing patterns or formulaic structures:
   "Whether X, Y or Z...", "From X to Y..." (unless a real range),
   "Designed to...", "Built to...", "Everything you need to...", "Seamlessly",
   "Effortlessly", "Unlock", "Elevate", "Transform", "Streamline", "Take control",
   "one-stop", "all-in-one", "your journey", "next-level", "world-class", generic
   motivational or corporate filler, artificially symmetrical lists, and repeated
   sentence rhythms.
4. Do NOT use generic SaaS filler or placeholder copy ("Manage everything in one
   place", "Get started today", "Coming soon", "No data available", "Welcome to
   your dashboard", "Something went wrong" as a lazy 500 title, etc.).
5. Do NOT mechanically synonym-swap AI-sounding text. Rewrite the sentence or
   paragraph structurally so it says what the screen actually needs to say.
6. Empty states must say what is empty, why, and what to do next. Errors must say
   what failed and what to try, without inventing causes the system does not know
   (admin surfaces may keep technical detail useful for troubleshooting).
7. Buttons are short, direct and action-oriented ("Add Product", "Place order",
   "Approve Design"); no promotional or verbose buttons.
8. Headings state the purpose of the page or section. Do not attach marketing
   subtitles to fill space; if a heading needs no subtitle, give it none.
9. Helper text only when it answers a question the user is likely to have.
10. Never invent business facts, statistics, awards, guarantees, customer numbers,
    testimonials, product specifications or capabilities. Real figures come from
    owner settings or database data only. EXCEPTION: the seeded business stats
    (business.stat_projects "2,400+", business.stat_brands "180+",
    business.stat_services "12+", business.stat_turnaround "48h") are
    OWNER-APPROVED DEFAULT DISPLAY VALUES for the hero/About stats band; keep
    them rendering exactly as seeded unless the owner changes them in Settings.
11. Preserve established product terminology (e.g. "ready-to-buy" vs
    "made-to-order", status names, "Creativity with Ease" motto, role names).
    Improve the sentence around the term, not the term.
12. Payment and money copy must be explicit: what is being paid, why, when, and
    what happens next. No clever phrasing on prices, fees, refunds or failures.
13. Keep copy concise enough to render well at 320 px; rewrite long sentences
    rather than stretching layouts.
14. Reference copy lives in the codebase: the storefront hero, portal dashboard,
    checkout and auth screens are good examples of the intended voice. When in
    doubt, match their plainness and specificity.

See also CONTRIBUTING.md ("Content and copy") which points here.

0. MANDATORY CONTRACT FOR ANY FUTURE AI SESSION
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Before modifying ANYTHING, a new AI session MUST:
  1. Clone the repository.
  2. Read this AI_HANDOFF.txt file.
  3. Verify every statement here against the actual codebase.
  4. Treat the codebase as the ULTIMATE source of truth. If this document and the
     code disagree, the CODE wins — and then update this document to fix the
     discrepancy.
  5. Continue from the current state rather than repeating completed work (the
     CHANGELOG.md and REFINEMENT_PROGRESS.md record what was already done).
  6. Maintain (keep up to date) AI_HANDOFF.txt after every meaningful change.
  7. Update or remove stale information instead of letting contradictory
     project-state details accumulate.

After every meaningful development change, update THIS file during the same task:
capture what was requested, what was implemented, affected files/components,
business rules changed, architectural changes, bugs fixed, tests added/updated,
verification performed, current build/test state, remaining work, newly
discovered issues, and important decisions. If a previous statement becomes
obsolete, edit/remove it.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
1. PROJECT OVERVIEW
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Product: "DE-CHIMEX PRINT HUB" — "Creativity with Ease."
Business problem: a Nigerian graphics / branding / printing / promotional-items
business (De-Chimex Graphics, Abeokuta + Lagos) needs a full business-management
and e-commerce platform. It manages a public storefront (products & services),
customer accounts/portal, a staff admin dashboard (catalog, orders, quotations,
payments & receipts, invoices, inventory, procurement/suppliers, customers,
gallery, notifications, reports/analytics/activity), and external integrations
(Paystack payments, Brevo transactional email, WhatsApp). Employees/roles and
permission-based access control are central.

The application was originally built as a pure JSON Web API with a separate
vanilla-JS single-page frontend (SPA). It was later converted to ASP.NET Core MVC
as the primary user interface, with the API surface retained.

Repo layout (single consolidated solution root):
  DeChimexPrints/    -> the .NET solution (DeChimex.sln), src/, tests/, database/, docs/, postman/
  (The original vanilla-JS SPA, DeChimexPrintsFrontend/, was REMOVED on 2026-08-31; the app is
   now a single server-rendered ASP.NET Core MVC solution. No frontend folder remains.)
  * CHANGELOG.md, MVC_MIGRATION_PLAN.md, MVC_MIGRATION_REPORT.md,
    REFINEMENT_PROGRESS.md, REMEDIATION_PLAN.md, DeChimex_MVC_Manual_Test_Plan.md
    -> prior-session work logs (used to reconstruct history)

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
2. TECHNOLOGY STACK
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Framework/runtime: .NET 9 (net9.0), ASP.NET Core (MVC + Web API in one host).
- Database: SQL Server via EF Core 9. Dev/preview can use InMemory (opt-in).
- Architecture: Clean/Onion + DDD + CQRS (MediatR 12) + FluentValidation 11,
  repository + query-service read model, pipeline behaviors (logging,
  performance, validation).
- UI: Razor Views (MVC) for admin/portal/storefront; Tailwind CSS v3 build +
  custom design system (wwwroot/css/app.css, tailwind.build.css). SignalR client
  vendored for notifications. QuestPDF (community license) for invoice PDF.
- Auth: ASP.NET Core COOKIE auth only (MVC browser + the retained /api/v1 surface).
  JWT bearer, the "smart" policy scheme, ITokenService and refresh-token rotation
  were all REMOVED on 2026-09-01 (JWT-independence change; see section 22) — no
  Jwt:SigningKey is required to boot. Policy-based authorization with a dynamic
  PermissionPolicyProvider (transport-agnostic, reads claims); PBKDF2 password hashing.
- Logging: Serilog (console + rolling file), correlation IDs.
- External: Paystack (payments), Brevo (email), WhatsApp (deep links), local
  file storage for uploads.
- Testing: xUnit; unit + integration (InMemory, no SQL Server needed).

Project references (DeChimex.sln):
  src/DeChimex.Domain        (core; entities, VOs, enums, events, exceptions, repo interfaces)
  src/DeChimex.Shared        (cross-cutting: Permissions.cs, RoleNames.cs, WhatsApp.cs)
  src/DeChimex.Contracts     (DTOs + ApiResponse envelope)
  src/DeChimex.Application   (CQRS commands/queries + handlers, validators, behaviors)
  src/DeChimex.Persistence   (DbContext, EF configs, repositories, query services, migrations)
  src/DeChimex.Infrastructure (JWT/hasher, Paystack, Brevo, cache, storage, seeder)
  src/DeChimex.Host          (the deployable: controllers, MVC views, middleware,
                              Program.cs — this is the renamed DeChimex.Api project)
  tests/DeChimex.UnitTests
  tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests

Dependency direction points inward: Domain depends on nothing (only
MediatR.Contracts for the domain-event marker). Application depends on Domain +
Contracts + Shared. Infrastructure & Persistence depend on Application (they
implement its interfaces).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
3. CURRENT ARCHITECTURE (IMPORTANT — what actually exists)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- There is NO separate "DeChimex.Api" project any more. The original API host was
  renamed/refactored into src/DeChimex.Host, which is BOTH a Web API and an MVC
  app in a single deployable. Program.cs uses AddControllersWithViews().
- API controllers live directly under src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers. As of
  2026-09-05 there are only THREE of them (5 .cs files: ApiControllerBase.cs,
  SupersededUiDtos.cs + the 3 controllers below). They inherit ApiControllerBase
  ([ApiController], [Route("api/v{version:apiVersion}/[controller]")]) and are
  versioned via Asp.Versioning (default v1). The /api/v1 surface is NO LONGER the
  broad product contract it once was: 21 of the original 25 controllers were
  removed as superseded by the MVC UI (Task 2, 2026-08-30), and AuthController was
  removed with the JWT surface (2026-09-01). The three remaining /api/v1 actions are:
    GET  /api/v1/config                    (ConfigController.GetPublicConfig, anon)
    GET  /api/v1/settings/public           (SettingsController.GetPublic, anon)
    POST /api/v1/payments/webhook/paystack (PaymentsController.PaystackWebhook, anon)
- MVC controllers live under src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/ (39 controller
  classes + 4 base classes + CartBadgeViewComponent):
  Mvc/Account, Mvc/Admin/Admin* (dashboard, products, categories, services,
  orders, customers, inventory, suppliers, procurement, users/roles, settings,
  reports, analytics, gallery, invoices, receipts, messages, notifications,
  security), Mvc/Portal/Portal* (customer area, incl. PortalWishlist),
  Mvc/Store/* (StoreHome, StoreProducts, StoreServices, StoreGallery, StorePages,
  StoreCart, Checkout), Mvc/Home, Mvc/Error, Mvc/PublicInvoice.
  NOTE: there is NO AdminQuotationsController any more — the standalone Quotations
  feature was removed on 2026-09-01 (quoting now lives on the Invoice path).
- MVC and API SHARE the same Application/Domain/Persistence/Infrastructure code
  and services. MVC controllers call MediatR ISender, query services, or the
  DbContext directly — NEVER via HTTP to the app's own API. Confirmed: grep found
  NO internal HttpClient self-calls to /api in MVC or Application code.
- Reports/analytics/invoices/receipts/suppliers have no CQRS layer;
  their MVC controllers use the direct-DbContext / query-service approach
  (a deliberate decision documented in MVC_MIGRATION_REPORT).
- Swagger/OpenAPI is enabled in Development at /swagger
  (Asp.Versioning.Mvc.ApiExplorer + Swashbuckle). It documents only the /api/v1
  actions. Swagger is a development-only concern.
- API endpoints that MUST be preserved (verified 2026-09-05 — these are the ONLY
  three /api/v1 actions left):
    PaymentsController.PaystackWebhook  (Paystack server->server callback; cannot
                                         be an MVC page because Paystack posts to it)
    ConfigController.GetPublicConfig    (anon; exposes the Paystack PUBLIC key)
    SettingsController.GetPublic        (anon; storefront business info)
  CORRECTION (2026-09-05): earlier revisions listed
  "PaymentsController.Initialize/Verify (Paystack JS inline checkout flow)" here.
  Those API actions NO LONGER EXIST. The whole customer payment flow now runs
  server-side in MVC CheckoutController using Paystack's HOSTED REDIRECT flow,
  not the JS inline popup:
    POST /checkout/{orderId}/pay  -> InitializePaymentCommand -> Redirect(authorization_url)
    GET  /checkout/return/{ref}   -> VerifyPaymentCommand -> confirm order (shared
                                     pipeline with the webhook)
  No view or JS file references PaystackPop / js.paystack.co any more (the CSP in
  SecurityHeadersMiddleware still allowlists https://js.paystack.co — harmless but
  now vestigial; candidate for cleanup, see section 13).
  There is consequently NO broad /api/v1 product contract left for future
  mobile/SPA consumers — if one is needed again it must be rebuilt deliberately.
- Realtime: NotificationHub at /hubs/notifications (RequireAuthorization), with
  INotificationPusher implemented in Host over IHubContext; SignalR 8.0.7 client
  vendored in wwwroot/lib/signalr.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
4. FEATURE MAP (major modules & current flows)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Storefront (public): Home, products catalog + detail (with wishlist toggle),
  services, gallery (Instagram-style dense square grid + lightbox), about/contact/
  faq/privacy/terms, cart (server-side, in DB, route /cart), checkout
  (Paystack HOSTED REDIRECT — see section 3), /checkout/complete/{orderId} and
  /checkout/return/{reference}. Server-rendered MVC views.
  NOTE (2026-09-04): the curated homepage showcase sections ("Featured products"
  and "From our gallery") were REMOVED at the owner's request — those items are
  viewed in their own sections (/products, /gallery). Products and services still
  have their "Featured on storefront" flag; gallery's "Show on homepage" is gone.
- Customer Portal: dashboard, orders (list + details + /portal/orders/track),
  addresses, profile (edit + change password), payments, invoices, wishlist.
- Admin Dashboard: KPIs/charts (analytics) + per-module CRUD for products,
  categories, services, orders, customers, inventory, suppliers, procurement,
  users/roles/permissions, settings, reports, gallery, invoices,
  receipts, contact messages, notifications, security (password change).
  (No quotations module — removed 2026-09-01.)
- Auth: register + email-verify, login, logout, forgot/reset password
  (COOKIE only — the JWT/API auth surface was removed 2026-09-01); forced owner
  first-login password change.
- Payments: initialize + verify + webhook; manual staff confirmation of
  counter payments (bank transfer / cash); refunds; receipts after payment.
- Invoicing: invoice issued at order placement (before payment); quote invoices
  (draft, no order); shareable invoice/receipt links; PDF download.
- Quotations: REMOVED (2026-09-01). The standalone Quotation/QuotationLine feature
  (create/edit/send/accept/expire/cancel, convert to order) was deleted as a
  duplicate of the "quote invoice" path. Quoting a customer is now done by creating
  a DRAFT invoice at /admin/invoices/new and converting it to an order (which adopts
  the same INV number). The Quotations/QuotationLines tables were dropped by the
  20260901202823_DropDormantTables migration.
- Inventory: products have a single Inventory row; every stock change appends an
  immutable InventoryTransaction; reservation on payment; procurement.
- Procurement: request/approve/purchase/receive; receiving raises stock.
- Notifications: per-permission scoped, real-time over SignalR.
- Audit/activity log AND Staff activity report (/admin/reports/staff): BOTH sides
  are now LIVE. GAP-01 was CLOSED on 2026-08-31 — the write side is implemented in
  AuditableSoftDeleteInterceptor (an AuditLog row per created/updated/deleted
  aggregate, written atomically in the same SaveChanges), covered end-to-end by
  tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/AuditLogTests.cs. NOTE: it does NOT
  go through IAuditLogWriter/AuditLogWriter — that abstraction is now unused (see
  section 13). Sections 12/13 previously described this as an open gap; that text
  was stale and has been corrected.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
5. BUSINESS RULES (confirmed from code)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Confirmation key: [C] = verified in code / tests. Distinguish from assumptions.
- [C] Orders: One invoice per order (1-1). OrderType (Inventory/Service/Mixed)
  is DERIVED from the lines present. Only products deduct inventory; services
  never do.
- [C] Inventory deducted/reserved only AFTER payment is confirmed (see
  PaymentConfirmationService + OrderFulfilmentService). Shortage on payment ->
  order moves to AwaitingProcurement. Stock reservation on payment; released etc.
  via transaction types (Reservation / ReleaseReservation).
- [C] Payments: Online checkout uses Paystack ONLY. There is deliberately NO
  pay-on-delivery option. PaymentMethod enum: BankTransfer=0, Paystack=1, Cash=3
  (value 2 is a KEPT GAP for legacy rows; the enum member was removed from
  selectable lists but the stored int value is kept for data compatibility).
- [C] Payment confirmation is idempotent (no-op once order is already Paid/
  Completed/Delivered), shared by Verify flow, webhook, and manual confirmation.
  The payment is persisted BEFORE the confirmation pipeline runs (a latent bug
  was fixed: it used to query before SaveChanges).
- [C] Invoice is issued at order PLACEMENT (status Issued), before payment. The
  confirmation pipeline marks it Paid. Receipt is generated AFTER the first
  Succeeded payment (RCT-{year}-{D6}).
- [C] Quote invoices: /admin/invoices/new (staff-only, StaffCounterPolicy via
  orders.create + not Customer role) creates a DRAFT invoice (Invoice.OrderId
  nullable). Converting it to an order ADOPTS the same INV number, links the
  order, re-snapshots lines/totals, Draft -> Issued. A converted quote cannot be
  converted twice; a quote already tied to an order is rejected.
- [C] Quotation line rules (HISTORICAL — the standalone Quotation feature was
  REMOVED on 2026-09-01): name required, qty >= 1, unit price > 0; Draft->Sent->
  Accepted status sequence. These guards no longer exist in code. The equivalent
  rules for the surviving "quote invoice" path live on InvoiceLine/Invoice.
  Do NOT reintroduce a Quotation entity without an explicit owner request.
- [C] Contact form: /contact validates, PERSISTS a ContactMessage FIRST, then
  best-effort enqueues a staff notification email (email failure is swallowed;
  the message is never lost and nothing gates on email). New /admin/messages
  (list/detail/resolve + reopen) behind messages.view permission.
- [C] WhatsApp: business.whatsapp stores a normalized number (digits + country
  code). A shared helper (DeChimex.Shared.WhatsApp) strips wa.me/URLs, rewrites
  Nigerian 0-prefixed numbers to 234, and builds https://wa.me/{digits} on the
  consumer side. No view/admin screen hand-builds a URL.
- [C] Marketing stats: four settings (business.stat_projects, stat_brands,
  stat_services, stat_turnaround) seeded 2400+/180+/12+/48h, editable in
  /admin/settings, consumed by Home, login/register, and About. No
  auto-calculation.
- [C] Owner bootstrap: seeded Owner must change password on first login
  (User.MustChangePassword). Login issues a must_change_password claim, cookie
  sign-in redirects to /admin/security/password?forced=1, a middleware bounces
  every other /admin page while flagged. Cleared by first successful change.
  Bootstrap password ChangeMe123! is rejected after the change.
- [C] Seeding is idempotent, runs on every startup, and does NOT seed catalog
  (categories/products/services start EMPTY). It does purge legacy starter SKUs
  (MUG-001/PEN-007/FLK-021/JER-014/NBK-003/GFT-099), legacy starter categories
  (Fashion/Gift Items/Office Supplies/School Supplies/Corporate Gifts) and
  legacy starter services (Mug Printing/Jersey Printing/Engraving/Vehicle
  Branding) if any pre-exist.
- [C] A role's canonical permission map is applied only when the role is FIRST
  created; afterwards the admin checklist at /admin/users/roles is the source of
  truth and survives restarts (re-sync would wipe admin changes).
- [C] Reference formats (from docs/dataset): DCX orders, QT quotations, PRC
  procurement, INV invoices, RCT receipts, PAY payments, share tokens.
- [C] Caching: memory cache for categories/services/settings (auto-invalidate on
  write); static file cache headers; response caching/compression enabled.
- [C] Rate limiting: global fixed window 100 req/min per client IP; named "auth"
  policy 10 req/min applied to /api/v1/auth only. MVC cookie login sits under the
  global limiter. Tests disable rate limiting in the test host.
- [C] Authorization is TRANSPORT-AGNOSTIC (PermissionPolicyProvider reads claims)
  and runs unchanged over cookie or JWT.

Assumptions / to confirm before changing (not asserted as fact):
- Whether email verification is actually invoked/sent in a working Brevo account
  is partially config-dependent (Brevo call sites are fire-and-forget; key may
  need IP allowlisting — see section 8).
- Some docs (README verification counts, docs/01-architecture mermaid diagram
  still labeling the host "DeChimex.Api", docs draft email template list
  including an "Otp" template) are STALE relative to the current code. See
  section 14.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
6. API vs MVC STATUS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Both surfaces live in one host, but the balance has shifted decisively to MVC.
  API = /api/v1 (3 actions, see section 3). MVC = conventional route
  {controller=Home}/{action=Index}/{id?} plus attribute-routed controllers:
  storefront at the SITE ROOT ("" -> /, /products, /services, /gallery, /cart,
  /checkout), portal under /portal*, admin under /admin*.
  CORRECTION (2026-09-05): earlier revisions said the storefront routes live under
  "/store" — they do NOT. /store/cart returns 404; the real route is /cart. The
  controllers are namespaced under Controllers/Mvc/Store/ but their [Route]
  attributes are root-level (StoreHomeController [Route("")], StoreCartController
  [Route("cart")], CheckoutController [Route("checkout")], etc.).
- API now contains THREE controllers / THREE actions (was 25 controllers /
  ~115-130 actions before the 2026-08-30 Task-2 removal and the 2026-09-01
  JWT/AuthController removal).
- No application code path internally calls the app's own API. MVC and API share
  the same Application/Domain/Infrastructure/Persistence but never self-HTTP.
  Re-verified 2026-09-05.
- /api/v1 is still documented by Swagger (Development only) and by postman/
  DeChimex.postman_collection.json, but that collection is now LARGELY STALE: it
  describes the removed controllers/actions. The three surviving endpoints are
  integration-tested. Treat the Postman collection + docs/ as historical until
  someone deliberately re-trims them (see section 13).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
7. OLD FRONTEND STATUS — REMOVED
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- DeChimexPrintsFrontend/ was the ORIGINAL vanilla-JS SPA (55 HTML pages: 15
  public, 5 auth, 8 portal, 27 admin) that called the API directly
  (assets/js/api.js baseUrl = https://localhost:7061/api/v1). It is now HISTORICAL
  and the whole folder was REMOVED on 2026-08-31.
- The Development-only static-file block in Program.cs (serving the SPA via
  UseStaticFiles) was also removed with it. The app is now a single
  server-rendered ASP.NET Core MVC solution.
- Its SPA auth pages (verify-email, reset-password, track, payment-callback.html)
  were replaced by MVC pages. The AppUrlProvider still references some of these
  routes and is documented as a stale target (see section 14).

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
8. INTEGRATIONS
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Paystack: Infrastructure/Payments/PaystackGateway.cs. Initialize (amount to
  minor unit/kobo), verify, refund. The webhook endpoint
  (/api/v1/payments/webhook/paystack) validates the HMAC-SHA512 signature
  (constant-time compare) and maps provider statuses (success/failed/abandoned/
  reversed/ongoing/...); refunds are acceptance-based via the admin Refund
  action — there is NO refund webhook flow and NO PaymentRefundedEvent (see the
  2026-09-23 audit section at the end). Config via "Paystack" section
  (SecretKey, PublicKey, ApiBaseUrl, Currency, Channels, PercentageFee).
  A FakePaymentGateway replaces the real gateway in tests.
- Brevo: Infrastructure/Email/BrevoEmailService.cs. Sends via POST /v3/smtp/email
  with api-key header. When disabled OR no ApiKey it logs-and-skips (no throw).
  Config via "Brevo" section (ApiKey, ApiBaseUrl, SenderName, SenderEmail,
  Enabled). EmailQueue + EmailTemplateRenderer. Health check configured.
  IMPORTANT operational note: Brevo enforces an authorized-IP allowlist. A valid
  key can still return 401 "unrecognised IP address" until the deployment/sandbox
  IP is added at app.brevo.com/security/authorised_ips.
- WhatsApp: Shared.WhatsApp (NormalizeNumber / BuildUrl). Setting stores number
  only. No external API call — just wa.me deep links.
- PDF: Host/Services/InvoicePdfBuilder.cs with QuestPDF (community license; set
  in Program.cs). GET /i/{token}/pdf (PublicInvoiceController) returns the PDF.
  Receipts stay HTML/print (intentional, no duplicate PDF work).
- Email templates: rendered via EmailTemplateRenderer (Welcome, EmailVerification,
  ForgotPassword, PasswordResetConfirmation, OrderConfirmation, OrderStatusUpdate,
  PaymentReceipt). NOTE: the CHANGELOG legacy-audit removed OTP feature code; the
  docs may still mention an OTP template — verify before relying on it.
- Storage: LocalFileStorageService; uploads under wwwroot/uploads; static cache
  headers 7 days for /uploads.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
9. CREDENTIAL MAPPING (WHERE THE KEYS GO — VALUES ARE INTENTIONALLY OMITTED)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
The repo intentionally has secrets REMOVED (empty in appsettings). Missing keys
are NOT a defect. Only ConnectionStrings:DefaultConnection is required to run
(Program.cs fails fast if empty); the JWT signing key is NO LONGER required (the
JWT token service was removed — see the JWT-independence task below).

Mechanism currently documented in the code/docs: environment variables or
`dotnet user-secrets` (Program.cs message says so). appsettings.json already has
the section structure with empty key values.

Where each value belongs (configuration section names):
  - Database connection string -> ConnectionStrings:DefaultConnection (required to run).
  - (Removed) JWT signing key -> no longer needed; there is no `Jwt` section and no
    ITokenService. Nothing to configure for JWT.
  - Paystack -> Paystack:SecretKey / Paystack:PublicKey (blank; owner must supply to use payments).
  - Brevo -> Brevo:ApiKey (blank; owner must supply to use transactional email).
    For local preview without SQL Server: set Database:UseInMemory=true.
  - Paystack test key -> Paystack:SecretKey (sk_test_...) and Paystack:PublicKey
    (pk_test_...). Public key is exposed via /api/v1/config (safe; secret never).
  - Brevo API key -> Brevo:ApiKey (xkeysib-...). Also confirm Brevo Enabled is
    true (it is) and that the deployment IP is on the authorized-IP list.

Validated during this session (without printing values):
  - Paystack test SECRET key: VALID — authorised GET to /transaction returned
    HTTP 200, "Transactions retrieved".
  - Brevo API key: returned HTTP 401 "unrecognised IP address ... add the IP at
    https://app.brevo.com/security/authorised_ips". This is the documente IP
    allowlist step, NOT an invalid key.

SECURITY RULE: never print secret values back to the user, never commit secrets
to Git, and never include secrets in a package/deliverable ZIP unless the user
explicitly asks them to be packaged. Keep the values in environment variables /
user-secrets / a protected config (not appsettings.Development.json if it could
be committed). The existing appsettings.Production.json is git-ignored — use it
for production and keep it out of the repo.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
10. DATABASE / DATA BEHAVIOR
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- DbContext: DeChimexDbContext (DbContextOptions). ALL mapping is in separate
  IEntityTypeConfiguration classes (Catalog/Inventory/Procurement/Sales/Security/
  SupportingConfigurations). Never inline Fluent API in the context.
- The context overrides SaveChangesAsync to "promote" new child entities whose
  Guid key is absent from the store from Modified -> Added, to avoid
  DbUpdateConcurrencyException when adding a new child to a tracked navigation.
- Soft-delete + audit-field interceptor: AuditableSoftDeleteInterceptor sets
  Created/Modified/Deleted audit fields and converts hard deletes of
  soft-deletable aggregates to soft deletes (IAuditable / ISoftDeletable on
  AggregateRoot). CurrentUser + IDateTimeProvider feed it.
  SINCE 2026-08-31 IT IS ALSO THE AUDIT-LOG WRITE SIDE (GAP-01 fix): for every
  tracked AggregateRoot in the change set it adds an AuditLog row (action
  create/update/delete, entity = CLR type name, entity id, performedBy =
  ICurrentUser.Email with UserId/"system" fallback, ip, plus best-effort old/new
  JSON value snapshots) to the SAME DbContext, so audit rows commit atomically
  with the business change. Action intent is captured BEFORE the soft-delete
  conversion (so a soft-deleted aggregate logs "delete", not "update"); base
  audit/soft-delete props are excluded from the diff; snapshot serialization is
  try/caught so it can never break a business save. This is why the AuditLogs
  table and /admin/reports/staff now have data.
- Entities (Domain/Entities, 32 files as of 2026-09-05): User, Role, Permission,
  RolePermission, UserRole, Customer, CustomerAddress, Category, Product,
  ProductImage, Service, GalleryMedia, Inventory, InventoryTransaction, Order,
  OrderItem, OrderServiceLine, Invoice, InvoiceLine, Payment, Receipt,
  Procurement, ProcurementItem, ShoppingCart, CartItem, WishlistItem, Review,
  Notification, AuditLog, ApplicationSetting, ContactMessage, Supplier.
  REMOVED entities (do not resurrect without an owner request): RefreshToken
  (gone with the JWT surface, 2026-09-01), Quotation and QuotationLine (standalone
  quotations feature removed, 2026-09-01). Their tables were dropped by the
  20260901202823_DropDormantTables migration and they are no longer mapped in
  DeChimexDbContext (only explanatory comments remain).
- Value objects: Money, Address, Email, PhoneNumber, Slug. Enums: OrderStatus,
  OrderType, InventoryTransactionType, PaymentStatus, PaymentMethod, InvoiceStatus,
  ProcurementStatus, GalleryMediaType, NotificationType, OrderLineType.
- Migrations (EF Core, in order — SEVEN as of 2026-09-05):
    20260820005520_initial
    20260828221847_CustomerGuestSupport
    20260828224705_QuoteInvoiceAndReceipts
    20260829001357_RemediationOwnerPasswordAndContactMessages
    20260829201816_AddNotificationRequiredPermission
    20260901202823_DropDormantTables            (drops QuotationLines, RefreshTokens, Quotations)
    20260904214019_RemoveGalleryShowOnHomepage  (drops GalleryMedia.ShowOnHomepage)
  Section 14's "additive only on top of the 5 existing ones" guardrail therefore now
  means: additive only on top of these SEVEN. Never edit a released migration.
- Seeding (DatabaseSeeder, runs on startup): migrations applied (relational) or
  EnsureCreated (InMemory); seeds permissions, roles (with canonical permission
  maps on first create), the Owner account (MustChangePassword), 16 settings,
  and purges legacy starter catalog. Does NOT seed catalog products.
- database/migrate.sql is a STALE manual SQL script (references migration id
  20260717163025_InitialCreate, which is NOT one of the current EF migrations).
  It does not match the EF migration set. Treat as superseded; do not rely on it.
- Development DB: default connection is SqlServer (localhost,1433; database
  DeChimexDb). For preview without a server set Database:UseInMemory=true.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
11. AUTHENTICATION / AUTHORIZATION
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- ONE scheme: ASP.NET Core COOKIE auth (LoginPath /Account/Login). The former
  "smart" policy scheme (ForwardDefaultSelector picking JWT bearer for requests
  with an Authorization header) was REMOVED on 2026-09-01. Verified 2026-09-05:
  ServiceCollectionExtensions registers only
  .AddCookie(CookieAuthenticationDefaults.AuthenticationScheme, ...) — there is no
  AddJwtBearer and no AddAuthentication(...).AddPolicyScheme(...) call anywhere.
- JWT: GONE. No Jwt config section, no JwtOptions, no ITokenService, no
  RefreshToken entity/table, no /api/v1/auth endpoints. The app boots without any
  signing key (Program.cs only fast-fails on an empty connection string when not
  using InMemory). Historical detail (15-min access / 7-day hashed rotating
  refresh tokens / revoke-all-on-reuse) is retained here only so a future session
  does not "restore" it by accident — re-adding an API token surface would be a
  deliberate new project, not a bug fix.
- Cookie: HttpOnly, SameSite=Lax, Secure = SameAsRequest, sliding 8h. For /api
  requests the cookie events return 401/403 (no login redirect).
- Authorization model: policy-based via PermissionPolicyProvider +
  PermissionAuthorizationHandler + NotRoleAuthorizationHandler. ~35 permission
  strings in Shared/Constants/Permissions.cs (products/categories/services/
  inventory/orders/procurement/customers/users/gallery/messages/reports/settings).
  RoleNames: Owner, Manager, SalesStaff, InventoryStaff, Designer, Customer.
- Roles seeded with canonical permission maps: Owner = all; Manager = all except
  users.*; SalesStaff = orders view/viewall/create/update + customers + products/
  services view + messages.view; InventoryStaff = inventory + products.view +
  procurement view/receive; Designer = gallery + products.view + orders.view;
  Customer = orders.create only.
- StaffCounterPolicy ("staff-counter-orders"): requires orders.create AND NOT
  the Customer role — used for staff-only counter forms (POS orders, quote
  invoices) so portal customers can never reach them.
- Roles/permissions are administrable at /admin/users/roles (create/edit roles,
  permission checklist). The seeder only applies canonical maps on first creation.
- Forced owner first-login password change (MustChangePassword flag + claim +
  middleware) as described in section 5.
- AccountController (MVC) reuses LoginCommand/RegisterCommand; builds claims
  (roles as ClaimTypes.Role, permissions as "permission" claim, name, email,
  nameidentifier) and SignInAsync with cookie scheme. Landing page chosen by
  role (staff -> first admin page; customer -> portal).
- API AuthController: REMOVED (2026-09-01) together with the JWT surface. All
  auth flows (login, register, verify-email, forgot/reset password, logout) are
  MVC-only under /Account/*. Earlier revisions documented "login/refresh/register/
  verify/reset over JWT" here — that no longer exists.

        the template was left behind. It has NO call site — a dead enum member, see
        section 13. docs/06's "OTP 123456" is the Paystack TEST-CARD OTP and is
        unrelated to email templates (also correct).
  - database/migrate.sql mismatch with EF migrations — REMOVED 2026-09-05 (owner-approved
    cleanup); its 6 doc references were updated in the same pass.
  - DeChimex.Host.http "/weatherforecast" template line — REPLACED 2026-09-05 with the
    real surviving endpoints (3 API actions + health + swagger + an MVC route cheat-sheet).
  - SecurityHeadersMiddleware CSP still allowlists https://js.paystack.co although no
    view/JS uses the Paystack inline popup any more (harmless, vestigial).
  - IAuditLogWriter / AuditLogWriter (Domain interface + Persistence impl + DI
    registration) is dead code now that the interceptor writes audit rows.
  - Brevo authorized-IP allowlist must include the deployment IP.
  - Paystack/Brevo keys are intentionally blank in appsettings — payments and
    transactional email will not work until the owner supplies them.
  - No real SQL Server is available in the AI sandbox; runtime end-to-end with a
    real DB must be verified on the owner's machine (the InMemory path covers
    preview/tests).
  - RESOLVED (was listed here): "Stale references to the old SPA in AppUrlProvider".
    Verified 2026-09-05 that all three builders now target LIVE MVC routes —
    BuildEmailVerificationLink -> /Account/VerifyEmail?email=&token=,
    BuildPasswordResetLink -> /Account/ResetPassword?email=&token=,
    BuildOrderTrackingLink -> /portal/orders/track?orderNumber= — and that
    AccountController.VerifyEmail/ResetPassword and PortalOrdersController.Track
    all exist. Nothing links to the deleted SPA pages any more.

INTENTIONAL / NOT A BUG (do not "fix" without instruction):
  - PaymentMethod value-2 gap, retained enum values (PaymentStatus.Abandoned,
    InvoiceStatus.PartiallyPaid/Cancelled, NotificationType.UserActivity/System),
    users.update + reports.export permissions (registered/granted but not enforced
    — a permission-model decision).
  - Checkout uses Paystack's HOSTED REDIRECT flow (server-side
    Redirect(authorization_url) -> /checkout/return/{reference} verify). CORRECTION:
    earlier revisions called this "the Paystack JS flow (inherently client-side)" —
    that described the deleted SPA. The current flow is intentionally server-side;
    do not "restore" a PaystackPop inline integration.
  - The reduced 3-action /api/v1 surface is intentional (webhook cannot be an MVC
    page; config/settings-public are consumed by nothing in-repo but are kept as
    the documented anon bootstrap endpoints). Do not re-add the removed API
    controllers unless the owner asks for a mobile/SPA client again.
  - No seeded catalog (categories/products/services/gallery start EMPTY) and no
    mock/demo data — by design.

--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
14. IMPORTANT TECHNICAL DECISIONS / GUARDRAILS (do not break these)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
- Architecture: clean/onion, single host (MVC + API), CQRS via MediatR for most
  features; admin/portal/storefront all server-rendered Razor.
- Models: MVC controllers call MediatR/query services/DbContext directly. No new
  HTTP self-calls.
- Auth: keep the transport-agnostic PermissionPolicyProvider + role checks over the
  single COOKIE scheme. DO NOT weaken or remove any access-control rule, and do not
  re-introduce a JWT/"smart" policy scheme without an explicit owner request.
- Rules that must be preserved: no pay-on-delivery; inventory only after payment;
  invoice before payment + receipt after; WhatsApp
  number normalization; owner forced password change; contact message persists
  first then best-effort email; admin role checks are the source of truth after
  first creation; enum value-2 gaps are kept for legacy data; the audit-log write
  side in AuditableSoftDeleteInterceptor (it is the only thing populating AuditLogs).
  (The old "quotation unit-price>0" guardrail is historical — the Quotation feature
  is gone; the equivalent invoice-line rules apply.)
- QuestPDF community license must be set before any document generation (it is,
  in Program.cs).
- Migrations: additive only on top of the SEVEN existing ones; never edit a released
  migration; keep the model snapshot in sync.
- The InMemory provider must NOT become the production path (opt-in via
  Database:UseInMemory only). Default is SqlServer.
- Build warnings (16: nullable CS8602/CS8601, CS8981 lowercase migration name,
  CS8321 unused local, CS4014 un-awaited test calls) are pre-existing;
  treat them as out of scope unless asked. (ASP0026 no longer occurs.)

    curl -sSL https://dot.net/v1/dotnet-install.sh -o /var/tmp/dotnet-install.sh
    chmod +x /var/tmp/dotnet-install.sh
    /var/tmp/dotnet-install.sh --channel 9.0 --install-dir /var/tmp/dotnet   # -> 9.0.317
    export DOTNET_ROOT=/var/tmp/dotnet; export PATH="/var/tmp/dotnet:$PATH"
    export NUGET_PACKAGES=/var/tmp/nuget
    export NUGET_HTTP_CACHE_PATH=/var/tmp/nuget-http-cache
    (SDK + BOTH NuGet caches MUST be stored outside /home/user — the package cache
     (~/.nuget) and the HTTP cache (~/.local/share/NuGet/http-cache) balloon the
     persisted workspace snapshot and are NOT in the snapshot exclusion list. If
     /var/tmp is gone, reinstall SDK via the dotnet-install script; caches re-download.
     A cold restore+build takes ~30s once packages are cached.)
    cd DeChimexPrints
    dotnet build DeChimex.sln -c Debug           # 0 errors / 16 warnings
    dotnet test DeChimex.sln -c Debug            # 266/266 green, no secrets needed
    # run the app in preview (no SQL Server). Bind 0.0.0.0 so the sandbox preview proxy works:
    export Database__UseInMemory=true
    export ASPNETCORE_ENVIRONMENT=Development
    export ASPNETCORE_URLS=http://0.0.0.0:5119
    dotnet run --project src/DeChimex.Host/DeChimex.Host.csproj --no-launch-profile
    # storefront at / ; admin login at /Account/Login ; swagger at /swagger (Development)
    # ffmpeg is OPTIONAL: only the gallery video re-encode/backfill path needs it
    # (FfmpegVideoEncodeService no-ops and uploads still succeed without it).
- On the owner's machine: configure ConnectionStrings:DefaultConnection + secrets,
  run `dotnet ef database update`, and `dotnet run --project src/DeChimex.Host`,
  then browse /Account/Login (owner@dechimexprinthub.com / ChangeMe123! — must
  change on first login; a later session used ChangeMe456! after that change).

(in a new dated/versioned section), and must reconcile stale statements above.

file as the current-state reference.

18. CHANGE LOG (in reverse chronological order)

### 2026-08-30 — Task 1 of API-relevance cleanup: wishlist feature + broken auth email flows
Request: In the API-relevance classification, Task 1 = migrate the [A]/[D] gap
items. The owner decided the wishlist should be IMPLEMENTED ("authenticated users
can add a product to wishlist; the view should persist adequately (buttons &
page(s))"). Also fixed the live-and-broken email flows whose links pointed at the
retired SPA.

Wishlist feature (completed + tested):
- src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/Store/StoreProductsController.cs
  - Added Wishlist flow to the storefront: [HttpPost("wishlist/{id}")]
    AddToWishlist, [HttpPost("wishlist/{id}/remove")] RemoveFromWishlist; both
    [Authorize] + [ValidateAntiForgeryToken]; resolve the customer via
    ICustomerRepository.
  - Details() now computes ViewBag.InWishlist from IWishlistRepository.FindAsync.
  - Removed the class-level [AllowAnonymous] (which the ASP0026 analyzer flagged as
    overriding action-level [Authorize]) and put [AllowAnonymous] only on Index +
    Details. This fixes the latent auth ambiguity.
- Views/StoreProducts/Details.cshtml: added a wishlist button next to
  "Add to cart" / WhatsApp:
    * Authenticated + already saved  -> "Saved" (Removes).
    * Authenticated + not saved      -> "Add to wishlist" (Adds).
    * Anonymous                      -> "Sign in to save" (login link).
- Views/Shared/_PublicLayout.cshtml: added a Flash block (TempData Success/Error)
  so wishlist feedback renders on the public surface.
- Domain/Application/Persistence untouched: AddWishlistItemCommand /
  RemoveWishlistItemCommand / GetWishlistQuery / WishlistQueries / WishlistItem
  entity already existed; the API WishlistController was their only caller, so
  this makes the feature reachable in the (current) MVC app too.

Broken auth email flows (completed + tested):
- src/DeChimex.Infrastructure/Services/AppUrlProvider.cs: repointed the emailed
  links to the CURRENT MVC routes (the old SPA pages /verify-email, /reset-password,
  /track, /public/payment-callback.html are gone):
    BuildEmailVerificationLink -> /Account/VerifyEmail?email=&token=
    BuildPasswordResetLink     -> /Account/ResetPassword?email=&token=
    BuildOrderTrackingLink     -> /portal/orders/track?orderNumber=
    BuildPaymentCallbackUrl    -> /checkout/return/{reference}
- src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/AccountController.cs: added ForgotPassword
  (GET/POST), ResetPassword (GET/POST), VerifyEmail (GET/POST). These use the same
  Application commands (ForgotPasswordCommand, ResetPasswordCommand,
  VerifyEmailCommand) as the JSON API AuthController. ForgotPassword always shows
  a generic "reset link sent" (anti-enumeration). Reset/Verify surface the domain
  business-rule messages (invalid/expired token) and account-not-found.
- src/DeChimex.Host/Models/AuthFlowModels.cs: new ForgotPasswordViewModel,
  ResetPasswordViewModel, VerifyEmailViewModel.
- New Views: Views/Account/ForgotPassword.cshtml, ResetPassword.cshtml,
  VerifyEmail.cshtml (self-contained auth-card pages).
- Views/Account/Login.cshtml: "Need help? /faq" replaced with "Forgot password?"
  linking to /Account/ForgotPassword.

NOT done yet (still flagged, awaiting owner decision — Task 2 / remainder of the
[A]/[D] items):
- Resend-verification UI (the API AuthController.resend-verification has no MVC
  equivalent; the flow exists but there is no button/page to trigger it).
- The API AuthController.refresh endpoint is only meaningful for an SPA/mobile
  client (cookie auth uses sliding expiry).
- ConfigController and SettingsController.GetPublic were kept as deliberate
  anonymous contracts (not migrated).

Tests (all green):

### 2026-08-30 — API-relevance Task 2: removed the superseded /api/v1 controllers
Request: owner chose "Full sweep" — remove all category-B controllers, keep
webhook/paystack, ConfigController, SettingsController.GetPublic and the [D] items.

DELETED API controllers (21 of 25 API controller files removed):
  Activity, Analytics, Auth, Cart, Categories, Customers, Dashboard, Files,
  Gallery, Inventory, Invoices, Notifications, Orders, Procurement, Products,
  Quotations, Reports, Services, Suppliers, Users, Wishlist.

TRIMMED to keep only the genuinely-required API surface (Payments + Settings):
- PaymentsController -> webhook/paystack ONLY. initialize/verify/callback/
  {id}/confirm-manual/history/invoice/{orderId}/GET-list all removed (now served
  by MVC Store/Checkout + Admin/Portal controllers). webhook/paystack MUST stay an
  API endpoint (Paystack posts server-to-server); it is [AllowAnonymous].
- SettingsController -> GetPublic ONLY. Authenticated GET (list) + PUT (update)
  removed (MVC AdminSettingsController handles management). Kept the anonymous
  whitelisted storefront contract.
- ConfigController -> untouched (deliberate anonymous public config).

KEPT (compiled, retained): ConfigController, SettingsController, PaymentsController
(webhook), ApiControllerBase, + a new SupersededUiDtos.cs holding the read-model DTOs
(InvoiceListDto, QuotationDto, QuotationLineDto) that MVC views/controllers referenced;
their old home (InvoicesController/QuotationsController) was deleted.

NOTE ON AUTH: AuthController is fully removed (register/login/refresh/verify-email/
resend-verification/forgot-password/reset-password/change-password). Cookie-based MVC
auth is the only login path now. There is NO JWT access-token / refresh surface any
longer. The "smart" policy scheme in ServiceCollectionExtensions still registers both
schemes but only cookie is exercised; JWT bearer + Swagger Bearer security are now
unused (left in place; see REMOVAL_caveats §5.3). The named "auth" rate-limit policy is
defined but no longer referenced by any controller.

INTEGRATION TEST SUITE re-plumbed (the removed API register/login/users/products/etc.
were the suite's seeding + auth mechanism):
- tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/TestSeed.cs (NEW): shared helpers
  RegisterCustomerAsync, SeedProductAsync (category+product), CreateStaffUserAsync,
  LoginMvcAsync (cookie+antiforgery login), LoginOwnerMvcAsync (completes the forced
  owner password change), ExtractAntiforgeryToken. Seeds through the SAME Application
  layer the MVC controllers use.
- DELETED pure-API test files: AuthControllerTests, CartControllerTests,
  CustomersControllerTests, InventoryProcurementTests, OrdersControllerTests,
  ProductsControllerTests, QuotationsSuppliersTests, ReportsDashboardTests,
  UsersRolesTests, SettingsControllerTests, CatalogCacheTests.
- PaymentsControllerTests -> kept ONLY the webhook signature/no-op tests (dropped
  initialize/verify/invoice tests for removed actions).
- Rewired to test helpers + MVC surface: WishlistTests, MvcPortalTests (customer name
  now registers via TestSeed, "Ada Customer"), ContactMessagesTests, AuthFlowTests,
  RemediationBusinessRulesTests (dropped the API quotation price test; kept the MVC
  admin one), NotificationScopingTests (now exercises the MVC /admin/notifications
  list + /read + /unread-count instead of /api/v1/notifications; owner login completes
  the forced password change).

POSTMAN: DeChimex.postman_collection.json rebuilt to reflect the surviving surface
(webhook/paystack, settings/public, config, health). Removed all requests for deleted
endpoints. The baseUrl variable is unchanged.

================================================================================
20. CHANGE LOG (cont.) — follow-up: strip dead JWT/Swagger-bearer/rate-limit wiring
================================================================================

### 2026-08-30 — Removed the now-unused JWT + Swagger-bearer + "auth" rate-limit wiring
Context: after Task 2 removed the AuthController (JWT login surface), the browser-based
JWT bearer HTTP auth path and its Swagger/rate-limit companions became dead. Owner
approved stripping them. IMPORTANT (SUPERSEDED 2026-09-01 by the JWT-independence change): at
the time only the HTTP auth *scheme* was removed. The JWT access/refresh *tokens* were STILL
issued by the Application ITokenService (used by the MVC LoginCommand/RegisterCommand), so
Jwt:SigningKey + the Program.cs fast-fail were required. That dependency has since been removed
entirely (see task section 22) — no JWT key is needed now.

- src/DeChimex.Host/Extensions/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:
  - AddAppAuthentication: removed the "smart" AddPolicyScheme + AddJwtBearer; the app
    is now cookie-authenticated only (DefaultScheme/Challenge/SignIn = Cookie).
    ApiVersioning + the permission policy provider/handlers are unchanged.
  - AddSwaggerDocumentation: removed the dead "Bearer" OpenAPI securityScheme +
    securityRequirement (no API endpoint uses bearer auth now). SwaggerDoc +
    schema-id/conflict settings unchanged.
  - AddRateLimitingConfigured: removed the unused named "auth" policy (no controller
    references it); kept the global per-IP limiter.
  - Removed now-unused usings (System.Text, JwtBearer, IdentityModel.Tokens).
- src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/ErrorController.cs: added explicit [HttpGet] to
  NotFound and Error. (Pre-existing dev-only swagger bug: Swashbuckle/OpenAPI 3.0 could
  not classify those actions without an HTTP verb, so /swagger/v1/swagger.json 500'd.
  NOT caused by the bearer-scheme removal; fixed here so swagger.json works again.)

#### Current build/test state (post-auth-strip) ####
- Build: dotnet build DeChimex.sln -c Debug -> 0 errors. 16 warnings, ALL pre-existing

### 2026-08-31 — GAP-01 audit write side + SPA removal + folder consolidation + JWT question
================================================================================
REQUESTED (owner):
  1. Fix GAP-01: wire the AuditLog WRITE side (the read side exists in
     AdminReportsController.Staff; the entity/table/writer/DI were registered, but
     nothing wrote audit rows, so AuditLogs was always empty).
  2. Delete the DeChimexPrintsFrontend SPA entirely.
  3. Consolidate folders: the solution should have a single main folder, DeChimexPrints
     (no Frontend/Backend split). Move DeChimexPrintsBackend contents into it.
  4. JWT-signing-key question: determine whether MVC login/register can survive without
     Jwt:SigningKey. If independent, implement; otherwise educate with a recommendation.

WHAT WAS IMPLEMENTED:
  GAP-01 audit write side:
  - Extended src/DeChimex.Persistence/Interceptors/AuditableSoftDeleteInterceptor.cs.
    It now, for each tracked AggregateRoot in the change set, records an AuditLog row
    (action create/update/delete, entity name = CLR type, entity id, performedBy =
    ICurrentUser.Email (fallback UserId / "system"), ip = ICurrentUser.IpAddress, plus
    best-effort old/new value JSON snapshots). Rows are added to the SAME DbContext, so
    they persist atomically with the change in the same SaveChanges transaction.
  - Action intent is captured BEFORE the soft-delete conversion so a deleted-soft-deletable
    aggregate is correctly recorded as "delete" (not "update"). Base audit/soft-delete
    bookkeeping props (CreatedBy/CreatedDateUtc/ModifiedBy/ModifiedDateUtc/IsDeleted/
    DeletedDateUtc/DeletedBy) are excluded from the value diff; snapshot serialization is
    wrapped in try/catch so it can never break a business save.
  - Tests: added tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/AuditLogTests.cs — logs in as
    owner, creates/edits/deletes a category via the MVC admin surface, then asserts exactly
    one create/update/delete AuditLog row per action, attributed to owner@dechimexprinthub.com.

  SPA deletion:
  - git rm -r DeChimexPrintsFrontend (all tracked files removed).
  - Removed the Development-only static-file block in
    src/DeChimex.Host/Program.cs that served DeChimexPrintsFrontend (and the now-unused
    `using Microsoft.Extensions.FileProviders;`).
  - Removed the obsolete tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/LocalFrontendHostingTests.cs
    (asserted /public/index.html from the deleted SPA).

  Folder consolidation:
  - `git mv` on the whole directory aborted because of pre-existing staged deletions, so a
    plain `mv DeChimexPrintsBackend DeChimexPrints` + `git add -A` was used. Git detects the
    rename (status shows `R  DeChimexPrintsBackend/... -> DeChimexPrints/...`). The .sln uses
    relative backslash paths, so nothing in the solution had to change and the build is intact.
  - Updated every doc reference `DeChimexPrintsBackend` -> `DeChimexPrints` (AI_HANDOFF,
    CHANGELOG, SETUP, V012_SECURITY_HARDENING, DeChimex_MVC_Manual_Test_Plan,
    MVC_MIGRATION_REPORT, REFINEMENT_PROGRESS) and rewrote the stale
    `DeChimexPrintsFrontend` mentions in AI_HANDOFF, API_RELEVANCE_CLASSIFICATION, README,
    MVC_MIGRATION_PLAN, MVC_MIGRATION_REPORT, CHANGELOG to reflect removal.

  JWT question (RECOMMENDATION, not implemented):
  - Determined: MVC login/register sign in via COOKIE (HttpContext.SignInAsync cookie
    scheme) and never consume the JWT access/refresh tokens returned by the Application
    Login/Register commands. However ITokenService (called by Login/Register/RefreshToken
    commands) still needs a signing key, and Program.cs fast-fails without Jwt:SigningKey.
  - So the app is NOT independent of the key. Recommended path: keep Jwt:SigningKey. The
    only way to become independent is a larger change (remove TokenService + the AuthResponse
    token fields + the unused RefreshToken command, or switch the tokens to
    DataProtection-signed cookies). Not done — defer to owner; do not change on their behalf.

  Doc corrections made during this pass:
  - Corrected the false "removed 24 of 25 API controllers" claim to "21 of 25" in
    AI_HANDOFF, REFINEMENT_PROGRESS, CHANGELOG, API_RELEVANCE_CLASSIFICATION (verified:
    25 controller files at HEAD, 21 deleted, ConfigController/PaymentsController/SettingsController/
    ApiControllerBase kept, plus new SupersededUiDtos.cs).
  - Corrected the false Directory.Build.props claim (the file is NOT present at the solution
    root; the ArtifactsPath=/tmp redirect was a session-scoped override). Updated CHANGELOG
    and REFINEMENT_PROGRESS to state the file is absent and bin/obj remain gitignored.

AFFECTED FILES:
  - src/DeChimex.Persistence/Interceptors/AuditableSoftDeleteInterceptor.cs (audit write side)

### 2026-09-01 — JWT-independence: removed the JWT token surface (Jwt:SigningKey no longer required)
================================================================================
OWNER REQUEST: "do it" — make MVC login/register work WITHOUT Jwt:SigningKey.

WHAT WAS IMPLEMENTED (all verified build 0 errors; 231/231 tests green):
  1. Removed ITokenService (Application/Common/Interfaces/Services.cs), TokenService
     (Infrastructure/Authentication/TokenService.cs), JwtOptions (Options/AppOptions.cs),
     and their DI registrations (Infrastructure/DependencyInjection.cs).
  2. Removed the JWT fast-fail validation block from src/DeChimex.Host/Program.cs.
  3. LoginHandler/RegisterHandler no longer depend on ITokenService and no longer issue
     access/refresh tokens; they still return roles/permissions, which AccountController
     uses to build the cookie claims (MVC cookie-auth unchanged).
  4. AuthResponse (Contracts/Auth/AuthDtos.cs) dropped AccessToken/AccessTokenExpiresUtc/
     RefreshToken/RefreshTokenExpiresUtc; removed the now-unused RefreshTokenRequest and
     the unused RefreshTokenCommand (+handler/validator), deleted with RefreshToken.cs.
  5. Removed dead refresh-token plumbing: IUserRepository.GetByRefreshTokenHashAsync /
     TrackNewRefreshToken; User.AddRefreshToken/FindRefreshToken; RefreshToken.Issue. KEPT:
     the RefreshToken entity + table (dormant) and User.RevokeAllRefreshTokens (still used
     to invalidate sessions on password reset / user disable).
  6. Removed the `Jwt` section from appsettings.json + appsettings.Development.json and the
     Jwt__SigningKey lines from .env.example. SETUP.md §3 / env-var / production / troubleshooting
     updated (JWT no longer required; DB connection string is the only required setting).

VERIFICATION:
  - dotnet build DeChimex.sln -c Debug -> 0 errors.
  - dotnet test -> 231/231 (102 unit + 129 integration).
  - dotnet run with NO Jwt:SigningKey env and NO Jwt config (InMemory DB) -> reached
    "Now listening" + seeded owner, no JWT error. Confirms full independence.

### 2026-09-01 — Removed the standalone Quotations feature (quote now lives on the Invoice path)
================================================================================
OWNER REQUEST: "remove quotations and keep invoices."

RATIONALE (see Quotation_Removal_Evaluation.md): the Invoice table already has a Draft
"quote invoice" (`OrderId = null`) that is strictly more capable than a Quotation, so this
was redundant-feature cleanup, not a feature loss. It supports catalog product/service
lines, discount + tax, a public share link (/i/{token}), a printable document whose PDF
header reads "QUOTATION", and order adoption that reuses the same INV number.

WHAT WAS REMOVED:
  - src/DeChimex.Domain/Entities/Quotation.cs (+ QuotationLine).
  - src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/Admin/AdminQuotationsController.cs.
  - src/DeChimex.Host/Views/AdminQuotations/ (Create, Details, Index, Convert).
  - Read-models QuotationDto / QuotationLineDto (Controllers/SupersededUiDtos.cs).
  - View models QuotationFormViewModel / QuotationLineFormViewModel / QuotationConvertViewModel
    (Models/Admin/ReportAndGalleryModels.cs).
  - EF QuotationConfiguration / QuotationLineConfiguration (Configurations/ProcurementConfigurations.cs);
    DbSet<Quotation> / DbSet<QuotationLine> (Context/DeChimexDbContext.cs).
  - Sidebar Quotations nav entry (Views/Shared/_AdminLayout.cshtml).

DECISIONS TAKEN (both flagged in the eval):
  - Database (initial): DORMANT. The Quotations / QuotationLines TABLES were left in place.
    This was SUPERSEDED by the owner's follow-up (see "Second pass" below) -> DROP migration.
  - Customer-facing copy: reworded "quotation" -> "quote" in Faq.cshtml, Privacy.cshtml,
    Terms.cshtml, StoreServices/Index.cshtml (now served by the invoice quote path).

--- SECOND PASS (2026-09-01): drop the dormant tables via EF migration (owner decision (b)) ---
  - Added EF migration `20260901202823_DropDormantTables` that drops `Quotations`,
    `QuotationLines` and `RefreshTokens` (the other dormant table).
  - Removed the RefreshToken code from the model: the entity (`Domain/Entities/RefreshToken.cs`),
    its config (`SecurityConfigurations.RefreshTokenConfiguration` + the User.RefreshTokens
    HasMany/Navigation), `DbSet<RefreshToken>` (`Context/DeChimexDbContext.cs`), the
    `User.RefreshTokens` collection/navigation and `User.RevokeAllRefreshTokens`, and its two
    call sites (`ResetPassword` in Auth/Commands/PasswordManagement.cs, `SetUserActive` in
    Features/Users/UserManagementFeature.cs).
  - EF Core tools: dotnet-ef 9.0.0 installed to /var/tmp/ef-tools (must match the project's
    EF Core 9.0.0). The migration was generated against the Persistence project (it has a
    DesignTimeDbContextFactory). Verified via `dotnet ef migrations script --idempotent`
    (emits the 3 DROP TABLE statements) and `dotnet build` (0 errors; 227/227 tests;
    app boots InMemory + seeds owner).
  - NOTE: `database/migrate.sql` is the STALE manual script (superseded by EF migrations); it
    still contains CREATE TABLE for RefreshTokens and was NOT touched.

TESTS: 4 integration tests removed, all quotation-related:
  "/admin/quotations" from AdminPaths (drops 2 theory cases — that path feeds both the
  WithoutAuth + AsOwner theories), Quotations_AsOwner_ShowsNewQuotationLink (MvcAdminTests),
  and Quotation_ZeroOrNegativeUnitPrice_Is_Rejected_On_Admin_Create (RemediationBusinessRulesTests).
  The zero/negative-price guard is still enforced on the invoice path by
  CreateQuoteInvoiceValidator (Lines.NotEmpty, Quantity > 0, non-negative discount/tax) and
  Invoice.CreateQuote (non-negative grand total). (The earlier
  Api_Quotation_EmptyOrInvalidLines_Is_Rejected test was already removed with the API controllers.)

NOT IMPACTED (verified): no RBAC change (no quotation permission existed; gated by
Orders.View/Orders.Create, same as invoices), no dashboard/reports/activity widget, no domain
event/notification/email, no CQRS command, InvoicePdfBuilder unaffected.

VERIFICATION:
  - dotnet build DeChimex.sln -c Debug -> 0 errors.
  - dotnet test -> 227 pass / 0 fail = 102 unit + 125 integration (down from 231 by the 4
    quotation-related integration tests).

SYMPTOM: Homepage (and store) rendered with broken image icons for the hero, logos, product and
process images, while CSS/JS were fine.
ROOT CAUSE: When the backend was consolidated to the single DeChimexPrints folder
(DeChimexPrintsBackend -> DeChimexPrints, DeChimexPrintsFrontend SPA deleted), the 16 static
images under `src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/img/` were NOT copied into the consolidated folder — only
`wwwroot/img/reviews/*` survived. The views still reference `~/img/hero.png`, `~/img/prod-*.png`,
`~/img/stage-*.jpg`, etc., so those 404'ed. Git still tracked them (under the old
`DeChimexPrintsBackend/...wwwroot/img/` path), which is how they were recovered.
FIX: Recovered all 16 images from git (`git show HEAD:<old path>`) into
`DeChimexPrints/src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/img/` (hero-mobile.png, hero.png, hero-products.jpg,
logo-mark.png, logo.jpg, logo.png, prod-flask/gift/jersey/mug/notepad/pen.png, stage-brand/
deliver/design/print.jpg; reviews/* re-verified). Verified via `dotnet run` + curl:
`/img/hero.png`, `/img/prod-mug.png`, `/img/stage-brand.jpg`, `/img/reviews/...` all return
HTTP 200 with the correct image/* content-type.
NO OTHER files were lost: a full diff of the 490 tracked DeChimexPrintsBackend files against the
consolidated DeChimexPrints tree shows every missing file is an INTENTIONAL removal (superseded
API controllers + their tests, JWT TokenService, Quotation/RefreshToken entities + views/config).
GIT INDEX RECONCILED (staged, NOT committed): `git add -A` at repo root had been pending — the
index still tracked the old DeChimexPrintsBackend/ + DeChimexPrintsFrontend/ tree and the whole
DeChimexPrints/ folder was untracked. Staged 613 changes: rename DeChimexPrintsBackend ->

=== 2026-09-01 — FIX: home page lacked left/right padding on mobile ===
SYMPTOM: On mobile the storefront home page rendered edge-to-edge (no side margins), while the
Products/About pages had proper inset. Root cause: the home hero uses `class="container hero-grid"`,
and the inline `.hero-grid{padding:72px 0 32px}` (in StoreHome/Index.cshtml @section Styles, which is
rendered AFTER app.css) overrides `.container{padding:0 24px}`. On desktop this is masked by
`.hero-left` margin-left, but under the 720px media query `.hero-left{display:contents}` removes that
margin and the hero-grid sets only padding-top/bottom, so content went edge-to-edge.
FIX: in the 720px media query, changed `.hero-grid` to `padding:12px 24px 20px` (restores the
24px side inset, matching `.container` used by other pages). Verified in served HTML (rule present)
and app boots + serves `/img/hero.jpg` (200). Build 0 errors; 227/227 tests green.
================================================================================

=== 2026-09-02 (video playback + aspect-ratio verification) ===
Verified end-to-end against a running build (in-memory DB, host :5118). Findings:
PLAY/PAUSE + PROGRESS BAR — WORK (lightbox viewer). The video lightbox injects
`<video src controls playsinline preload="metadata" style="width:100%;max-height:78vh;object-fit:contain;background:#0F172A;border-radius:12px">`
into the modal via openModal (ui.js uses innerHTML, so it becomes a real <video>). The `controls`
attribute gives the NATIVE browser play/pause button + progress/seek bar (no custom JS). Kestrel
serves the media with `Accept-Ranges: bytes` (HTTP 200, Content-Type: video/mp4), so seeking/scrubbing
in the progress bar works. IMPORTANT CAVEAT: controls render for every video tile, but they only
actually PLAY if the file is a real, encoded video. The previously-stored .mp4 files in
wwwroot/uploads/gallerymedia/ were all 4-16 BYTES (fake/empty, no moov atom) — those show empty
controls and will NOT play. I generated two REAL mp4s (1280x720 @2s, 720x1280 @1.5s via cv2) and
confirmed the entire chain: they upload, persist, are served as video/mp4 HTTP 200 with full bytes,
open in cv2, and have a valid moov atom. => If a gallery video won't play, its stored file is
truncated/fake; re-upload a real video via /admin/gallery.
DISPLAY ASPECT RATIO — CORRECT. Two contexts:
  * Grid tiles (/gallery + homepage showcase): forced SQUARE via `.gitem{aspect-ratio:1/1}`.
    Thumbnails center-crop into the square via `.gitem img{width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover}`;
    videos without a thumbnail get a dark `.vid-empty` play tile. (Intentional Instagram-square look.)
  * Lightbox/modal: `object-fit:contain` + `max-height:78vh` => video keeps its NATIVE aspect ratio
    (16:9 landscape or 9:16 portrait), letterboxed on a dark background, no distortion, capped to
    viewport height. No global CSS forces a height/aspect-ratio on the modal media, so the <video>
    sizes to its intrinsic dimensions. Both a 16:9 and a 9:16 real file rendered at their correct ratios.
================================================================================

=== 2026-09-02 (video playback ROOT CAUSE + real fix) ===
USER REPORT: the play/pause button renders but "won't play on click". Verified in a real browser
(Playwright + Chromium headless). The player markup/native controls are CORRECT — the blocker is the
VIDEO CODEC, not the UI:
  * H.264/AVC video  -> plays (currentTime advanced 0 -> 1.30s on a real click; toggles off on 2nd).
  * MPEG-4 Part 2 (mp4v) / HEVC / empty 4-10 byte files -> Chromium reports
    "DEMUXER_ERROR_NO_SUPPORTED_STREAMS" / play() rejects with NotSupportedError. Browsers only decode
    H.264/AVC, VP8/VP9, AV1.
FIX (build 0 errors; 227/227 tests green):
  1) NEW IVideoEncodeService (+ Application.Common.Interfaces) and FfmpegVideoEncodeService
     (Infrastructure/Storage). At gallery-video upload, LocalFileStorageService now calls
     MakeBrowserPlayableAsync: ffprobe reads the codec; if it's not h264/vp8/vp9/av1 it re-encodes to
     H.264 (libx264, baseline-aware, yuv420p, +faststart); h264 is remuxed to fast start. Works only
     when ffmpeg/ffprobe exist; otherwise it leaves the file untouched and UploadAsync still succeeds.
     Registered in DI (services.AddScoped<IVideoEncodeService, FfmpegVideoEncodeService>()).
  2) Graceful in-player message: StoreGallery + StoreHome openGallery now inject a .g-media-wrap and an
     onerror -> galleryVideoError() that shows "This video can't be played ... re-upload as MP4". Applies
     to legacy videos uploaded BEFORE this fix (they are NOT re-encoded retroactively) and to
     no-ffmpeg environments.
  3) Fixed a pre-existing JS crash on EVERY public page: _PublicLayout logo onerror did
     "this.parentElement.textContent='DC'; this.parentElement.setAttribute(...)" — the first statement
     detaches the <img>, so parentElement became null and setAttribute threw. Fixed both occurrences to
     capture the parent once ("const _p=this.parentElement; ..."). (The /img/logo.png 404 remains — that
     file is separate and was never in git; see asset note below.)
KEY EVIDENCE (stored file after upload of an mp4v source): ffprobe codec_name=h264, pix_fmt=yuv420p,
fast start (moov before mdat), and Chromium click test showed readyState 4, paused:false,
currentTime 1.306, no error.
NOTE existing videos: files already in wwwroot/uploads/gallerymedia were uploaded before this fix and
will NOT be retro-transcoded; edit each item and re-upload the video (or add a re-upload path) to fix.
NOTE missing assets: wwwroot/img now contains only reviews/ — hero.jpg, hero-products.jpg, logo.png are
gone (recurring sandbox reset; recover from the v016 ZIP / git history). The logo onerror falls back to
text 'DC' so the site still renders.
================================================================================

=== 2026-09-04 (BACKFILL, supersedes the "will NOT be retro-transcoded" note above) — one-time re-encode of existing gallery videos + hero images restored ===
USER: "yes and restore the hero images" = (1) run a one-time background backfill that re-transcodes
existing gallery videos to H.264 so every stored file becomes browser-playable WITHOUT manual re-upload,
and (2) restore the missing hero/branding images.
SUPERSEDES: the "will NOT be retro-transcoded" note in the previous entry. Existing videos are NOW
re-encoded automatically on startup (opt-out via config) and on demand from the admin gallery.

BACKFILL AUTOMATIC (new, on startup):
  - src/DeChimex.Host/Services/GalleryVideoReEncodeService.cs (NEW): RunAsync() lists all gallery media
    via IGalleryRepository, filters Type == GalleryMediaType.Video, resolves each URL to a physical file
    (StorageOptions.LocalRootPath + the PublicBaseUrl prefix stripped, with a path-traversal guard),
    then calls IVideoEncodeService.MakeBrowserPlayableAsync. It NEVER changes the DB Url — only the
    stored file. Skips files that are missing (counted, not failed). Returns
    GalleryReEncodeResult(TotalVideos, ReEncoded, AlreadyPlayable, MissingFiles, Failed).
  - src/DeChimex.Host/Services/GalleryVideoReEncodeBackgroundService.cs (NEW): a BackgroundService that
    waits ~3s after startup, then runs GalleryVideoReEncodeService once if Gallery:ReEncodeExistingVideos
    is true (default true). Zero-fatal on exception (logs the error). GalleryBackfillOptions holds
    SectionName = "Gallery".
  - FfmpegVideoEncodeService made IDEMPOTENT: H.264 files whose moov atom already precedes mdat are
    skipped via a new IsFastStart helper (scans the first 1 MiB). This makes repeated runs cheap and
    safe to leave enabled.
  - Wired in Program.cs (AddGalleryVideoReEncodeBackfill in Extensions/ServiceCollectionExtensions.cs:
    binds GalleryBackfillOptions with ValidateOnStart, registers the re-encode service scoped, and
    AddHostedService<GalleryVideoReEncodeBackgroundService>). appsettings.json gained:
    "Gallery": { "ReEncodeExistingVideos": true }.

MANUAL TRIGGER (owner-facing, for re-running after adding old files):
  - Admin gallery toolbar now has a "Re-encode videos" button -> POST /admin/gallery/reencode
    (AdminGalleryController.ReEncode, [Authorize(Policy = Permissions.Gallery.Manage)]); the controller
    injects GalleryVideoReEncodeService and shows a TempData summary (fixed/already-playable/missing/failed).

HERO / BRANDING IMAGES RESTORED: re-materialized from the v016 snapshot ZIP into
src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/img/ (hero.jpg 1536x1024 JPEG, hero-products.jpg — byte-identical to hero.jpg,
hero-mobile.png 800x1093, logo.png 1024x1024 RGBA — same bytes as logo-mark.png, prod-*.png, stage-*.jpg,
reviews/*). Verified the app serves /img/hero.jpg, /img/hero-products.jpg, /img/hero-mobile.png,
/img/logo.png all HTTP 200 with the correct image/* content-type. These are UNTRACKED in git (the

WHAT CHANGED (Product SKU — live suggestion & validation from the product name)
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Request: "Redesign the Product SKU field so users do not have to manually think of or invent SKUs. The
SKU should be automatically suggested from the Product Name while remaining fully editable." Only the SKU
field/behaviour/validation changed; the rest of the app untouched; existing Product.Sku property reused.

- src/DeChimex.Shared/Constants/ProductSkuGenerator.cs (NEW): single source of truth. Format
  `[NAME-PREFIX]-[CODE]`, exactly 9 chars (prefix 3 + '-' + suffix 5). BuildPrefix = 3-char uppercase
  initials of up to 3 meaningful words (drop stopwords), pads from the name; GenerateSuffix = random
  5-char alnum; Suggest/SuggestKeepingPrefix = valid + unique; IsValidFormat enforces
  `^[A-Z0-9]{3}-[A-Z0-9]{5}$` (no spaces/lowercase, exactly 9). FallbackPrefix = "PRD".
- src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/js/product-sku.js (NEW): live suggestion on Name input; only overwrites while
  `data-sku-auto=true`; first keystroke in SKU marks it manual (stops regeneration); subtle Regenerate
  button; sanitises input to 9-char uppercase; `window.__dechimexExistingSkus` keeps suggestions unique.
- src/DeChimex.Application/Features/Products/Commands/CreateProduct.cs: CreateServiceHandler-style auto
  generation + auto collision-resolution. Blank SKU -> generated from name; supplied SKU validated to the
  9-char format; any collision auto-resolved with a fresh unique suffix. CreateProductValidator requires
  the exact format when a SKU is supplied (blank allowed). UpdateProduct/other flows unchanged.
- src/DeChimex.Host/Models/Admin/AdminFormModels.cs (ProductFormViewModel): Sku no longer [Required].
- src/DeChimex.Application/Features/Products/Queries/GetProducts.cs: added GetAllProductSkusQuery +
  handler. src/DeChimex.Application/Common/Interfaces/QueryServices.cs: IProductQueries.GetSkusAsync.
  src/DeChimex.Persistence/Queries/CatalogQueries.cs: ProductQueries.GetSkusAsync impl.
- src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/Admin/AdminProductsController.cs: Create GET/POST prefills a valid
  unique suggestion + exposes `ViewBag.ExistingSkus`.
- src/DeChimex.Host/Views/AdminProducts/_ProductForm.cshtml: SKU field (create: live input + regenerate
  button + helper "Automatically generated from product name"; edit: readonly, unchanged) + SKU data/script.
- src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/css/app.css: added `.sku-wrap`/`.sku-regen`.
- tests/DeChimex.UnitTests/Shared/ProductSkuGeneratorTests.cs + tests/DeChimex.UnitTests/Application/
  Features/Products/CreateProductHandlerTests.cs (NEW).

  - Cleanup scope: "All safe ones" = remove the dead IAuditLogWriter abstraction +
    delete stale database/migrate.sql + fix the dead /weatherforecast line in

1) DEAD AUDIT-WRITER ABSTRACTION REMOVED (3 sites, comment left at each):
  - src/DeChimex.Domain/Repositories/Repositories.cs: deleted
    `public interface IAuditLogWriter { Task WriteAsync(AuditLog log, ...); }`.
  - src/DeChimex.Persistence/Repositories/BusinessRepositories.cs: deleted
    `public sealed class AuditLogWriter(DeChimexDbContext context) : IAuditLogWriter`
    (its WriteAsync did AddAsync + its OWN SaveChangesAsync).
  - src/DeChimex.Persistence/DependencyInjection.cs: deleted
    `services.AddScoped<IAuditLogWriter, AuditLogWriter>();`.
  RATIONALE (recorded so nobody reintroduces it): nothing injected it — grep across
  src/ and tests/ returned only those three declaration sites — and a separate
  SaveChangesAsync in a writer would BREAK the atomicity that
  AuditableSoftDeleteInterceptor now guarantees (audit rows added to the same
  DbContext commit in the same transaction as the business change). Each site keeps
  a short comment pointing at the interceptor as the real write side.
  This was safe precisely BECAUSE GAP-01 was closed first (2026-08-31). Removing it
  earlier would have left no audit write path at all.

2) database/migrate.sql DELETED (`git rm`):
  - It referenced migration id 20260717163025_InitialCreate, which is not in the
    current SEVEN-migration set (the real initial migration is
    20260820005520_initial), so running it would have produced a schema that does
    not match the EF model. The database/ folder is now empty/gone.
  - Its 6 inbound references were updated in the same pass (a deleted file with live
    doc links pointing at it is worse than a stale file):
      * README.md: the "Idempotent DB script: database/migrate.sql" link replaced by
        a note explaining the removal + the on-demand
        `dotnet ef migrations script --idempotent ...` command.
      * V012_SECURITY_HARDENING.md (HIGH-008 audit method): reworded to a
        historical note — the script it inspected has since been removed; the
        audit's conclusion (no injection vectors) is unchanged.
      * docs/04-database-setup.md: the `--output database/migrate.sql` example now
        outputs to /tmp with a comment saying the committed script was removed.
      * docs/08-deployment.md section E: "Run database/migrate.sql during release"
        replaced by the three real options (startup auto-migrate,
        `dotnet ef database update`, or a generated idempotent script).
