25. CHANGE LOG (cont.) — 2026-09-05 gallery lightbox caption redesign (+ two latent bugs fixed)
================================================================================
REQUEST (owner, with a screenshot of the /gallery lightbox): "this is how the media
in the gallery section currently display, need the title and category styled
properly, it currently looks basic and isolated. ensure visual appeal."

BEFORE: the lightbox (public /gallery only) rendered the item's metadata as a plain
centered `.g-meta` block (11px uppercase grey category over a 15px bold dark title)
floating in the modal's WHITE body padding, disconnected from the dark media box
above it — and it repeated the title that the modal header already showed.

AFTER (src/DeChimex.Host/Views/StoreGallery/Index.cshtml ONLY — no C#, no app.css,
no other view touched):
  - Media and metadata now form ONE object: a new `.g-card` (border-radius 14px,
    overflow hidden, #0F172A, layered drop shadow) wraps the `.g-view` media box and
    a new `.g-cap` caption strip, so the metadata is ATTACHED to the artwork instead
    of floating below it.
  - `.g-cap` is a horizontal strip on the same dark background with a hairline top
    border and a subtle vertical gradient: [category chip][title][media-type].
      * `.g-cap-cat` — pill chip: brand-indigo tint (rgba(129,140,248,.16) fill,
        .38 border, #C7D2FE text), 6px accent dot, 10.5px/700 uppercase, .09em
        tracking. Matches the service-icon palette accent already used in the brand.
      * `.g-cap-title` — #F1F5F9, 15.5px/600, -.01em tracking, single line with
        text-overflow ellipsis (long titles like "Premium business cards with
        spot-UV finish and rounded corners" truncate gracefully instead of wrapping
        into a ragged block).
      * `.g-cap-type` — right-aligned muted (rgba(203,213,225,.62)) 11px uppercase
        "Video"/"Image" label with the matching play/image glyph, so the media type
        is legible without hovering the grid badge.
      * <=520px: the strip wraps — chip + type on the first row, title full-width
        below (verified at a 390px viewport).
  - The modal HEADER no longer repeats the item title: openModal is now called with
    title 'Gallery' (section context) and the item's category + title live solely in
    the caption strip. (The header h3 remains the dialog's accessible name.)
  - New helper renderGalleryCaption(cat, title, type) builds the strip and HTML-
    escapes its inputs; the multi-image prev/next handler was rewritten to rebuild
    the whole `.g-card` (media + caption) so navigation can no longer drop it.

TWO LATENT BUGS FOUND AND FIXED WHILE DOING THIS (both pre-existing, both in the
same view):
  1. WRONG CATEGORY IN THE LIGHTBOX: the grid tiles expose the category as
     data-cat (that is what the filter pills read), but openGallery read
     el.dataset.category, which never exists — so EVERY lightbox silently fell back
     to "General". Fixed to `d.cat || d.category || 'General'`. Verified live: an
     item in category "Vehicle Branding" now shows VEHICLE BRANDING (previously it
     would have shown GENERAL). This is likely part of why the owner felt the
     metadata looked wrong, not just plain.
  2. LIGHT LETTERBOX GUTTERS ON IMAGES: app.css has a global
     `img{font-style:italic;background:var(--n-100)}` (~line 685, meant to make
     broken/alt images neutral). Because `.g-view img` uses object-fit:contain, that
     global background painted the letterbox gutters LIGHT grey inside the dark card
     (a video never showed it because <video> paints its own background). Fixed by
     giving `.g-view img` an explicit background:#0F172A + font-style:normal (higher
     specificity than the element selector), with a comment naming the app.css rule
     so nobody "cleans it up" later. Pixel-sampled before/after: gutters went from
     rgb(242,240,235) to the card's rgb(15,23,42).

NOT CHANGED (deliberately): the ADMIN gallery detail modal (Views/AdminGallery/
Index.cshtml) still uses the older centered `.g-meta` block plus its status-badge
row. It is staff-facing and its badges (Featured/Active/Archived) are functional;
mirroring the new caption strip there is a cosmetic follow-up the owner has not
asked for. The grid tiles, filter pills, hover overlay and lightbox media behaviour
(playback, aspect ratio, error states) are untouched.

VERIFICATION (live, InMemory DB, Playwright headless Chromium at 1280x900 and
390x800, with a real H.264 720x1280 test video generated via ffmpeg + two real JPEGs
uploaded through POST /admin/gallery/create):
  - Video lightbox: dark cohesive card; caption strip shows
    [• VEHICLE BRANDING] Every car sold deserves a signature that lasts ... [▶ VIDEO].
  - Image lightbox: dark gutters now match the card; caption shows
    [• PRINT] Premium business cards with spot-UV finish… ... [🖼 IMAGE] (ellipsised).
  - Mobile 390px: caption wraps to chip+type row then full-width title; no overflow.
  - No console/page errors on any of the three captures.
  - dotnet build -> 0 errors (16 pre-existing warnings unchanged).
  - dotnet test DeChimex.sln -> 266/266 green (138 unit + 128 integration);
    MvcStorefrontTests still pass against /gallery.

SANDBOX INCIDENT DURING THIS TASK (2026-09-05, ~11:18-11:22): the workspace suffered
a PARTIAL reset mid-turn — the .git directory and the tracked wwwroot/img/* assets
were deleted from the worktree (the doc's "RECURRING GIT-RESET CAVEAT" struck again,
this time without re-cloning). All edited FILES survived. Recovery performed and
verified: re-cloned origin into /tmp/restore-git and copied only its .git back into
the worktree (HEAD 192013a, my 15 modified files + migrate.sql deletion reappeared
as uncommitted changes exactly as before), then `git checkout --
DeChimexPrints/src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/img/` restored all 16 brand/product/stage
images + reviews/. ffmpeg and Playwright+Chromium (with libnspr4/libnss3 etc.) were
also installed in this session for the media generation and visual verification.
LESSON: after any reset, check `git rev-parse --is-inside-work-tree` and
`ls src/DeChimex.Host/wwwroot/img` FIRST — a missing .git does not mean the work is
lost, and the tracked images are one `git checkout` away.

26. PUBLIC GALLERY GRID GUTTERS (responsive left/right margins)  [2026-09-05]
===============================================================================
REQUEST (owner, with screenshot of localhost:7061): the media grid sat flush
against the viewport edges; owner wanted "space between the view width and the
media grid container, left and right margin ... evaluate the adequate space for
each screen size and apply."

ROOT CAUSE: #grid (.gallery-grid) was the ONE block rendered OUTSIDE the shared
.container shell (hero band, filter pills and footer all live inside it), so it
stretched full-bleed 0..vw while every sibling respected the page gutters.

SPACING EVALUATION (applied): reuse the site's single spacing system instead of
inventing per-breakpoint margins. .gallery-grid now sits inside
<div class="container gallery-shell">, so its left/right inset automatically
equals the container padding (24px) plus the container's own centering:
  <=1280px  -> 24px gutters  (container is fluid, padding only)
  1281-1535 -> gutters grow with (vw-1280)/2 + 24
  >=1536    -> container caps at 1536 (Tailwind .container in tailwind.build.css
               overrides app.css max-width:1280 - measured, see 24) so gutters
               are (vw-1536)/2 + 24, i.e. 216px @1920 and 536px @2560.
Measured grid left edge == hero text left edge at 390/768/1280/1920/2560, so the
grid now aligns on the same vertical rhythm as the headline and filter pills.
.gallery-shell adds padding-top:6px / padding-bottom:14px so the tile block also
breathes vertically between the pill row and the footer.

EXTRA POLISH + ONE REVERTED IDEA: gave .gallery-grid border-radius:14px and
overflow:hidden so the outer corners of the tile block are softly clipped now
that it is inset. A first pass also added background:var(--n-50) and a soft
box-shadow to make the grid read as a "card"; REVERTED in the same session
because when the item count is not a multiple of the column count (6 items in
the 8-column row at >=1400px) the trailing EMPTY cells painted as an obvious
grey slab. Left transparent, a partial last row reads as normal page whitespace
like any photo grid. A comment in the view records this so nobody re-adds the
background/shadow. Admin gallery (/admin/gallery) keeps its own full-bleed copy
of .gallery-grid on purpose - it is a management surface, not a showcase, and
touching it was out of scope (see 13: no unrequested refactors).

FILES TOUCHED: src/DeChimex.Host/Views/StoreGallery/Index.cshtml ONLY
  - wrapped #grid in <div class="container gallery-shell"> ... </div>
  - page <style>: new .gallery-shell rule; .gallery-grid gained
    border-radius:14px;overflow:hidden (no background, no shadow) + comments.
No CSS file, controller, JS or markup-structure change; tiles, filters and the
lightbox are untouched.

VERIFICATION: dotnet build 0 errors. Headless Chromium (script
/tmp/shot_grid_widths.py) at 390/768/1280/1920/2560 -> symmetric gutters
24/24, 24/24, 24/24, 216/216, 536/536; columns 3/5/6/8/8; radius 14px; grid
left == hero left at every width. Screenshots /tmp/grid_<w>.png reviewed:
desktop shows the inset rounded block aligned under the pills with the empty

test DeChimex.sln -> 266/266 (138 unit + 128 integration), unchanged.

    DisplayOrder + Url = FIRST image (grid thumbnail). Children = extra images,

  - Migration 20260905122710_AddGalleryPostCarousel (ParentId + index + FK).

GOTCHAS FIXED (worth remembering)
  1. Severing children from the tracked collection does NOT delete them even
     with OnDelete(Cascade): the tracker nulls the optional FK and orphans them
     into parent-less "posts" that show up in the grid. SetGalleryImagesHandler
     therefore calls gallery.Remove(child) for every replaced child.
  2. data-urls JSON must be emitted RAW inside a SINGLE-QUOTED attribute; manual
     &quot; escaping double-encodes through Razor and breaks JSON.parse.
  3. The carousel counter must be re-rendered per slide (a const captured at
     open froze it at 1/6); the arrow-key listener lives on document because
     swap() removes the focused nav button.
  4. FinalOrder tokens are the single source of truth when present - a "safety
     net" re-adding unmentioned current urls resurrected explicitly removed
     images (E4). No-token fallback (no-JS) = keep order + append uploads.
  5. Admin layout header contains a submit button (logout): test scripts must
     click the Save button by text, never a bare button[type=submit].

32) MIGRATION #8 ON SQL SERVER: SELF-FK CASCADE -> NO ACTION (1785)
============================================================
Owner ran `update-database` against real SQL Server; migration
20260905122710_AddGalleryPostCarousel FAILED with error 1785:
"Introducing FOREIGN KEY constraint 'FK_GalleryMedia_GalleryMedia_ParentId'
... may cause cycles or multiple cascade paths". SQL Server always rejects
ON DELETE CASCADE on a SELF-REFERENCING FK. The InMemory provider accepted it,
which is why 266 tests passed while the real DB blocked.

FIX (4 files, semantics preserved - children are deleted by the app, not the DB):
 1. Configurations/CatalogConfigurations.cs: GalleryMedia self-relation
    OnDelete(Cascade) -> OnDelete(Restrict)  (= ON DELETE NO ACTION in SQL).
 2. Migrations/20260905122710_AddGalleryPostCarousel.cs: AddForeignKey
    ReferentialAction.Cascade -> ReferentialAction.Restrict. Safe in place:
    the migration had never been APPLIED anywhere (failed attempt rolled back;
    InMemory never runs migrations).
 3. Migrations/20260905122710_AddGalleryPostCarousel.Designer.cs + model
    snapshot: same behavior change so history/snapshot stay consistent.
 4. Features/Gallery/Commands/GalleryCommands.cs DeleteGalleryMediaHandler:
    now loads via GetWithImagesAsync and gallery.Remove(...) each carousel
    child BEFORE removing the root (with NO ACTION the DB would otherwise
    reject deleting a post that still has child rows). Child-only deletes are
    unchanged (removing one image still never deletes the record).
    SetGalleryImagesCommand already removed replaced children explicitly.

VERIFICATION:
 - dotnet ef migrations script <#7> <#8> now emits:
   ALTER TABLE [GalleryMedia] ADD CONSTRAINT [FK_GalleryMedia_GalleryMedia_ParentId]
   FOREIGN KEY ([ParentId]) REFERENCES [GalleryMedia] ([Id]) ON DELETE NO ACTION;
 - dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes -> "No changes".
 - script #8 -> head = zero schema ops (designer/model/history consistent).
 - dotnet test 266/266 (nothing relied on InMemory cascade for gallery children).
OWNER ACTION: simply RE-RUN the same update-database command; the failed
attempt left no __EFMigrationsHistory row (transaction rolled back), so #8
applies cleanly from migration #7's state.

PITFALL LOG (for future snapshot surgery): never anchor snapshot/designer
edits on `Entity("<FullName>"` - the FIRST occurrence is the property block;
the relationship block (with HasOne/OnDelete) lives later in the relationship
section. Anchor on the HasOne(...) line instead. A probe `migrations add`
also REGENERATES the main snapshot - useful for self-healing a bad hand edit
(inspect its Up(), then delete the probe files if the diff was hand-edit noise).

9TH SANDBOX RESET (mid-turn): wiped /var/tmp/dotnet SDK itself, .git, bin,
wwwroot/img, ms-playwright. Recovered: dotnet-install.sh -> /var/tmp/dotnet,
clone -> .git, checkout img, rebuild, restart, seed. All §32 edits survived.

35) SIGNUP 500 FIX: ValidationAppException UNHANDLED IN AccountController
============================================================
Owner hit an unhandled DeChimex.Application.Common.Exceptions.ValidationAppException
("One or more validation failures occurred.") at ValidationBehavior line 27 when
submitting /Account/Register (VS break + filled form screenshots).
ROOT CAUSE: the MediatR ValidationBehavior throws ValidationAppException, which
derives from System.Exception - NOT FluentValidation.ValidationException. The
Register POST only caught FluentValidation.ValidationException (dead catch), so
pipeline validation failures escaped as 500s instead of redisplaying the form.
Same latent gap in Login, ForgotPassword, ResetPassword, VerifyEmail (their
validators also run through the behavior; none caught ValidationAppException).
FIX (Controllers/Mvc/AccountController.cs only):
 - new private AddValidationErrors(ValidationAppException): maps ex.Errors into
   ModelState, stripping the "Request." prefix from keys ("Request.Phone" ->
   "Phone") so the views' asp-validation-for spans show field-level messages;
   unmapped keys fall to the validation summary.
 - Register: dead FluentValidation catch replaced with ValidationAppException.
 - Login / ForgotPassword / ResetPassword / VerifyEmail: ValidationAppException
   catch added (ForgotPassword send wrapped in try/catch; anti-enumeration
   behaviour untouched - ViewBag.Sent only set on success).
VERIFIED (Playwright, live): weak password submit -> HTTP 200, form redisplayed
with values kept + inline "Password must contain an uppercase letter." under the
Password field (screenshot gallery-redesign-shots/register_validation.png);
strong password -> account created, redirected to /portal; malformed-email login
-> HTTP 200 with "Enter a valid email address." (was 500). dotnet test 266/266.
NOTE: the owner's rejected signup data itself was fine except the password
policy (phone 09030085049 passes MaxLength(20); email passes EmailAddress()).
12th sandbox reset between turns wiped .git/img/.cache(ms-playwright) again;
running app process survived with the fix loaded; git+img restored per recipe.

36) GATED SIGNUP: BREVO TOKEN EMAIL MUST BE ENTERED TO FINISH
============================================================
Owner: "ensure brevo sends token email and user input's the correct token
before successful sign up". Signup is now gated end-to-end:
 1. Register creates the account UNVERIFIED (24h token on User) and NO session.
    AccountController.Register redirects to /Account/VerifyEmail?email=...
    with a TempData info banner.
 2. UserRegisteredEvent -> verification email via Brevo (BackgroundEmailQueue ->
    BrevoEmailService, api-key from BrevoOptions). The EmailVerification
    template NOW shows the raw token in a dashed code block AND the verify
    button/link ({{Token}} added; both UserEventHandlers pass it).
 3. VerifyEmail accepts the raw token OR the pasted full link (QueryHelpers
    parses token=/email=). Wrong/expired token -> BusinessRuleValidationException
    surfaced in the validation summary. Correct token -> VerifyEmailCommand now
    returns AuthResponse (roles via GetWithRolesAsync - GetByEmailAsync does NOT
    load Roles, which initially produced an AccessDenied redirect) -> cookie
    sign-in via new SignInAsync(AuthResponse) helper -> /portal. Signup is only
    "successful" after this step; welcome email fires on UserEmailVerifiedEvent.
 4. Login gate: LoginHandler throws ForbiddenException for unverified users
    ("Your email is not verified yet...") so the gate can't be bypassed by
    logging in. Staff (CreateStaff) and seeded owner are verified at creation;
    legacy/admin accounts without a pending token are unaffected in practice
    because only public Register issues unverified users.
 5. Resend: new Account/ResendVerification action (existing
    ResendVerificationEmailCommand, anti-enumeration) + "Resend verification
    email" button on the verify page (email input appears when not prefilled).
TESTS: TestSeed gained GetVerificationTokenAsync/VerifyCustomerAsync; all
login-after-register helpers now verify first; AuthFlowTests gained
Register_DoesNotSignIn_AndRedirectsToVerify + Login_BeforeVerification_IsBlocked
and VerifyEmail_WithValidToken now asserts redirect->/portal + session.
Suite: 268/268 (138 unit + 130 integration).
LIVE E2E (sandbox can't receive Brevo mail - IP allowlist; verified via a local
Brevo v3 stub on 127.0.0.1:9999 through Brevo__ApiBaseUrl override, removed
afterwards): captured POST carried the correct api-key header + token code
block; wrong token rejected; typed token -> /portal signed-in (screenshot
gallery-redesign-shots/portal_after_verify.png); welcome email captured;
resend issued a NEW different token. Real Brevo from the owner's machine
delivers normally once their egress IP is allowlisted at
app.brevo.com/security/authorised_ips (401 otherwise - documented earlier).
NOTE: sandbox egress to api.brevo.com intermittently blackholes in-flight TLS
connections (curl fine, app HttpClient hangs) - environment quirk, not code.

42) v2 REPO DEFECT: MISSING RemoveCategoryIconColor MIGRATION RESTORED
    [2026-09-07]
============================================================
SYMPTOM (owner, real SQL Server, running from the v026-RedundancySweep zip
extract in Visual Studio PMC):
  1st error: Update-Database -> "File '...DeChimex.Domain\bin\Debug\net9.0\
     DeChimex.Persistence.dll' not found."  = TOOLING: a fresh unzip has no
     .vs state, so VS defaulted the STARTUP project to DeChimex.Domain (the
     first project in the .sln), which does not reference Persistence, so its
     bin never contains DeChimex.Persistence.dll. Fix: pass
     -StartupProject DeChimex.Host (or set DeChimex.Host as the VS startup
     project and the PMC default project to DeChimex.Persistence). Reproduced
     byte-for-byte in the sandbox.
  2nd error (with correct projects): System.InvalidOperationException for
     PendingModelChangesWarning: "The model for context 'DeChimexDbContext'
     has pending changes. Add a new migration before updating the database."
     = REAL DEFECT in the packaged state (see root cause).

ROOT CAUSE: the v026 zip / DeChimexPrints-v2 repo captured section 39's
Category-IconColor removal only PARTIALLY: the entity/config/DTO/view/test
edits are present (Category no longer has IconColor), but the
..._RemoveCategoryIconColor MIGRATION files and the model-snapshot update were
LOST (another casualty of the sandbox-reset file-eating pattern). The repo
therefore had NINE migrations while the model had moved past the last
snapshot -> EF Core 9 defaults PendingModelChangesWarning to an error and
Migrate() aborts BEFORE applying anything (by design in EF9: model, snapshot
and migrations must agree). Verified in the sandbox:
  dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes -> "Changes have been made to
  the model since the last migration." (Service.IconColor in the snapshot is
  INTENTIONAL - kept per section 39; the drift was Categories only.)

FIX (commit b939536 on the local v2 clone; owner must push):
  dotnet ef migrations add RemoveCategoryIconColor
    --project src/DeChimex.Persistence --startup-project src/DeChimex.Persistence
  (startup = Persistence -> DesignTimeDbContextFactory, per section 32; do NOT
  set Database__UseInMemory when scaffolding - the factory is SqlServer.)
  Generated migration #10 = 20260907204537_RemoveCategoryIconColor:
    Up:   DropColumn("IconColor", "Categories")  - single operation
    Down: AddColumn nvarchar(120) nullable (faithful reverse of initial)
  The scaffold also refreshed DeChimexDbContextModelSnapshot (now exactly ONE
  IconColor - Service's), restoring model/snapshot/migration agreement.
  Verified: has-pending-model-changes -> "No changes have been made...";
  dotnet build 0 errors; dotnet test 272/272 (138 unit + 134 integration);
  10 migrations total. The column's data loss was already owner-acknowledged
  in section 39.

ALSO THIS SESSION (13th sandbox reset, mid-turn): .git + the 16 tracked
wwwroot/img brand images were wiped from the worktree again. Recovered per the
section 25 recipe (clone origin -> cp .git back -> git checkout -- img).
Because the keys commit (originally 9af909b) was local-only and never pushed,
it was recreated as 8205d6b. LESSON REINFORCED: push to origin after every
session - local commits do not survive resets.

PACKAGING: tools/make-deliverable-zip.sh recreated in the v2 repo (lost in the
v2 transition) as the single packaging entry point (section 31 rule: the
appsettings*.json WITH keys always ship; only git-tracked files are packaged).
Produced /home/user/v027-CategoriesMigrationAndKeys.zip = v026 layout
(DeChimexPrints/ + AI_HANDOFF.txt) + keys + migration #10 + img assets.

OWNER ACTION (choose ONE, not both):

43) SSPI HANDSHAKE FAILURE ON REAL SQL SERVER -> Encrypt=False  [2026-09-07]
============================================================
SYMPTOM (owner, real SQL Server, v027 extract, correct PMC projects):
  Update-Database -Project DeChimex.Persistence -StartupProject DeChimex.Host
  -> Microsoft.Data.SqlClient.SqlException "Failed to generate SSPI context."
  at TdsParser.ProcessSSPI during CompleteLogin (stack reaches a REAL login
  attempt -> the EF tooling chain is fully healthy now; this is a SQL-client
  <-> server TLS/security handshake failure, not a project bug).

DIAGNOSIS:
  - The project uses EF Core 9 (Microsoft.Data.SqlClient 5.x), in which
    Encrypt=True is the DEFAULT (since SqlClient 4.0). "Failed to generate
    SSPI context" is the classic symptom of the TLS negotiation failing
    during the encrypted pre-login/login handshake.
  - Section 32 (2026-09-05) proves the SAME connection string connected fine
    two days earlier (it reached real SQL error 1785), so something on the
    owner machine changed since: most likely the dechimex-sql Docker
    container was recreated/updated (docs/04 pins mcr.microsoft.com/
    mssql/server:2022-latest - a MUTABLE tag, so a re-pull swaps the image
    and its generated self-signed cert/TLS state), or a Windows/Docker
    update changed Schannel TLS behaviour. Owner to confirm what changed.
  - Local dev does not need TLS on a loopback connection; docs/04 and
    docs/08 already mandate Encrypt=True + a real certificate only for
    PRODUCTION.

FIX (commit 0539d82 on the local v2 clone; owner must push):
  - Appended ";Encrypt=False" to ConnectionStrings:DefaultConnection in BOTH
    src/DeChimex.Host/appsettings.json AND appsettings.Development.json (the
    Development file overrides the base at runtime - same trap as the blank
    Paystack keys in section 41). TrustServerCertificate=True kept (moot with
    Encrypt=False, but needed again if encryption is ever re-enabled).
  - With Encrypt=False the client skips the mandatory TLS handshake and the
    container (ForceEncryption off by default) accepts a plain connection.
  - The -Connection override form for a one-shot test:
      Update-Database -Project DeChimex.Persistence -StartupProject DeChimex.Host `
        -Connection "Server=localhost,1433;Database=DeChimexDb;User Id=sa;Password=Dev@2026Pass!;TrustServerCertificate=True;Encrypt=False"
  - PRODUCTION GUIDANCE UNCHANGED: production uses appsettings.Production.json
    (git-ignored) or env vars with Encrypt=True + valid cert (docs/04 line 17,
    docs/08 deployment notes).

VERIFICATION LIMITS: no real SQL Server exists in the AI sandbox (section 12),
so the actual handshake cannot be exercised here; the change is config-only
(both files JSON-valid, Encrypt=False confirmed inside v028 zip), build/tests
unaffected by design (tests use InMemory + a dummy connection string). Owner
confirms live via the PMC command above.

ALSO THIS SESSION (14th sandbox reset): .git wiped again mid-session; the
entire local commit stack (keys, migration, tools script) had to be rebuilt
as c1979a2 / 6529f00 / f4f6e89 / 0539d82 after recovering .git from origin
(section 25 recipe) - local commits do NOT survive resets; PUSH after every
session. Packaged /home/user/v028-SspiEncryptFix.zip (= v027 + Encrypt=False,
665 entries, keys + migration #10 + AI_HANDOFF.txt verified inside).

44) SIGNUP TOKEN EMAILS NOT ARRIVING -> BREVO AUTHORIZED-IP ALLOWLIST
    [2026-09-07]
============================================================
SYMPTOM (owner): registered with hjkl00@yopmail.com (disposable Yopmail
inbox, per their screenshot of /Account/VerifyEmail); "Resend verification
email" shows the anti-enumeration banner but NO token email ever arrives.

DIAGNOSIS (proven live from the sandbox with the committed key):
  curl -H "api-key: $KEY" https://api.brevo.com/v3/account ->
  HTTP 401 {"message":"We have detected you are using an unrecognised IP
  address 136.66.48.230. If you performed this action make sure to add the
  new IP address in this link: https://app.brevo.com/security/authorised_ips",
  "code":"unauthorized"}
  => The API KEY IS VALID (Brevo recognised it; an invalid key returns a
  different error). The account has the AUTHORIZED-IP ALLOWLIST feature
  ENABLED: every API call from a non-allowlisted IP is rejected with 401.
  The app's EmailQueue is fire-and-forget: BrevoEmailService logs
  "Brevo send failed (Unauthorized) for {to}: {body}" and swallows it, so
  signup appears successful but no mail is ever sent. The same 401 wall hits
  the owner's machine (their egress IP is not on the list either) - this is
  the documented section 8/9 operational note, now actually biting.

CONSOLE TRIAGE (BrevoEmailService log lines - tell these apart):
  - "Email sending disabled or unconfigured. Would send '...' to ..." ->
    running a KEYS-BLANK build (e.g. the old v026 extract) -> switch to v028.
  - "Brevo send failed (Unauthorized) ... unrecognised IP address ..." ->
    allowlist block -> fix in Brevo dashboard (below).
  - "Email '...' sent to {to}." -> Brevo ACCEPTED the mail; if the inbox is
    still empty, suspect recipient-side filtering (Yopmail/disposable domain)
    -> retest with a Gmail address.

OWNER FIX (requires their Brevo web login - cannot be done from the sandbox):
  A) app.brevo.com -> profile -> Security -> Authorized IPs
     (https://app.brevo.com/security/authorised_ips) -> ADD the machine's
     current PUBLIC IP (find via whatismyip.com). Home IPs are often dynamic;
     re-add when it changes.
  B) OR deactivate the IP restriction entirely (acceptable for test-mode
     keys / dev).
  Optionally ALSO add the sandbox IP shown in the 401 message so the AI
  session can run end-to-end send tests (IP may change after sandbox resets;
  re-check the 401 message for the current one).

SECOND KNOWN BLOCKER (check while in the dashboard): Brevo requires the
SENDER to be validated. The app sends from Brevo:SenderEmail =
no-reply@dechimexprinthub.com (appsettings). Senders & IP -> Senders must
list it as active/validated; if the domain is not owned/verified in Brevo,
sends fail EVEN AFTER the IP fix (console will show a sender-invalid error).
If unverified: either verify the sender/domain in Brevo, or tell the AI the
address that IS verified (usually the Brevo signup email) and the config
will be updated + re-zipped.

VERIFICATION PATH: after the allowlist fix, click "Resend verification
email" on /Account/VerifyEmail, watch the console for
"Email 'Verify your email' sent to hjkl00@yopmail.com." then check the
Yopmail inbox (yopmail.com, enter hjkl00). BrevoHealthCheck is NOT a valid
verifier for this - it probes GET /v3 without the api-key, so it always
reports "Brevo reachable (HTTP 401)" even when everything works.

45) EMAIL FLOW FIXED END-TO-END: SENDER SWAP + LIVE DELIVERY PROOF
    [2026-09-07 evening]
============================================================
OWNER QUESTION: "does the sender email address actually have to exist cause
it's a domain email?" ANSWER: the SENDER MAILBOX does not need to exist, but
the sender must be VERIFIED in Brevo, either (a) DOMAIN verification (add
Brevo's DNS records - no mailbox needed, any address @domain then works) or
(b) INDIVIDUAL sender verification (Brevo emails that address; something
must receive that one mail). For the app's no-reply@dechimexprinthub.com,
NEITHER was possible: dechimexprinthub.com is NXDOMAIN (verified via
dns.google DoH - NO DNS records AT ALL), so the domain cannot be verified and
no address on it can receive mail. GET /v3/senders showed the account's ONLY
verified/active sender = bakareahmadio@gmail.com (the Brevo signup email);
sends from any other address are rejected by Brevo -> the token emails could
never send, even with authorized IPs.

DIAGNOSIS STEPS THAT GOT US HERE (reusable for future email issues):
  1. curl -H "api-key: $KEY" /v3/account -> 401 unrecognised IP = allowlist
     (owner then authorized the sandbox IP 136.66.48.230; note the IP may
     change across sandbox resets - re-read the 401 message for the current
     one). After: HTTP 200 with full account (free plan, 300 mails/day).
  2. curl /v3/senders -> sender inventory + active flags.
  3. DoH DNS check (dig not installed; dns.google/resolve works):
     A/MX/TXT all NXDOMAIN -> domain unconfigured.
  4. Brevo EVENTS API = authoritative delivery proof:
     GET /v3/smtp/statistics/events?days=1&email=... -> requests/delivered/
     opened/bounced/deferred events with timestamps.

FIX: Brevo:SenderEmail in appsettings.json (base only; Development does not
override it) no-reply@dechimexprinthub.com -> bakareahmadio@gmail.com
(SenderName kept "DE-CHIMEX PRINT HUB"). FLIP-BACK CONDITION: once the owner
registers/configures DNS for dechimexprinthub.com, verify the domain in
Brevo (Senders & IP -> Domains), then restore the no-reply sender AND set
App:FrontendBaseUrl to the real site so emailed links become clickable.

LIVE E2E PROOF (sandbox app, InMemory, :5119, real Brevo account):
  - Registered hjkl00@yopmail.com through the REAL MVC form (antiforgery
    token harvested; POST /Account/Register -> 302 /Account/VerifyEmail).
  - App log: "Queued verification email for hjkl00@yopmail.com" then
    "Email 'Verify your DE-CHIMEX email' sent to hjkl00@yopmail.com." (that
    line fires ONLY on HTTP 200 from Brevo).
  - Brevo events: requests 23:01:51 -> DELIVERED 23:01:52 -> opened 23:02:15.
    Envelope sender rewritten by Brevo to bakareahmudio@11698602.brevosend.com
    (normal relay behaviour; display From stays as configured).
  - Yopmail NOTE: scripted inbox reading is blocked (JS auto-submit form +
    "Complete the CAPTCHA to continue"); use the Brevo events API as proof.

KNOWN RESIDUAL QUIRKS:
  - Email verification LINKS point at App:FrontendBaseUrl
    (https://dechimexprinthub.com - NXDOMAIN) -> links are not clickable
    until the domain exists; users must copy the TOKEN (shown in a dashed

46) UX FIX: UNVERIFIED LOGIN STRANDED USERS (no token field on login page)
    [2026-09-07 night]
============================================================
SYMPTOM (owner screenshot of /Account/Login): after a disrupted email
verification session, the user tried to log in and got "Your email is not
verified yet. Enter the verification token we emailed you to finish signing
up." - but the LOGIN form only has Email/Password. The error message told
them to enter a token on a page that has NO token field and NO link to the
verify page. The token input lives only on /Account/VerifyEmail, so a user
who lost that page (closed the tab, session disrupted, navigated away) was
STUCK mid-signup with no way back.
ROOT CAUSE: LoginHandler threw a generic ForbiddenException for the
unverified case; AccountController.Login's catch just put the message on
ModelState and re-showed the login form.
Also relevant to "disrupted session": the sandbox preview app RESTARTS
between AI sessions (processes do not survive), and with
Database__UseInMemory=true its DB is wiped - accounts registered on the
preview vanish. On the owner's SQL-Server instance accounts persist, so
this fix fully solves it there; for sandbox testing, re-register first.

FIX (commit 1fb8199):
  1. ApplicationExceptions.cs: ForbiddenException un-sealed (public surface
     unchanged) + NEW EmailNotVerifiedException : ForbiddenException (still
     caught by every existing catch(ForbiddenException)/403 middleware path,
     so only the MVC login flow changes behaviour).
  2. Login.cs: the unverified throw now uses EmailNotVerifiedException
     (message unchanged).
  3. AccountController.Login POST: new catch (EmailNotVerifiedException)
     BEFORE the generic ForbiddenException catch -> TempData["Info"] banner
     + RedirectToAction(VerifyEmail, new { email }) - the page that HAS the
     token input, accepts the pasted link, and offers "Resend verification
     email". Disabled-account ForbiddenExceptions still show on the login
     form as before.
  4. AuthFlowTests.Login_BeforeVerification_IsBlocked updated: now asserts
     302 -> /Account/VerifyEmail?email=..., then 200 with the info banner
     ("not verified"), the "Verification token" field, and the prefilled
     email. NOTE for future edits: the redirect Location keeps '@'
     UNESCAPED (email=...@example.com); asserting Uri.EscapeDataString(email)
     fails on %40 - assert the raw email.

VERIFICATION: build 0 errors (16 pre-existing warnings); tests 272/272
(138 unit + 134 integration). LIVE on the sandbox app: re-registered
hjkl00@yopmail.com -> POST login (unverified) -> 302 to
/Account/VerifyEmail?email=hjkl00@yopmail.com -> page shows banner + token
field + prefilled email + resend button; fresh registration email SENT
(app log "Email 'Verify your DE-CHIMEX email' sent...") and Brevo events

47) VERIFICATION TOKEN -> 6-DIGIT NUMERIC OTP + BRUTE-FORCE LOCKOUT
    [2026-09-07 late]
============================================================
OWNER REQUEST: "make the token a 6 digit number, it's much better than e.g.
XD1DuNscrQZ0wcS2lEoBWydFISWO2T-fCDUZ52_Bs1bIPXfSP5do4V_Yns7Eo_E8".

DESIGN (keyspace shrank from 2^384-ish to 10^6, so brute-force protection
was added in the same change - not optional):
  - ISecureTokenGenerator gained GenerateNumericToken(digits=6):
    RandomNumberGenerator.GetInt32 (crypto-random, rejection-sampled, NO
    modulo bias), formatted D6 so LEADING ZEROS are preserved ("012345").
    NOTE: GetInt64 does NOT exist on this TFM - use GetInt32 (10^9 fits int).
  - RegisterHandler + ResendVerificationEmailHandler now issue the 6-digit
    OTP (the user TYPES it). ForgotPasswordHandler KEEPS GenerateToken()
    (long opaque string) - reset codes arrive as a CLICKED LINK, so keep the
    free keyspace. Do not "unify" these without thinking.
  - User entity: new EmailVerificationAttempts counter + const
    MaxEmailVerificationAttempts = 10. VerifyEmail(token):
    * at cap -> "user.verify.locked" ("Too many incorrect attempts. Request
      a new verification email (the code has been invalidated).")
    * wrong token -> increment; on the 10th wrong attempt the code is
      NULLIFIED (even the correct code is dead afterwards) + locked error;
      attempts 1-9 -> normal "user.verify.token" "Invalid verification
      token."
    * success / RequestEmailVerification (resend) reset the counter to 0
      (fresh code = fresh attempt budget).
  - VerifyEmailHandler: try/catch around user.VerifyEmail; on
    BusinessRuleValidationException it SAVES BEFORE RETHROWING so the
    attempt counter persists even though the command failed. Without this
    the cap never sticks (each request re-counts from 0).
  - Migration #11: 20260907224338_AddEmailVerificationAttempts (AddColumn
    int NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 on Users; Down drops it). ELEVEN migrations now.
    SecurityConfigurations maps the property explicitly.
  - Email template unchanged ({{Token}}) - a 6-digit code renders fine in
    the dashed code box; the emailed LINK still works too (token or link
    accepted by the verify form).

TESTS (284/284 = 149 unit + 135 integration):
  - NEW SecureTokenGeneratorTests (5): 2000 draws all ^[0-9]{6}$, digit
    count parameterization + leading zeros, distinctness (>1980/2000),
    rejects 0/10 digits, opaque token still >=48 chars URL-safe.
  - NEW UserVerificationTests (5 domain): correct verifies+clears, wrong
    counts, 10 wrong invalidates even the correct code, locked-after-lockout,
    resend resets budget.
  - NEW AuthFlowTests.VerifyEmail_SixDigitCode_Lockout_Then_Resend_NewCode_Works:
    end-to-end over HTTP - 9 wrong (invalid) -> 10th locked -> original code
    dead -> resend issues a DIFFERENT fresh 6-digit code -> verify -> 302
    /portal. This test proves the handler-persist-on-throw works per request.

VERIFIED LIVE (:5119, InMemory, real Brevo): registered hjkl00@yopmail.com,
submitted 10 wrong codes -> messages went "Invalid verification token." x9
then "Too many incorrect attempts...code has been invalidated."; resend ->
302 + app log "Email 'Verify your DE-CHIMEX email' sent" (fresh 6-digit
code delivered). Owner can complete signup on the live preview from the
Yopmail inbox.

DEBUGGING LESSONS RECORDED (both cost real time this session):
  1. TWO edit_file calls to the SAME file issued in ONE parallel tool block
     RACE - the second write clobbers the first (seen earlier with the
     appsettings keys, and AGAIN here: the VerifyEmailHandler try/catch
     silently vanished while the sibling using-line edit survived).
     RULE: sequential edits per file; parallel edits only across DIFFERENT
     files.
  2. When scripting against /Account/VerifyEmail: the page contains TWO
     forms (verify + resend), each rendering the antiforgery hidden input,
     so a naive grep harvest returns the token TWICE (two lines) and the
     doubled value fails antiforgery with a misleading "could not be
     decrypted" 400. Always | head -1 the token harvest.
  3. Integration-vs-curl: the xUnit test passed while curl failed - the
     test's Regex.Match takes the FIRST match only (equivalent to head -1).

DELIVERABLE: /home/user/v031-SixDigitOtp.zip (670 files; includes
AI_HANDOFF with this section). LOCAL COMMITS (17th reset consolidated
everything into one stack - owner MUST push): 87de717 (keys + Encrypt=False
+ sender + migration #10 + login redirect + 6-digit OTP + tests, all
consolidated) + 9ac5f62 (exec bit). After the owner pushes, future resets
recover cleanly from origin instead of re-piling unpushed work.

49) FIX: CONTACT PAGE SUCCESS BANNER RENDERED TWICE  [2026-09-08]
============================================================
OWNER REPORT (screenshot): after submitting /contact, "Message sent! We
will get back to you shortly." appeared TWICE. Instruction: keep the one
that renders right above the form.

ROOT CAUSE: two renders of the SAME TempData["Success"] value:
  1. Views/StorePages/Contact.cshtml line ~38 renders its own green banner
     right above the form card (the one the owner wants KEPT);
  2. Views/Shared/_PublicLayout.cshtml line ~128 renders the shared flash
     block ("flash flash-success") at the top of <main> - a generic public
     flash added long ago for wishlist feedback (section 18).
  A Razor layout executes AFTER the view body, so both rendered the same
  message. Verified by OCR of the owner screenshot (2 occurrences) and in
  served HTML before the fix.

FIX (Contact.cshtml ONLY - the shared layout flash is untouched so other
public pages keep their feedback): the view banner now calls
TempData.Remove("Success") right after reading it, CONSUMING the key so
the layout (executing later in the same request) finds nothing to render.
This consume-in-view pattern is the standard way to avoid double flashes
when a page wants its own placement.

SCOPE CHECK: grepped every view - only Contact.cshtml had this double
render (the Admin/Portal/Public layouts render their flash once each;
VerifyEmail.cshtml is self-contained with no layout). No other page is
affected.

VERIFICATION: build 0 errors; ContactMessagesTests + MvcStorefrontTests
21/21 green; LIVE: submitted the contact form -> redirected -> banner
occurs EXACTLY ONCE in the served HTML, positioned immediately above the
form card (index check), and no flash-success block at page top.
Committed as 7aec56b (consolidated stack rebuilt after the 21st reset);
v032-ContactFlashFix.zip packaged.

51) EMAIL/MARKETING SYSTEM - FULL IMPLEMENTATION + §18 REPORT
    [2026-09-08]
============================================================
OWNER REQUEST: complete the 18-section email/marketing spec
(transactional gaps + admin broadcast system), inspect first, no
duplicate services, test everything, deliver a final report.

WHAT ALREADY EXISTED (inspection, no duplication):
- 8 transactional email templates + BackgroundEmailQueue
  (fire-and-forget Task.Run) + BrevoEmailService + branded
  EmailTemplateRenderer + IAppUrlProvider. Triggers existed for:
  email verification, password reset, welcome (post-verification),
  OTP resend, order confirmation, order status (InProduction/
  Ready/Completed/Delivered), contact-form staff copy + owner reply.
- PaymentReceipt template existed but was DEAD CODE (no trigger).
- Cancelled/Refunded statuses were filtered OUT of the status email.
- No failed-payment email, no direct-password-change security email,
  no marketing system, no unsubscribe, no permission entries.

WHAT WAS IMPLEMENTED:
A) TRANSACTIONAL GAPS
- Status emails now include Cancelled + Refunded (OrderEventHandlers
  filter extended).
- PaymentFailedEvent (raised in Payment.MarkFailed) ->
  PaymentFailedEventHandler -> "Payment failed" email to the customer.
- Receipt email now actually sends: PaymentConfirmationService raises
  it via the (previously dead) PaymentReceipt template after payment
  confirmation.
- ChangePasswordHandler now sends a "password changed" security email
  (try/swallow - a failure never blocks the change).
B) MARKETING BROADCAST SYSTEM
- Domain: MarketingBroadcast aggregate (Draft->Queued->Sending->
  Completed; Queue() is DRAFT-ONLY with an at-most-once recipient
  ledger - double-click/refresh Send is a rejected no-op) +
  MarketingBroadcastRecipient ledger rows (Pending/Sent/Failed;
  Sent rows are never re-picked; RetryFailed requeues FAILED only).
  Customer: +MarketingSubscribed (default TRUE) + UnsubscribeToken
  (Guid, unique index, assigned in constructor).
- Shared: MarketingHtmlSanitizer (dependency-free blocklist: script/
  iframe/object/embed/form/input/button/select/textarea/link/meta/
  base tags, on* handlers, javascript:/vbscript:/data:text/html URLs;
  sanitization runs BEFORE persistence). Permissions: marketing.view +
  marketing.manage (registry 35 -> 37; Owner+Manager get both, staff/
  Designer/Customer do NOT; fresh-DB caveat as §50).
- Application: CreateMarketingBroadcast (sanitize->persist),
  SendMarketingBroadcast (snapshot eligible recipients + queue; NEVER
  sends inline), RetryFailedRecipients, queries for list/details/
  recipient-count/preview (page materialized in memory - enum.ToString
  is not EF-translatable). Contracts: MarketingDtos with string
  statuses (Contracts must not reference Application/Domain).
- Eligibility = registered customers only: !IsGuest && Email != null
  && MarketingSubscribed. Staff are structurally excluded (staff are
  User aggregates, never Customers). Consent is RE-CHECKED at send
  time in the worker (unsubscribe between queue and send wins).
- Infrastructure: BrevoEmailService.TrySendAsync (bool; disabled/
  unconfigured -> false); RenderMarketing (brand shell + per-recipient
  {{FirstName}}/{{UnsubscribeLink}} + auto-appended unsubscribe
  footer); PaymentFailed template; AppUrlProvider unsubscribe link.
- Host: MarketingBroadcastBackgroundService + MarketingBroadcastProcessor
  (poll loop, BatchSize commit-per-batch, per-recipient failure
  isolation); AdminMarketingController (/admin/marketing: Index,
  Compose, Details, server-rendered Preview, Send, Retry; View gate,
  Manage gate for writes); UnsubscribeController (GET confirm form +
  POST opt-out, AllowAnonymous, token-based, NO login needed);
  views AdminMarketing/{Index,Compose,Details} + Unsubscribe
  (standalone page, no admin layout); admin sidebar "Marketing" link
  behind Can("marketing.view").
- Dev safeguard: Marketing:AllowBroadcasts defaults FALSE in
  appsettings.json (prod must set Marketing__AllowBroadcasts=true to
  arm sending); Development.json has true. BatchSize=50,
  PollIntervalSeconds=10.

MIGRATION #12 (20260908095946_AddMarketingBroadcasts; 12 total):
- CreateTable MarketingBroadcasts + MarketingBroadcastRecipients
  (FK cascade, unique index (BroadcastId,CustomerId), index Status).
- AddColumn Customers.MarketingSubscribed (bit, default TRUE) +
  UnsubscribeToken (uniqueidentifier, unique index) + inline SQL
  backfill: UPDATE Customers SET UnsubscribeToken = NEWID() WHERE
  token = Guid.Empty (existing rows get DISTINCT tokens BEFORE the
  unique index is created - without this the migration would fail on
  any DB with 2+ customers).
- CAUTIONARY TALE (do not repeat): the first scaffold was generated
  against a stale assembly (empty Up) and left the snapshot with the
  marketing model, so a re-add produced another empty migration and
  a "revert snapshot to origin" produced a mega-migration that
  re-dropped IconColor/re-added EmailVerificationAttempts. Correct
  recovery: `dotnet ef migrations remove --force` (restores the
  snapshot from migration #11's Designer) then re-add. Also: grep for
  "migrations.CreateTable" NEVER matches - the class is
  "migrationBuilder".
- ROLE PERMISSION MAPS: unchanged in the seeder (Owner=All() picks up
  marketing automatically; Manager=All minus users.* likewise; other
  roles use explicit arrays without marketing). Existing databases
  will NOT re-sync role permissions (seeder only assigns at role
  creation) - on the owner's live DB, grant marketing.view/manage to
  Owner via /admin/users/roles if it predates this change (it does
  not: owner's DB predates §50 too, see that section's same caveat).

CONFIG / BREVO SETUP (unchanged from previous sections):
- Brevo section already in appsettings.Development.json (§31 keys
  may be committed/shipped; Production uses env vars/secrets; key
  NEVER frontend/hardcoded).
- New env knobs: Marketing__AllowBroadcasts (true to arm),
  Marketing__BatchSize (50), Marketing__PollIntervalSeconds (10).
- Sandbox testing: Brevo needs the CURRENT egress IP authorized
  (check via https://api.brevo.com/v3/account - 200 = authorized;
  the 401 body reveals the current egress IP). IPs rotate on reset.

INTENTIONALLY NOT IMPLEMENTED (with reasons):
- Brevo contact/list sync: would duplicate the customer DB as a
  second source of truth; per-recipient sends + local tracking
  ledger + local unsubscribe token avoid Brevo re-subscribe races.
- Drag-and-drop email designer: spec says unnecessary.
- New order statuses (Shipped etc.): Delivered covers it; Refund is
  covered by the existing status email.

LIMITATIONS (documented for the owner):
- Strict transactional/marketing separation: unsubscribing ONLY opts
  out of marketing; transactional emails (orders/receipts/security)
  always send.
- In an environment with AllowBroadcasts=true but no working Brevo
  config, the worker marks recipients Failed ("Email provider
  rejected the send.") - by design, visible in the broadcast Details.
- Rate limits: Brevo free tier 300/day - keep broadcasts to subsets
  or upgrade the plan for large audiences.

TESTS (all green, FULL SUITE 317/317 = 171 unit + 146 integration;
baseline was 284):
- NEW MarketingHtmlSanitizerTests (22): script blocks, active tags
  (incl. form controls), on* handlers, javascript:/vbscript:/
  data:text/html URLs neutralized, legitimate markup preserved
  verbatim, mixed-attack document.
- NEW MarketingBroadcastTests (7 integration): unsubscribe invalid
  token page; valid token confirm->opt-out (MarketingSubscribed
  false in DB); anonymous access; compose sanitizes + creates Draft;
  missing subject re-renders with error; Send snapshots recipients
  (2 registered customers) and SECOND SEND IS REJECTED with
  "already been queued" (duplicate-send guard, race-proof
  assertions vs the background poller); /admin/marketing as Customer
  redirects to AccessDenied (no marketing perms for customers).
- MvcAdminTests AdminPaths += /admin/marketing + /admin/marketing/
  compose (4 new theory cases).
- RolePermissionChecklistTests updated: registry 37, Owner 37,
  Manager 32 (31 after the settings.manage uncheck edit).

tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/EmailWorkflowE2ETests.cs

LESSONS (do not re-learn):
- The app uses SERILOG (builder.Host.UseSerilog). Microsoft
  ILoggerProvider registrations are IGNORED by the Serilog logger
  factory -> a test CaptureLoggerProvider sees NOTHING. Use the
  Brevo events API as the authoritative delivery proof instead.
- WebApplicationFactory does NOT default to Development here:
  the Brevo key + Marketing:AllowBroadcasts live in
  appsettings.Development.json, so the E2E factory must call
  builder.UseEnvironment("Development") or every send silently
  no-ops/disables.
- HttpClient with AllowAutoRedirect=true FOLLOWS the 302 from a
  successful form POST -> assert redirect on a client configured
  AllowAutoRedirect=false.
- Aborted E2E attempts still send real emails for the phases that
  completed before the assert failed - the inbox can contain
  partial duplicate batches (11:49 = partial, 11:51-11:52 = the
  full verified set).

57) INVENTORY VALUE NOW RESPONDS TO SUCCESSFUL ORDERS (OWNER
    DIRECTIVE)  [2026-09-08]
============================================================
OWNER ASK: "confirm if inventory value isn't affected upon a successful
order and fix".

CONFIRMED BEHAVIOUR (pre-fix): a successful (paid) order only RESERVES
stock (PaymentConfirmationService -> OrderFulfilmentService.Reserve:
QuantityReserved += qty, OnHand unchanged); OnHand deducts LATER when
staff advance the order to InProduction (UpdateOrderStatus ->
StockOut). The dashboard KPI "Inventory value"
(Persistence/Queries/DashboardUserQueries.GetKpisAsync) valued raw
QuantityOnHand x SellingPrice - so at order success the value NEVER
moved (and never moved at all for counter orders left in Paid).

FIX: value AVAILABLE stock (OnHand minus Reserved) - the same
definition the catalog, inventory list and low-stock KPI already use.
A paid order now drops the value immediately (reservation commits the
units); the later fulfilment StockOut moves Reserved into the OnHand
deduction, leaving Available - and the value - unchanged (no double
deduction).

VERIFICATION:
 - NEW test tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/
   InventoryValueOnOrderTests.cs: product + inventory (10 @ 2000) ->
   KPI 20,000; paid 3-unit order -> KPI 14,000 with OnHand 10 /
   Reserved 3; status InProduction -> OnHand 7 / Reserved 0 and KPI
   still 14,000.
 - dotnet build 0 errors; dotnet test 327/327 GREEN (171 unit + 156
   integration).
 - Live smoke on the sandbox instance: created category + product
   (10 units @ 2000; note: product SKU must match XXX-XXXXX or be left
   blank for auto-suggest), dashboard showed 20,000.00 BEFORE and
   14,000.00 AFTER a paid 3-unit cash order.

AFFECTED FILES: Persistence/Queries/DashboardUserQueries.cs,
  tests .../InventoryValueOnOrderTests.cs (new).

DELIVERABLE: v039-InventoryValueOnOrderSuccess.zip (supersedes v038).

§60 (2026-09-09) — BLANK PAGE ROOT CAUSE FOUND & FIXED (owner report: intermittent
completely blank pages, e.g. Admin → Users and public → Products; URL correct, no
error page, restart did not help, "started working later").
  ROOT CAUSE: the global rate limiter (ServiceCollectionExtensions.AddRateLimiting
  Configured) had NO OnRejected handler. ASP.NET Core's RateLimiterMiddleware default
  rejection = HTTP 429 with an EMPTY BODY. The 404-only status-code pages could not
  catch it (the limiter short-circuits upstream of them), so the browser rendered a
  blank document at the correct URL. Limit was 100 req/min/IP: every missing uploaded
  image falls through UseStaticFiles and burns a permit via the 404 re-execute, so one
  heavy page (Products with missing thumbnails) could cost 13+ permits; a burst of
  navigation exhausted the window → blank. "Heals later" = the 1-minute window reset.
  "Restart didn't help" = the fresh window re-filled within seconds of resumed browsing.
  REPRO PROOF (pre-fix): 105 sequential GET /products → 100×200 then 429 body 0 bytes.
  FIX (all generic, no page-specific code):
   1. OnRejected always writes a real response: styled HTML error page ("Too many
      requests" + Try again button) for browser requests, JSON envelope
      (errorCode too.many.requests) for /api, Retry-After header for both.
   2. Limits: RateLimiting:PermitLimit (default 600/min, was 100),
      RateLimiting:WindowSeconds (default 60). /hubs/* (authenticated SignalR) exempt
      via NoLimiter partition — negotiate/reconnect must not starve page loads.
   3. Blank-page safety net (Program.cs status-code pages): ANY empty-body 4xx/5xx on
      a browser request is re-executed to a real page (404 → /Error/NotFound, other →
      /Error?code=N). /api keeps its JSON contract (404 JSON preserved); loop-guarded.
   4. ExceptionHandlingMiddleware: if the response already started, rethrow instead of
      corrupting a half-sent document; HTML rendering moved to shared
      Middleware/ErrorPageWriter (used by exception handler + rate limiter).
   5. ErrorController.Error now uses ErrorPageWriter.TitleFor for non-404 codes.
  TESTS: tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/RateLimitingTests.cs (4): browser 429 = HTML
  page + Retry-After; API 429 = JSON envelope; /hubs exempt; within-limit unaffected.
  CustomWebApplicationFactory gained protected virtual DisableRateLimiting (true) —
  the rate-limit tests keep the real limiter (PermitLimit 3 / 1s window).
  VERIFIED: full suite 355/355; live burst → 429 with 1,603-byte HTML + Retry-After:60;
  Playwright: normal browsing fine, over-limit shows visible 429 page (not blank),
  Try again recovers after window; 404 HTML + API JSON 404 unchanged; /Error?code=503
  renders; owner login → password change → /admin/users 200 renders.

§61 (2026-09-09) — PRODUCT DATA RULES (owner directive): no $0 products, no image-less
products, hero-image placeholder REMOVED.
  RULES ENFORCED (three layers each):
   1. Price > 0: ProductFormViewModel [Range(0.01,...)] (client + server ModelState),
      Create/UpdateProductValidator GreaterThan(0), and domain guards in Product.Create/
      Product.Update (BusinessRuleValidationException "product.price"). Money itself still
      allows 0 (legit for totals/discounts) - the rule lives on the product.
   2. At least one image: create POST rejects empty/zero-byte uploads ("Upload at least one
      product image."); edit POST requires an upload only while the product has NO images
      (ViewBag.NeedsImage drives the required attribute + hint); domain Product.RemoveImage
      refuses to remove the LAST image ("product.image.last") and the controller surfaces it
      as a TempData flash on the product page.
   3. Hero fallback removed from all 4 spots (StoreProducts Index/Details main+zoom+related,
      PortalShop). New shared Views/Shared/_ProductThumb.cshtml renders the real image or a
      neutral dashed "No image" tile (legacy rows only). PortalWishlist already omitted
      image-less thumbs correctly - untouched.

§63 (2026-09-09) — "MESSAGE RESPONSE DOESN'T SEND EMAIL" — ROOT CAUSE: BREVO REJECTS
UNAUTHORISED IPs (401), AND THE OLD CODE SWALLOWED THE FAILURE WHILE THE UI CLAIMED
"Reply emailed to X".
  EVIDENCE: GET https://api.brevo.com/v3/senders with the committed key returned
  401 {"code":"unauthorized","message":"...unrecognised IP address... add the new IP
  address in .../security/authorised_ips"} - the Brevo account has IP authorisation ON.
  From the owner's machine every Brevo send (message replies AND OTP/password/order/
  marketing emails) fails the same way; /admin/messages/{id}/reply persisted the reply
  and flashed success regardless because the reply went through the fire-and-forget
  BackgroundEmailQueue and TrySendAsync failures were only logged.
  APP FIX (honest delivery reporting):
   - SendContactMessageReplyCommand now returns ContactReplyResult(Emailed, Failure) and
     the handler sends via IEmailService.TrySendAsync SYNCHRONOUSLY (staff need immediate
     truthful feedback; low-volume path). The reply is always persisted on the thread.
   - Controller flashes: success "Reply emailed to X." or error "Reply saved on the
     thread, but the email to X did NOT go out. ...check Brevo authorised-IP settings
     and the app log".
   - Tests never hit live Brevo anymore: CustomWebApplicationFactory swaps in a
     recording FakeEmailService (Fakes/FakeEmailService.cs); reply tests assert both the
     success flash + dispatched email and the honest failure flash with the thread kept.
     Suite 372/372.
  OWNER ACTION (the actual fix for delivery):
   1. Brevo dashboard -> Settings/Security -> Authorised IPs: add the server's public IP,
      or turn IP authorisation OFF (recommended for dynamic IPs - the API key alone
      authenticates). Find the server IP by Googling "what is my ip" ON that machine.
   2. Confirm the sender bakareahmadio@gmail.com is a VERIFIED sender in Brevo
      (Senders, Domains & Dedicated IPs).
   3. Retest a message reply: the flash now tells the truth; app logs (Serilog file sink
      under src/DeChimex.Host/logs/dechimex-*.log) carry the exact Brevo error
      ("Brevo send failed (Unauthorized) ... unrecognised IP address ...").
   4. If the flash says "Reply emailed" but nothing arrives, check Brevo -> Email -> Logs
      for the send status (blocked/soft-bounce/spam).
  NOTE: /v3/smtp/email may ACCEPT (201) from IPs that other Brevo endpoints reject; a 201
  is not proof of delivery - the Brevo dashboard log is.

SECTION 65 - v047: ROOT CAUSE FOUND - BREVO DAILY QUOTA (0/300)
================================================================
OWNER CONFIRMED via dashboard: "0/300 emails left". API confirms:
GET /v3/account -> plan:[{"type":"free","credits":0,"creditsType":"sendLimit"}]

This explains EVERYTHING: Brevo ACCEPTS sends with HTTP 201 even at
quota 0, then silently holds them - no events, no delivery, no error.
That is why the app said "sent" and nothing arrived at
gddh009@yopmail.com, and why the events feed froze at 08:35 (the last
send before quota hit 0). The earlier authorised-IP 401 was a real
separate issue from certain sandbox IPs, but the quota is the cause
the owner is seeing.

FIX SHIPPED (commit 9944dae):
- BrevoEmailHealthCheck now parses plan[0] from /v3/account:
  creditsType=sendLimit && credits==0 -> red "Daily email limit
  reached (0 sends left today)" + explanation (300/day, resets
  midnight UTC) + link to brevo.com/pricing. Test-email button
  REFUSES to send while quota is 0 (a 2xx would be meaningless).
  credits>0 -> healthy card shows "N sends remaining today".
- Unit tests (7 new, BrevoEmailHealthCheckTests.cs, NSubstitute per
  project convention - NOT Moq): quota-0 status, no wasted test send,
  remaining-count, IP-401 message, network error, disabled config.
- LIVE PROOF: settings card on the running app shows the red quota
  banner reading live Brevo; test button reports the quota instead of
  sending. Suite 382/382 (198 unit + 184 integration).

OWNER NEXT STEPS:
1. Quota resets at midnight UTC (1am Nigeria). After that, reply to
   any message in Admin -> Messages -> the email WILL deliver.
   (Emails "sent" while quota was 0 are held/discarded by Brevo -
   re-send the reply after reset; do not expect it to arrive later.)
2. Or upgrade the Brevo plan (brevo.com/pricing) for more volume.
3. IMPORTANT for future testing: every live E2E test burns real
   daily quota (300/day total for the whole account). Keep live sends
   to a handful per session; integration tests use fakes.

67) CUSTOM ORDER FLOW / CATEGORY-DRIVEN ORDERING — IMPLEMENTED
    [2026-09-09]
============================================================
CONTEXT: owner approved the design in CUSTOM_ORDER_FLOW_PLAN.md (repo root) and
directed implementation. This section records what landed at this pass so a
future session can continue without re-reading the whole plan. Full build is
green and the complete test suite passes (198 unit + 188 integration = 386;
was 382/382 at §65; 4 new integration tests cover the custom flow).

WHAT THE FEATURE IS (one coherent lifecycle, no competing flow):
 - A Category now owns configurable "ordering options" (single choice, multiple
   choice, short text, long text, file upload, conditional visibility). Products
   inherit their CATEGORY's options — there is deliberately NO product-level
   option system. The prohibited "Category Options + Product Options" duplicate
   from the original bad spec does NOT exist.
 - Auto-routing CTA (Views/StoreProducts/Details.cshtml): a product whose
   category has active options OR whose IsCustomizable flag is set shows
   "Order / Customize" -> /products/{id}/order (order request + staff review).
   Plain products keep the unchanged cart -> checkout path.
 - Order workspace per request/order (customer + staff): structured details,
   option responses, files, real-time chat (SignalR), status, pricing, timeline,
   design proofs.
 - Design/proof stage: staff upload a versioned proof; customer approves or
   requests changes; the approved version is recorded (who + when).
 - Staff finalize requirements + pricing -> order enters PendingPayment and the
   EXISTING Paystack flow (server-side verify + webhook/idempotency) is reused
   unchanged. Invoice issued at finalize (before payment); receipt after payment.

KEY IMPLEMENTATION FACTS:
 - New statuses (additive, values 10+, stored as string so no data migration):
   AwaitingReview=10, InDiscussion=11, RequirementsConfirmed=12,
   AwaitingDesignApproval=13, QualityCheck=14, OutForDelivery=15.
   Customer-facing projection in DeChimex.Domain/Enums/OrderStatusPresentation.cs
   (kept in DOMAIN, not Shared, because Domain references Shared only).
 - New enums: CategoryOptionType, DesignProofStatus; new NotificationType
   members 8-15; new EmailTemplate members (OrderRequestReceived,
   OrderMessageNotification, OrderQuoteReady, DesignProofReady,
   DesignProofApproved).
 - New entities (all cascade-deleted with their owner):
   CategoryOption/CategoryOptionChoice (on Category),
   OrderRequirementResponse, OrderFile, OrderDesignProof, OrderMessage,
   OrderTimelineEntry, OrderCustomLine (on Order). Order.ItemSubtotal now
   includes custom lines; InvoiceLine.CreateCustom supports ad-hoc invoice lines
   so invoice line items reconcile with the grand total.
 - ONE new migration: AddCustomOrderFlow (20260909152531) — additive only,
   new tables only. has-pending-model-changes is clean. Never edit released
   migrations.
 - Application: SaveCategoryOptionsCommand, SubmitOrderRequestCommand,
   GetOrderWorkspaceQuery (IOrderWorkspaceQueries), SendOrderMessageCommand,
   UploadOrderFileCommand, DeleteOrderFileCommand, UploadDesignProofCommand,
   ApproveDesignProofCommand, RequestDesignChangesCommand, FinalizeOrderCommand,
   SetOrderRequirementsStatusCommand; UpdateOrderStatus extended with
   QualityCheck + OutForDelivery. Domain events + handlers mirror
   OrderEventHandlers (fire-and-forget email/notification).
 - Host: OrderChatHub at /hubs/orderchat (customer-owner or staff with
   orders.view/updatestatus can join order:{id}); IOrderChatPusher registered in
   Program.cs. Controllers: StoreProductsController (order form GET/POST),
   PortalOrdersController (workspace + message/files/approve/request-changes),
   AdminOrderWorkspaceController (staff workspace), AdminCategoriesController
   (options editor). Views: StoreProducts/OrderForm.cshtml,
   PortalOrders/Workspace.cshtml, AdminOrderWorkspace/Index.cshtml,
   AdminCategories/Options.cshtml. SignalR client in the workspace views with
   normal page-refresh fallback.
 - Permissions: NO new permission strings (per the plan §12) — orders.view for
   workspace read, orders.updatestatus for proof/finalize/transitions,
   categories.manage for the options editor. Customer actions are
   ownership-checked like CheckoutController.
 - Storage: two new FileCategory values (OrderAttachment = images+documents,
   DesignProof = images) handled by LocalFileStorageService.

NOTES / KNOWN BOUNDARIES:
 - "Order via WhatsApp" button on the product page is left untouched (owner did
   not ask to remove it).
 - Category-option edits rebuild the option set (delete + re-add); historical
   orders are unaffected because responses snapshot question/type/value.
 - Proof-upload only auto-moves the order to AwaitingDesignApproval from
   InDiscussion/RequirementsConfirmed; staff confirm requirements separately.
 - The finalize step is allowed from AwaitingReview/InDiscussion/
   RequirementsConfirmed/Draft (finalizing IS the confirmation).

68) QA E2E PASS ON THE CUSTOM ORDER FLOW — 2026-09-09
====================================================
The custom flow was exercised against the RUNNING app as a real QA engineer
(Playwright-driven browser, InMemory DB, mock Paystack on 127.0.0.1:9090).
Scripts live in /home/user/e2e/ (setup.py, phaseA_admin.py, phaseA2_options.py,
phaseB_customer.py, phaseC_chat.py, phaseD_finalize_proof.py,
phaseE_payment.py, phaseF_fulfilment.py, phaseG_regression.py); screenshots and
the email dev-outbox in /home/user/qa-artifacts/.

COVERED AND GREEN (clean run, 143 E2E checks passed):
 - Admin category create/edit + options editor config + persistence +
   validation (blank question rejected); product create/edit with NO
   product-level option fields; storefront auto-detect CTA.
 - Storefront journey; dynamic scenarios A (customize=Yes, options shown) and
   B (customize=No, conditional options hidden, submits without them).
 - Validation: missing/empty/invalid quantity, wrong file type (.exe) and
   oversize (>10 MB) rejected WITH previously valid input preserved.
 - Order-request persistence; customer + staff workspaces; order chat both
   directions (SignalR real-time + refresh fallback, away/back, long 3999-char
   message, empty message blocked, per-order isolation).
 - Additional file upload; staff finalize with a realistic quote
   (₦108,000 = 3×₦32,000 + ₦10,000 design-prep + ₦2,000 delivery).
 - Design/proof upload → approve AND request-changes; revision v2 supersedes
   v1 without overwriting (approved version recorded).
 - Paystack mock payment: success (server-verified, idempotent re-visit, no
   duplicate payment rows), cancel (order stays Payment Required, no
   corruption), retry-after-cancel succeeds. Gateway charged the exact
   customer payable (₦109,746.20 = 108,000 + 1,746.20 customer-borne charge).
 - Production status flow paid → AwaitingProcurement → InProduction →
   QualityCheck → Ready → OutForDelivery → Delivered (+ the admin "Completed"
   shortcut), each mirrored to the customer as the friendly label and written
   to the order timeline; track page renders for a completed order.
 - Notifications (PaymentReceived, ProcurementRequired, OrderCompleted) and
   email generation (order confirmation, status update, quote ready, receipt)
   verified via /admin/notifications and the dev outbox.
 - Authorization/IDOR: Customer B cannot open/post/upload on Customer A's
   order (all 404) and vice versa; staff can open both workspaces.
 - Refresh / back / forward on the workspace: no blank pages, no 4xx/5xx.
 - Regression: a PLAIN product (no category options, not customizable) keeps
   Add-to-cart → cart → checkout → place order → order history; the custom
   "Order / Customize" CTA does not appear on it.

BUGS FOUND BY THE E2E PASS AND FIXED (root cause → fix → verification):
 1. GET /checkout/return 500 after a successful payment. The invoice fallback
    in PaymentConfirmationService issued an invoice but never set its required
    ShareToken (EF InMemory: "Required properties '{ShareToken}' are missing").
    Fix: invoice.SetShareToken(references.NewShareToken()) in the fallback.
    Verified: success page renders, invoice + receipt links present.
 2. Customer UNDERCHARGED: Paystack was initialized for ₦99,593.91 instead of
    the finalized ₦108,000 + charge. OrderRepository.GetWithLinesAsync did not
    Include CustomLines, so GrandTotal silently dropped the custom line.
    Fix: added .Include(o => o.CustomLines). Verified: mock checkout shows
    ₦109,746.20; new integration test
    CustomOrderFlow_PaymentAmount_IncludesCustomLineAndCharge asserts the
    gateway amount.
 3. Staff could not reach QualityCheck or OutForDelivery from the UI (domain
    supported them but AllowedActionsFor()/the view omitted them). Fix: added
    StartQualityCheck/Dispatch actions + labels to AllowedActionsFor and
    AdminOrders/Details.cshtml. Verified: full status walk in E2E.
 4. Production status changes were missing from the order timeline (the plan
    requires payment + status entries). Fix: UpdateOrderStatusHandler and
    PaymentConfirmationService now write timeline entries. Verified: E2E
    counts 6 "Order status changed to …" entries on a delivered order.

NOT A BUG (harness note): customer login POST /Account/Login correctly
redirects to /portal; an earlier E2E "FAIL" was the harness reusing an already
authenticated session. The harness now clears cookies before login.

SUITE AT THIS PASS: 386/386 (198 unit + 188 integration) — see §67. E2E suite:
143/143 checks across the 9 scripts listed above. Out of scope / unchanged:
real hosted Paystack checkout (Cloudflare-blocked from the sandbox) and real
Brevo delivery (Brevo daily quota 0/300 at test time) — both exercised through
the mock gateway and the dev email outbox respectively.

ROOT CAUSES + FIXES (view-only; no controller/domain/contract change):
 1. AdminOrderWorkspace/Index.cshtml: each custom-line row (persisted
    + the blank row) now carries a remove button (type=button, class
    remove-line); an "+ Add another line" button (id=addCustomLine)
    appends new desc/price/qty rows via JS (delegation-free wiring).
    Removing a row drops that line from the posted set (SetCustomLines
    replaces the whole set — server semantics unchanged). New inputs
    carry maxlength=250 / min-max 1..10000 to mirror the finalize
    validator. Backend already supported everything — UI-only gap.
 2. DOUBLE ENCODING: both workspace views rendered chat bodies as
    @System.Net.WebUtility.HtmlEncode(m.Body) INSIDE a Razor
    expression — Razor encoded the already-encoded string again, so
    the browser displayed entities ("I&#39;m"). FIX: render @m.Body
    (Razor auto-encodes once). Fixed in BOTH AdminOrderWorkspace/
    Index.cshtml and PortalOrders/Workspace.cshtml. Also hardened the
    SignalR live-append JS in both views: the header (authorName) and
    body are now built with textContent instead of innerHTML string
    concatenation (author names are user-controlled text).
 3. WHITE-SCREEN ON NAVIGATION: both layouts (and the storefront,
    public and Account standalone pages) loaded Google Fonts with a
    RENDER-BLOCKING <head> stylesheet — a slow/unreachable fonts CDN
    holds FIRST PAINT hostage: full-white page that "returns" when
    the CDN answers or the request times out (most visible jumping
    into the portal workspace on a flaky connection). FIX: async
    font loading everywhere (preconnect x2 + media="print"
    onload="this.media='all'" + <noscript> fallback) — system-font
    fallback paints instantly, Inter swaps in when ready. Files:
    _AdminLayout.cshtml, _PortalLayout.cshtml, _Layout.cshtml,
    _PublicLayout.cshtml, Account/{Login,Register,VerifyEmail,
    ForgotPassword,ResetPassword,AccessDenied}.cshtml.
    NOTE: server-side workspace query was audited (single
    eager-loaded query, no N+1) — no server-side slowness found.

71) DOUBLE-ENCODING SWEEP: MARKETING PREVIEW + GALLERY
    LABELS — CHECKED, VERIFIED, FIXED  [2026-09-10]
============================================================
OWNER REQUEST: "check, verify and fix" — referring to the §70
DISCOVERED item (AdminMarketing srcdoc suspected double-encode).

CHECKED / VERIFIED (all confirmed REAL, same root family as the §70
chat bug — WebUtility.HtmlEncode/Html.Encode applied INSIDE a Razor
output or attribute, so Razor encodes a second time):
 1. AdminMarketing/Details.cshtml: srcdoc="@...HtmlEncode(Model.
    HtmlContent)" — the broadcast preview iframe received the markup
    entity-escaped TWICE, so staff saw literal "&lt;h2&gt;..." soup
    instead of the rendered email. Content is sanitized on save
    (MarketingHtmlSanitizer strips script/iframe/event-handlers/
    javascript: URLs) and the iframe carries bare "sandbox" (no
    scripts, no same-origin), so rendering the RAW value is correct
    AND safe: Razor attribute-encodes exactly once for transport.
 2. AdminGallery/Index.cshtml: aria-label="Open @Html.Encode(m.Title)"
    — double-encoded; screen readers read entity soup for titles with
    ' " &.
 3. StoreGallery/Index.cshtml: same pattern with g.Title.
FIXES: all three now render the raw value (@Model.HtmlContent /
@m.Title / @g.Title). Compose.cshtml's JS-side srcdoc= assignment
was audited — property assignment, not markup — correct as-is.
NO other srcdoc/Html.Encode-in-Razor instances remain (swept).

TESTS (+2, 392 total passing):
 - MarketingBroadcastTests.BroadcastPreview_RendersSanitizedHtml
   WithoutDoubleEncoding: compose (with script + javascript: URL),
   open Details, assert srcdoc single-encoded form AND sandbox AND
   stripped script; asserts NOT &amp;lt;/&amp;#x27;/&amp;quot;.
 - MvcAdminTests.Gallery_ItemTitles_RenderSingleEncodedInAriaLabels:
   seeds a media item titled Owner's "Bold" & Bright Reel; asserts
   the EXACT single-encoded aria-label on /admin/gallery AND public
   /gallery. LESSON (cost 2 debug cycles): Razor's HtmlEncoder emits
   HEX entities (' -> &#x27;, + -> &#x2B;) not decimal (&#39;), and a
   page-wide NotContain("&amp;quot;") false-positives on the pages'
   own inline-JS attribute-escaped quotes — scope double-encode
   assertions to the affected substring, and remember &amp; inside a
   label is the CORRECT encoding of a literal &.

LIVE E2E (InMemory boot): composed "Mid-Year Encoding Check" with
<hi-tags>, 'smart quotes', &amp;/&ndash; entities, <script>alert +
javascript: URL -> Details serves srcdoc="&lt;h2&gt;It&#x27;s..."
(tags decode as tags), script STRIPPED, javascript: neutralized to
href="#", sandbox present, zero &amp;lt;. Gallery fix covered by the
integration test (real server instance).
