============================================================
72) PRODUCT EDIT RELIABILITY: PRICE EDITS NOW ALWAYS
    PERSIST — SLUG COLLISION ROOT CAUSE FIXED  [2026-09-10]
============================================================
OWNER REQUEST: "The edited product price is not taking effect after
saving. Investigate the root cause across the full edit flow, from
the MVC form/model binding through validation, controller/service
logic, repository/database update, and the subsequent product
display. Fix so the submitted price is persisted and reflected
everywhere. Treat as a broader reliability issue: ALL valid product
edits must persist. No unrelated changes."

INVESTIGATION (every layer audited + proven):
 - MVC form/view (_ProductForm.cshtml): single form, single
   asp-for="SellingPrice" input (type=number), hidden Id/Sku — no
   JS touches the price; binding target ProductFormViewModel.SellingPrice
   decimal, [Range(0.01,..)] — verified live: POST binds correctly.
 - Controller (AdminProductsController.Edit POST): image rule,
   UpdateProductCommand(model fields), then optional image uploads,
   PRG to Details which RE-QUERIES the DB (no display cache).
 - Validation: UpdateProductValidator SellingPrice > 0 — verified.
 - Handler/domain/repo/UoW (tracked entity, diff-based SaveChanges,
   audit interceptor benign, owned Money type standard): verified —
   and PROVEN live on InMemory: price 1000 -> 2500 persisted, shown
   on Details, edit form after fresh load, admin list, storefront.
 - No caching anywhere on the product read path (only Category/Service
   lists cache; ResponseCaching middleware only sets static-file
   headers; no MediatR cache behavior).
 => The happy path was never broken in code; the FAILURE MODE is
    provider-specific and data-dependent:

ROOT CAUSE: Product.Update (and Create) REGENERATED the slug from
the name on EVERY save with NO uniqueness resolution, against the
UNIQUE FILTERED INDEX on Products.Slug ([IsDeleted] = 0) that SQL
SERVER (the owner's DB) enforces but the INMEMORY test provider
SILENTLY IGNORES. Any rename onto another product's slug (near-
identical names produce identical slugs — Slug.FromText strips all
non-alphanumerics) aborted the WHOLE SaveChanges in an unhandled
DbUpdateException -> 500 -> the submitted price (and every other
field) was NOT persisted; nothing caught or resolved it. Same hole
on CREATE with a duplicate name. This is exactly the "valid edits
are not reliably persisted" class, invisible to every InMemory
integration test (390/390 green while SQL fails).

FIX (product edit flow only, per scope):
 1. Domain: Product.Create/Update take an optional pre-resolved
    Slug; Update now KEEPS the existing slug when the name is
    unchanged (a price/detail edit never churns the URL) and only
    re-derives on a real name change.
 2. IProductRepository.SlugExistsAsync (+ Persistence impl matching
    the filtered index semantics via the !IsDeleted query filter).
 3. UpdateProductHandler: unchanged name -> keep slug; changed name
    -> resolve collisions up front (-2, -3, ... suffix, mirroring
    the existing SKU resolver) BEFORE saving.
 4. CreateProductHandler: same resolution so duplicate-name creates
    succeed with unique slugs instead of 500ing on SQL.
 5. Belt-and-braces in AdminProductsController (Create + Edit POST):
    catch DbUpdateException -> redisplay the form with the submitted
    values and a clear message — a DB-rejected save can never again
    be silently dropped without the user seeing why.

TESTS (+3 integration, +3 unit; suite now 396/396 = 201 + 195):
 - EditProduct_WithChangedPrice_PersistsAndSurvivesReload: the
   missing persistence assertion — redirect shows new price, FRESH
   edit form shows value="3456.78", details again, plus DB-row
   assertions for price/description.
 - EditProduct_RenamingOntoAnotherProductsSlug_PersistsWithUnique
   Slug: rename onto a taken slug + price change persists; both
   products keep distinct slugs; rival untouched.
 - CreateProduct_WithDuplicateName_SucceedsWithUniqueSlug.
 - Unit: Update same-name keeps slug; Update/Create use provided
   resolved slug.
 - RED/GREEN PROVEN: stashing only the src fix and re-running the
   two collision tests FAILS pre-fix exactly as predicted (duplicate
   "duplicate-name-test"/"big-deal" slugs written unchecked; on SQL
   these are the 500s that ate the edits). Pop -> green.

LIVE E2E (running app): created "Price Edit Target" @₦1,000 +
"Colliding Slug" @₦77; price edit 1000->2500 verified on Details
after redirect, edit form after FRESH LOAD (value="2500"), details
after second refresh, admin list (₦2,500.00) and the PUBLIC product
page (&#8358;2,500.00); rename onto "Colliding Slug" + price
5750.25 saved cleanly (no 500), rival still ₦77.00; third same-
named product created successfully — 3 same-named products coexist
with distinct slugs/prices on the list.

NOT CHANGED (flagged, out of scope): Service.cs has the same
regenerate-slug-on-update pattern against its own unique index —
same latent SQL-only failure class; fix in a dedicated pass if the
owner reports service edits.

STANDING INSTRUCTION COMPLIANCE: appsettings keys re-verified;
deliverable zip rebuilt via tools/make-deliverable-zip.sh after the
commit. NOTE (recurring): this workspace's snapshot restore again
rolled HEAD back before this task (§70/§71 commits re-created as
c480a2d at the start of this pass); keep exports of the zip as the
durable artifact.

§74 2026-09-10 TASK 6 — PRICING ROOT-CAUSE FIX (₦0.00 PER UNIT)
================================================================================
SYMPTOM: products showed ₦0.00 per unit in the customer portal Order Summary
and admin Finalization despite valid catalog prices.

ROOT CAUSE (single defect): SubmitOrderRequestHandler
(src/DeChimex.Application/Features/Orders/Commands/CustomOrderCommands.cs)
called order.AddProduct(..., 0m, r.Quantity) with a HARDCODED 0 placeholder
("staff set real pricing during finalization"). Every surface that renders
the STORED line price therefore showed zero: customer workspace Order Summary
+ /portal/orders/{id} details, admin finalization prefill
(value="@i.UnitPrice"), order GrandTotal (qty × 0), any invoice built from
the order pre-finalize, and the Paystack payable (order.GrandTotal).
FIX: snapshot product.SellingPrice.Amount onto the line at submission —
identical to the cart path (CreateOrderHandler). Staff override at
finalization is preserved (UpdateProductLine / FinalizeOrderCommand) —
proven by test (override 9,999 accepted; GrandTotal 2×9,999 + 5,000 + 1,500
= 26,498).

FULL PRICING TRACE (audited, no other instances):
- Product display (storefront + portal shop): live SellingPrice ✓
- Cart display: live catalog read (OperationsQueries.GetCart) ✓ (0-fallback
  only for deleted products, unreachable at checkout)
- Cart→checkout→order: CreateOrderHandler snapshots SellingPrice ✓
- Custom order submit: FIXED (was the 0m)
- Finalization: staff-submitted prices, fallback = stored line price ✓
- Invoice.Issue: builds lines from order items + totals ✓ (inherits fix)
- Quote invoices (CreateQuoteInvoice): snapshot SellingPrice per line ✓
- Payment init/confirm/mark-paid: order.GrandTotal-driven ✓ (guard: only
  PendingPayment/Draft can initialize, so custom orders pay post-finalize)
- Wishlist: live SellingPrice ✓. Admin order builder: server-side pricing,
  no client price inputs ✓. Track page: order totals ✓.
- REVIEWED, NOT CHANGED (intentional per code comment, flagged): quote→order
  conversion (CreateOrderCommand.QuoteInvoiceId) re-prices lines from the
  CURRENT catalog, not the quote's line prices. Quote lines can only ever be
  catalog snapshots (no quote price editing exists), so divergence is only
  possible if the catalog is repriced between quote and conversion — current
  behavior is documented intent ("snapshot from the current catalog"). If the
  owner wants quote-price lock-in, do it as a dedicated pass.

TESTS: NEW tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/OrderPricingTests.cs
(3; RED→GREEN proven — the snapshot test failed with UnitPrice 0 pre-fix):
1. CustomOrder_Submission_SnapshotsCatalogUnitPriceAndTotals — ₦2,750.50 × 3:
   item price + line total + ItemsSubtotal + GrandTotal = 8,251.50; customer
   workspace renders "2,750.50"/"8,251.50"; admin finalize prefill contains
   the price. 2. CustomOrder_Finalization_StaffOverrideStillSetsLinePrice.
   3. CartCheckout_MultipleProductsAndQuantities_SnapshotCorrectPrices
   (2×₦1,200 + 1×₦4,500.25, subtotal 6,900.25, total identity check).
Suite: 406/406 (201 unit + 205 integration).

LIVE E2E (Release/InMemory boot): products "Pricing Mug" ₦1,200 +
"Pricing Banner" ₦4,500 (customizable) — public pages show correct prices;
owner walk-in order 2×mug + 1×banner → admin order details, admin invoice
AND public share invoice (/i/{token}) all show ₦1,200.00 / ₦2,400.00 /
₦4,500.00 / ₦6,900.00. AUTHENTICATED-CUSTOMER LEG: registration works but
live verification is again blocked by the EXTERNAL Brevo delivery/indexing
outage (app log "Email … sent", Brevo list empty; mail.tm/GuerrillaMail
also undelivered earlier today) — same environment issue as §73; the
customer path is covered by the in-pipeline tests (register→verify→login→
submit→workspace→finalize and cart→checkout). No app defect; do not chase.

NOT CHANGED: order lifecycle, checkout internals, cart storage, Paystack

   unterminated character class because the passed prefix ends with '['.

   RegExp. Product AND service cloners verified producing [0..n] indexes.

78) SALES ASSIGNMENT & ORDER CONVERSATION WORKFLOW — IMPLEMENTED 2026-09-11
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Request: per-order Sales ownership on top of the v047 (§68) orderflow
architecture. Order stays the single lifecycle aggregate (no OrderRequest; no
chat/payment/invoice/inventory/permission/timeline/cart changes).

DESIGN (as built):
- Order += AssignedSalesUserId (Guid?), AssignedSalesName (string?),
  ConversationState (OrderConversationState: Active/WaitingForCustomer/
  WaitingForStaff/Resolved). Purely additive migration
  20260911012805_AddSalesAssignment (3 columns + index on AssignedSalesUserId).
- NotificationType += SalesAssigned, SalesReassigned. OrderEvents +=
  SalesAssignedEvent (IsReassignment flag).
- Auto-assign on SubmitCustomOrder: ISalesAssignmentService.PickForNewOrderAsync
  over eligible staff (active, non-deleted, SalesStaff role) ordered by lowest
  ACTIVE workload, tie-break earliest-created account then email
  (SalesAssignmentPolicy.Pick — pure, unit-tested). Active sales work =
  assigned orders in AwaitingReview/InDiscussion/RequirementsConfirmed/
  AwaitingDesignApproval (Order.IsActiveSalesWork); Paid+ history never counts.
  No eligible staff -> order stays unassigned, visible in the triage bucket.
- Conversation flips: customer message -> WaitingForStaff, staff message ->
  WaitingForCustomer (no-op once Resolved); completion/cancel/refund resolve
  the conversation but keep chat+timeline history. OrderStatus untouched.
- Manual reassign: ReassignSalesCommand (users.assignroles permission;
  ineligible target -> ForbiddenException -> TempData error; same-person =
  idempotent no-op), timeline entry keeps names on STAFF views, notifies the
  new owner via SalesAssignedEventHandler.
- Sales inbox: AdminSalesController /admin/sales (orders.view), buckets new/
  active/waiting-customer/waiting-staff/resolved/unassigned; Owner/Manager see
  all + staff filter; SalesStaff forced to own queue.
- CUSTOMER PRIVACY (defect caught by E2E screenshot review, fixed same pass):
  the customer timeline rendered "Automatically assigned to sales (NAME)".
  PortalOrdersController.Workspace now returns order.RedactForCustomer()

  HasAssignedSales. Staff projections untouched. Covered by unit tests
  (OrderWorkspaceRedactionTests) + integration test
  CustomerWorkspace_NeverLeaksSalesStaffNames. NOTE: staff chat messages still
  show the staff EMAIL as author — pre-existing §68 behavior, left intact.
- SignalR OrderChatHub authorization unchanged; verify via existing tests.

79) CSP form-action GATEWAY ALLOWLIST — FIXED 2026-09-11 (owner "do it")
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Follow-up to the §78 finding. Host/Middleware/SecurityHeadersMiddleware.cs now
emits `form-action 'self' https://checkout.paystack.com` (one-line change +
comment; consistent with the existing frame-src entry for the same host).
Effect: Chrome/Edge follow the hosted-checkout 302 from POST
/checkout/{id}/pay to real Paystack authorization URLs instead of blocking it
as a CSP violation. No other directive touched; policy stays deny-by-default
otherwise (object-src 'none', base-uri 'self', etc.).
Verification: new SecurityHeadersTests.Csp_AllowsSelf_AndPaystackCheckout-
FormAction asserts the exact directive through the REAL middleware pipeline
(WebApplicationFactory). Full suite 452/452 green (236 unit + 216
integration). Sandbox E2E keeps its GET-navigation step for the MOCK gateway
(http://127.0.0.1:9090 stays cross-origin by design and is NOT allowlisted —
correctly so; only the production gateway host is trusted).
Committed with §78 work; zip rebuilt from HEAD (same filename).

80) STAFF CREATION 500 (ValidationAppException) — FIXED 2026-09-11
--------------------------------------------------------------------------------
Owner report: creating a staff user (SalesStaff selected) crashed with an
unhandled DeChimex.Application.Common.Exceptions.ValidationAppException from
ValidationBehavior (VS break + HTTP 500).
ROOT CAUSE: two validation layers disagree and the controller only handled
one. The form annotations (StaffUserFormViewModel) require password min-
length 8 and no role; the command validator (CreateStaffUserValidator)
additionally requires password complexity (upper+lower+digit) and a
non-empty RoleIds. Input passing MVC but failing the command (weak password
and/or no role actually selected) threw out of AdminUsersController.Create,
which caught only ConflictException. (The browser screenshot's
"Last name is required." beside a filled field was a stale server-rendered
message from an earlier submit, not the crash itself.)
FIX (3 files, follows the AccountController mapping pattern):
- AdminUsersController.Create POST: catch ValidationAppException, strip the
  "Request." prefix from error keys, add to ModelState, repopulate roles,
  re-render the form.
- CreateStaffUserValidator: friendly WithMessages copied from Register.cs
  ("Password must contain an uppercase/digit...") + "Select at least one
  role." for RoleIds.
- Views/AdminUsers/Create.cshtml: added the missing RoleIds validation span
  (+ required marker on the Roles label) so the role error is visible.
VERIFICATION: 3 new StaffCreationTests (weak password re-renders with the
password message; missing role re-renders with the role message; valid
SalesStaff create redirects + persists user with role). Full suite 455/455
(236 unit + 219 integration). Sandbox UI E2E: all checks passed — weak
password, missing role, valid create, persistence after reload, new staff
login + sales inbox access, duplicate-email edge. Evidence (final run only):
DeChimexPrints/TestArtifacts/StaffCreationFix/01..07.
NOTE: asp-validation-for shows the FIRST error per field only — by design;
tests assert accordingly (e.g. "password" shows the uppercase message).

81) STAFF USERNAMES — CUSTOMER-FACING CHAT IDENTITY — IMPLEMENTED 2026-09-11
================================================================================
REQUIREMENT: every staff account has a unique username; inside
customer-facing chat the username is the ONLY staff identity shown (no
staff name/email/id anywhere: bubbles, quotes, typing, presence,
receipts, notifications, payloads). Internal/admin views keep full info.

IMPLEMENTATION (username is display identity only — login still by email;
no auth-architecture change):
- Domain: new Username value object (lower-case normalised; 3–30 chars;
  [a-z0-9._-], must start/end alphanumerically; reserved: admin,
  administrator, staff, customer, system, support, dechimex) +
  Username.SuggestFromEmail for backfill/seeds. User.Username (null for
  customers) + StaffDisplayName (username ?? "Staff" — never name/email) +
  SetUsername. User.CreateStaff now REQUIRES a username.
  OrderMessage.RenameAuthor for rename propagation/backfill (quotes resolve
  the parent live, so they follow automatically).
- Send path: SendOrderMessageHandler labels staff messages with the
  author's username (resolved via IUserRepository; "Staff" fallback). The
  stored snapshot flows unchanged into bubbles, quotes, reply bar, state
  JSON, send JSON, hub orderMessage pushes and the OrderMessageAddedEvent,
  so notifications ("{username} replied on {order}") follow automatically.
- Typing: OrderChatHub.Typing now sends `name` (staff username /
  customer full name); workspace-chat.js renders "{name} is typing…"
  (role label kept as fallback). Presence/read/delivered events were
  already identity-free (roles/ids only) — untouched.
- RedactForCustomer: timeline ActorName now stripped for ALL entry types
  (customer views render descriptions only).
- Staff management (existing patterns): Username field on the admin create
  form (validator mirrors the domain type, case-insensitive); new
  admin/users/{id}/username GET+POST page (UsernameFormViewModel +
  Username.cshtml, Users.Create policy, same Conflict/ValidationApp mapping
  as Create); Username column + search in the users list (UserDto + queries
  extended); SetUsernameCommand (rejects customer accounts, duplicate check
  excluding self, propagates to the author's staff messages).
- Uniqueness: application check (UsernameExistsAsync) + filtered unique DB
  index ([Username] IS NOT NULL AND [IsDeleted] = 0); migration
  20260911115602_StaffUsernames.
- Existing data: idempotent startup backfill
  (DatabaseSeeder.SeedStaffUsernamesAsync) — staff without usernames get
  deterministic email-derived names (de-duplicated with numeric suffixes,
  never reserved/confusing); historical staff messages (previously labelled
  with the staff EMAIL) relabelled to the author's username, unknown
  authors to "Staff". Owner seed gets a username too.
- Deactivated staff: username + history intact; snapshots keep rendering.

SURFACES AUDITED (all customer payloads/views/events): message bubbles,
reply quotes + reply bar, conversation header/count, typing, presence,
ticks/receipts, state feed, send JSON, hub pushes, bell dropdown + push
DTOs, email template (no name — unchanged), timeline, assigned-sales

Staff-side chat also renders the username (shared partial; username is
authorised internal info per §3). Customers unaffected (Username null).

82) DESIGN-PROOF EVALUATION + REFINEMENT — IMPLEMENTED 2026-09-11
================================================================================
VERDICT: the existing implementation already satisfied most of the brief
(versioned proofs, supersede rules, ownership checks, timeline, live pushes,
proof-ready customer notification, collapsible history, chat/proof separation).
Audit found 5 genuine gaps, all fixed with the smallest possible changes
(no rebuild, no new lifecycles, Order stays source of truth):
1. Approve() WIPED CustomerFeedback — approving a previously-rejected version
   destroyed the recorded change reason. Fix: feedback is kept (audit trail).
2. RequestProofChanges raised NO domain event — staff got no notification for
   a change request (the plan §8.10 ALREADY specified "staff notification";
   code never implemented it). Fix: new DesignProofChangesRequestedEvent +
   NotificationType.DesignProofChangesRequested=19 (string-stored, no
   migration) + handler beeping Orders.View staff with version + reason
   excerpt. Every request (incl. repeat feedback updates) notifies.
3. DesignProofApprovedEvent fired ONLY when status was AwaitingDesignApproval
   — approvals from other stages (e.g. after a change request moved the order
   to InDiscussion) never notified staff. Fix: event always raised; only the
   AwaitingDesignApproval->RequirementsConfirmed hop stays conditional.
4. ApproveProof/RequestProofChanges had NO mutability guard — decisions could
   land after payment. Fix: EnsureMutable() in both (domain's own Paid+
   immutability rule). Customer UI fails gracefully via existing TempData
   error mapping; staff upload-on-completed returns the existing 422 page.
5. Proof cards showed NO submitted/decision timestamps (data existed in the
   DTO). Fix: "Submitted …" + "Approved …" lines in both workspace views +
   live JS render (orderProofStatus payload gained approvedAtUtc; proofNode +
   syncProofStatus insert the lines; fmtDate reused).
Deliberately NOT changed: no submitter column (timeline actor is the record;
customer DTOs must never carry staff identity per the redaction rule); no
chat auto-message on proof events (proofs follow the files pattern: separate
card + timeline + notification); no view-gating of decision buttons (single
source of truth stays EnsureMutable; post-payment edge errors clearly);
finalize still doesn't require approval (existing lifecycle preserved).
VERIFICATION: 7 new domain unit tests (feedback preserved, both events incl.
payloads, Paid+ guards, supersede/history rules, superseded-approve throws) +
2 new integration tests (change-request notifies staff w/ reason + full
v1-rejected/v2-approved history over HTTP; approve-after-change-request still
notifies + keeps feedback) + untouched realtime tests still green. Full suite
540/540 (303 unit + 237 integration), 0 failures.
Sandbox E2E ws_proof_e2e.py (fresh TAG per run; committed under e2e/design-proof/
with brevo_stub.py + fixtures.py + README): 44/44 GREEN (final TAG=final1) —
register/verify via stub-captured OTP, order request, confirm, v1 upload,
change request, staff bell, v2 upload, chat photo+video (proof card stays
exactly v1+v2), live approval toast on staff side, approve v2, second-customer
404, finalize, mark-paid, production, ready, completed; full proof/decision/
chat/timeline history on BOTH workspaces after completion + reload. Evidence
(final run only, full-page): e2e-evidence/design-proof/01..08.
E2E DEBUGGING NOTES: (1) edit_file dropped 3/5 JS edits + 4/6 script inserts
despite "success" — grep-verify EVERY edit immediately, singly; (2) parallel
read_file image results display in unreliable order — verify evidence images
SERIALLY; (3) inner_text skips closed <details> — expand proof/timeline nodes
before text assertions AND screenshots; (4) .NET posts chunked (no
Content-Length) + Expect: 100-continue — the Brevo stub must send 100 then
decode chunks; (5) screenshot() captures CURRENT[0] — audit the use() chain
or shots mislabel pages; (6) order-submit redirects to the LIST or the
WORKSPACE depending on state — handle both; (7) transition forms use
per-action BUTTONS (button[value=confirm]), not selects; (8) order-form
submit must be form-scoped (page-level hits layout sign-out — §81 note stands).

C) BEHAVIOURAL BUGS FOUND BY THE SUITE/E2E AND FIXED (root causes):
 1. OrderDesignReviewDto had NO Url, so previews could not link to the
    original file and non-previewable uploads (PSD/AI/ZIP) had NO download at
    all -> added Url to the DTO + query projection + command projection

    canonical proof-handler pattern (customers.GetByUserIdAsync(...)?.Id !=

84) MIGRATION-SET REPAIR: PendingModelChangesWarning + DUPLICATE OrderDesignReviews
    [2026-09-21, against v047-DesignReview-Fixed-v3 @ 8a33f33]
================================================================================

    null) + Payment.RefundedAtUtc (datetime2, null) — Payment.cs has them and

   datetime2 null; Down() = the two DropColumns. NOTHING design-review — that
   schema is fully covered by 20260918100000.

85) SQL-SERVER QUERY-TRANSLATION FIX: product-create crash (SlugExistsAsync)
    [2026-09-21, against v047-DesignReview-Fixed-v3 @ 322a151-restored]
================================================================================
CONTEXT: owner ran the app against their local SQL Server (post-§84 migrations)
and hit an unhandled exception on EVERY product create (Visual Studio debugger
break, BusinessRepositories.cs:36):
  System.InvalidOperationException: The LINQ expression
  'DbSet<Product>().Where(p => !(p.IsDeleted)).Any(p => p.Slug.Value == __slug_0)'
  could not be translated...

ROOT CAUSE: Product.Slug is a value object mapped with
HasConversion(v => v.Value, v => Slug.FromText(v)) (CatalogConfigurations.cs).
EF Core 9 CAN translate member access on a converted VO inside PROJECTIONS
(Select(c => c.Slug.Value) -> SELECT [c].[Slug]) but NOT inside PREDICATES
(Where/Any comparisons) — the .Value comparison in the SlugExistsAsync
predicate throws at translation time, before any SQL is sent. InMemory (all
tests, all prior E2E) never translates LINQ, so this shipped green: THIRD
InMemory blind spot after §84's missing-migration and stale-snapshot defects.

FIX (one method, BusinessRepositories.cs ProductRepository.SlugExistsAsync):
  OLD: Set.AnyAsync(p => p.Slug.Value == slug && (excludeId == null || p.Id != excludeId), ct)
  NEW: var normalized = Slug.FromText(slug);
       Set.AnyAsync(p => p.Slug == normalized && (excludeId == null || p.Id != excludeId), ct)
Comparing the VO itself lets EF apply the converter to the parameter ->
WHERE [p].[Slug] = @__normalized_0. Semantics preserved: callers
(CreateProduct/UpdateProduct handlers) always pass Slug.FromText outputs
(idempotent under re-normalization) and ValueObject defines structural ==/!=
operators, so InMemory behavior is unchanged. A translation-note comment now
sits on the method.

VERIFICATION (design-time; sandbox has no SQL Server — stated honestly):
 a) Offline probe harness (/tmp/transprobe, not shipped): instantiates the REAL
    repositories/query classes against UseSqlServer(fake connection
    127.0.0.1:1). Translation happens BEFORE connection, so:
    InvalidOperationException "could not be translated" = bug;
    SqlException/network = translation OK. Before fix: 73 probes, exactly ONE
    failure (SlugExistsAsync — the owner's crash reproduced). After fix: 0
    failures (1 kept negative-control variant proves probe sensitivity).
    Sweep covered EVERY Persistence query class (Catalog, Operations, Sales,
    Dashboard, User, Invoice, Contact, CustomOrder, Marketing, Gallery,
    Procurement, Cart, Wishlist, Notification, SalesInbox) + repositories.
    Enum ToString() usages and owned-type Email/Phone/Username .Value accesses
    all translate — no other latent defects found.
 b) PERMANENT GUARD: tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/SqlServerQueryTranslationTests.cs
    (11 facts, ~70 probes, same fake-connection technique, no DB needed).
    Runs as part of the normal suite from now on. Add a probe whenever a new
    query class or non-trivial LINQ shape is added to Persistence.
 c) Full suite: 574/574 (318 unit + 245 existing integration + 11 new).
 d) Live browser E2E on a fresh boot: ALL 35 CHECKS PASSED (TAG=transfix);
    fixtures create the category + product through the admin UI, so the fixed
    code path runs live. evidence/ 01-16 refreshed to that run.
Evidence for THIS request: evidence-sql-translation/ 01-09 (ordered: owner
crash -> root cause -> harness -> before-sweep -> fix -> after-sweep -> guard
-> suite -> live E2E).

INCIDENT — .git LOST between sessions: the workspace snapshot/restore dropped
the whole .git directory (and also 17 wwwroot/img binaries, recovered). Repo
rebuilt: commit 322a151 = exact tree of the delivered MigrationFix zip
(== old 537bef3), minus the accidentally-committed
e2e/design-review/__pycache__/*.pyc junk (now ignored via new ROOT .gitignore:
__pycache__/, *.pyc, .pytest_cache/, /e2e-dr-wip/). Old history acb7ce3 ->
8a33f33 -> 537bef3 is documented here + in §83/§84. IF .git IS MISSING AGAIN
AT SESSION START: restore from the LATEST delivered zip (unzip over the tree,
git init, commit), check wwwroot/img count == 17, and harden the zip junk
check to also reject __pycache__/.pyc.

OWNER INSTRUCTIONS: replace the project from the new zip (no DB changes —
migrations untouched by §85), rebuild, product create works on SQL Server.
The 11 translation tests also run on their machine inside the normal suite.

CHECKLIST ADDITIONS (standing, every Persistence change): (1) §84's
has-pending-model-changes == false + empty probe scaffold; (2) §85's
SqlServerQueryTranslationTests green — add probes for any new query class;
(3) zip junk check now also rejects __pycache__/.pyc entries.

87) MAJOR CATEGORY DISCOVERY HIERARCHY [2026-09-22; owner approved Option A]
================================================================================
AUDIT FIRST: inspected Category/Product aggregates, category options, CQRS
commands/queries, category/product admin forms, both shopping surfaces,
shared navigation, migrations/constraints and relevant tests. Presented
major-category-architecture-audit.md; owner explicitly selected "Approve
Option A (recommended)" through the architecture decision prompt BEFORE code.
The audit is shipped in docs/MAJOR_CATEGORY_ARCHITECTURE_AUDIT.md.

DECISION: separate MajorCategory -> existing Category -> Product. Existing
Category is a product-type aggregate that OWNS ordering questions. A tree
would mix navigation-only roots with product-bearing leaves and require
root/leaf guards across existing workflows. A string/settings map would
lack relational integrity and stable identity. Exactly two levels requested;
no generic hierarchy, no extra product-to-major FK.

IMPLEMENTATION:
- MajorCategory aggregate: name/normalized unique name, optional description,
  automatic DisplayOrder, standard audit + soft deletion. Max 120/1000 chars.
- Nullable indexed Category.MajorCategoryId + optional navigation, restricted
  FK. Existing products retain unchanged CategoryId; no data rewriting.
- Additive migration 20260922025355_AddMajorCategories (migration pair +
  regenerated snapshot). Up creates one table, nullable column, FK + indexes;
  no destructive operations. SQL at database/20260922-MajorCategories-Upgrade.sql
  assumes prior AddPaymentRefundColumns baseline; prefer standard EF update.
- Major query/repository, CQRS save/delete/list handlers and validation; major
  names case-normalized unique among non-deleted rows. Database unique index
  handles concurrent duplicate writes; category/major SQL unique violations
  map to a friendly ConflictException in UnitOfWork (entity-scoped catch).
- AdminMajorCategories CRUD/detail MVC with existing categories.view/manage
  permissions and antiforgery. View-only staff can browse, not mutate.
- Existing category form is the sole assignment/move/unassign editor. Existing
  category list has major column/filter + Unassigned only. Major detail links
  to existing category and options editors; no duplicate subcategory CRUD.
- CategoryDtos append optional major fields. UpdateCategoryRequest also adds
  UpdateMajorCategory=false so OLD callers cannot accidentally clear membership.
  Admin form sets it true. Handler repository dependencies are required via DI.
- Product create/edit still submits only CategoryId; labels show Major -> Leaf.
- Shared ProductFilter.MajorCategoryId is intersected with CategoryId on the
  existing joined category; it also verifies a non-deleted major exists.
  Query remains composable with search, price bounds, featured and active-only.
- Both /products and /portal/shop show grouped mobile-accessible category
  navigation via _CatalogCategoryNavigation. Unassigned leaves remain under
  Other categories. Shared public footer also links majors. Existing leaf
  URLs stay valid. GUID query-string major URLs survive renames.
- Mismatched pair = empty results + clear error; normal group selection clears
  incompatible leaf by linking with major only. Invalid GUID syntax returns
  BadRequest, unknown/deleted major returns zero products, never all products.
  Search/sort/pagination retain major/category state.
- Audit found existing price sort happened AFTER paging. Both MVC list actions
  now use existing query SortBy/SortDescending BEFORE paging; price ties use Id.
  Default featured/newest ordering remains unchanged.
- Automatic ordering follows existing category convention: new majors append,
  edits retain order. Category moves retain existing DisplayOrder and options.
- Block major deletion with ANY non-deleted assigned leaf, including inactive.
  Existing leaf deletion with products still blocked. Invalid/deleted parents
  rejected. Navigation/category cache invalidated on move, rename and deletion.
- Existing category GLOBAL name/slug uniqueness preserved; this is stricter
  than within-parent uniqueness. Do not permit duplicate leaves across majors.

VERIFICATION, FINAL CODE:
- Full suite 589/589 = 318 unit + 271 integration; none failed/skipped.
- New MajorCategoryTests: 12 tests cover required/length boundaries, name-only
  + description, duplicate create/edit/case/whitespace, order preservation,
  cache invalidation, legacy update compatibility, product/options retention,
  empty/in-use/inactive-parent deletion, invalid/deleted parents, unassigned,
  combined/mismatched/unknown filters, price/search/featured/inactive filters,
  25-product pagination, real MVC sort, authorization (customer/anonymous/
  view-only staff), antiforgery and invalid filter syntax.
- New MajorCategoryRelationalTests: 2 tests. Real SQLite file database using
  production catalog mappings verifies unique index, FK rejection, physical
  restricted delete, and products/membership surviving connection reopen after
  a move; additive migration operations are inspected. Focused harness omits
  ordering option tables; it is NOT a live SQL Server migration test.
- SQL Server query translation guards: added major query/repository/membership

  expected unavailable-DB condition, not arbitrary exceptions. All pass.
- EF has-pending-model-changes: no changes. Generated probe scaffold: EMPTY

- Connected live design-review regression on fresh app: 36/36 (TAG=majorreg).

2026-09-23 — ACTIVITY TIMESTAMP "1 HOURS AGO" SKEW — ROOT-CAUSED / FIXED
======================================================================
Owner report: "the time in business activity timestamp says '1 hours' ago
across all activity? fix" (both the +1h skew and the "1 hours" plural bug).

ROOT CAUSE (two stacked defects + one masked-by-InMemory blind spot):
 1. SQL Server (and SQLite) materialise DateTime columns as Kind=Unspecified.
    _Rows.cshtml emitted @TimestampUtc.ToString("O") — for Unspecified that
    is an ISO string WITHOUT a zone suffix ("2026-09-23T12:46:51.1234567").
 2. JS Date.parse treats an offset-less ISO string as LOCAL time. In the
    owner's UTC+1 (Africa/Lagos) every UTC stamp was therefore read one hour
    LATE, so age = true_age + 3600s: every entry younger than 2h floored to
    "1 hours ago" — exactly the reported symptom "across all activity".
 3. InMemory preserves Kind=Utc (ctor sets DateTime.UtcNow), whose "O" form
    DOES end in Z — so every prior sandbox E2E was blind to it. FOURTH
    InMemory blind spot after §84's missing migration + stale snapshot and
    §85's query-translation defect.
 4. Secondary: the formatter always pluralised ("1 minutes ago", "1 hours
    ago").

FIXES (minimal, behaviour-preserving apart from the corrections):
 - Views/AdminActivity/_Rows.cshtml: both machine-read timestamps
   (time[datetime] and data-history-cutoff) now emit
   System.DateTime.SpecifyKind(value, Utc).ToString("O") — explicit UTC ISO
   ending in Z regardless of the materialised Kind. Display/title strings
   unchanged.
 - wwwroot/js/business-activity.js: times() now goes through parseUtc()
   (an offset-less ISO stamp is normalised as UTC before Date.parse —
   defence in depth so even legacy/stale markup renders correctly in ANY
   timezone) and ageText() (NaN-safe; correct singular/plural: "1 minute
   ago" / "2 minutes ago" / "1 hour ago" / "2 hours ago"). Buckets and the
   >=24h absolute-UTC fallback (el.title) are UNCHANGED.

REGRESSION PIN (tests/.../Controllers/BusinessActivityTests.cs):
 ActivityRowsEmitUnambiguousUtcIso_EvenWhenTimestampKindIsUnspecified —
 inserts an AuditLog row via Describe(occurredUtc: SpecifyKind(now-30min,
 Unspecified)) (the exact SQL-materialised shape), GETs /admin/activity
 ?format=rows as owner, and asserts every datetime attribute AND the
 data-history-cutoff end with Z and round-trip the instant. Run against the
 unfixed template FIRST: it FAILED (repro at the emission layer); after the
 fix: GREEN. Suite is now 617/617 (325 unit + 292 integration).

E2E (new e2e/activity/timestamps.py — final run TAG=fts2, ALL 12 CHECKS):
 runs in Playwright timezone_id='Africa/Lagos' (the owner's UTC+1 — the
 defect's exact environment). (a) real rows: every datetime attr ends Z,
 parses within minutes of now (no +1h skew), visible text is
 "Just now"/"N minutes ago", never "1 hours"; (b) full-reload persistence:
 same guarantees hold; (c) the real page formatter (times() via its own 15s
 tick) against controlled stamps shaped exactly like the old template's
 output (offset-less ISO) and Z ISO at the bucket boundaries — 30s legacy ->
"Just now" (THE defect vector), 90s legacy -> "1 minute ago", 1h01m legacy
 -> "1 hour ago", 2h30m -> "2 hours ago", 25h -> absolute UTC fallback.
 Connected regression: e2e/activity/workflow.py ALL 42 CHECKS on a fresh
 slate (screenshots redirected outside the repo; log ships).
 Evidence/ (THIS fix run only — prior keys-run shots removed, no mixed
 runs): 01 live rows in Lagos, 02 reload persistence, 03 age buckets
 singular/plural matrix, 04 absolute 25h fallback + the three final logs
 (suite, 42-check regression, 12-check feature run). Shots 01 and 03
 visually reviewed (rows read "Just now"; matrix shows Just now / 1 minute
 ago / 1 hour ago / 2 hours ago / 2026-09-22 12:34:56 UTC).

TEST-HARNESS INCIDENTS THIS PASS (methodology, not product defects):
 - §78's warning bit again: two PARALLEL edit_file calls on the same
   (timestamps.py) file raced and silently dropped one insert. Caught by
   the post-edit grep verify; re-inserted sequentially and re-ran
   everything. NEVER parallel-edit one file — sequential + grep verify.
 - Do not pipe long E2E runs through `head` (SIGPIPE kills the runner
   mid-suite). Capture full output to a file, inspect afterwards.
 - workflow.py's "Historical-only price event waits for explicit refresh"
   check requires a FRESH InMemory slate (rows from a previous run on the
   same host trip it). Restart the app between full runs (§78 note 2).

OBSERVED SIBLING PATTERN (not changed — out of the reported scope):
 wwwroot/js/ui.js timeAgo() and workspace-chat.js build relative times from
 new Date(iso). System.Text.Json emits Z for Kind=Utc values, so the normal
 path is safe; a Kind=Unspecified value serialized to JSON would carry no
 suffix and skew the same way in non-UTC browsers. If a similar "off by an
 hour" report ever lands on chat/notifications, apply the parseUtc pattern.

IDEMPOTENCY GUARD (standing owner directive): if this exact timestamp-fix
request arrives again AND the template/JS fixes are present AND the pin is
green AND the committed verified zip exists, reply with exactly:
"this has been done".

2026-09-23 — PAYSTACK PAYMENT INTEGRATION AUDIT (decisions A-G)
================================================================================
Full audit of the Paystack lifecycle (init -> hosted checkout -> callback ->
webhook -> verify -> paid -> receipt/invoice/emails -> lifetime spending ->
refunds) against current Paystack docs. The existing architecture (server-side
IPaymentGateway, per-attempt Payment records, order status machine) was kept;
verified end-to-end and fixed what was broken. Details + problem table in
PAYSTACK-PAYMENTS-AUDIT.md at the repo root.

FINDINGS/CHANGES (A-G + P1 CSP):
- P1 CRITICAL: CSP form-action 'self' blocked the hosted-checkout redirect —
  payment could never start in a browser. SecurityHeadersMiddleware now
  allowlists the configured Paystack:ApiBaseUrl origin (prod =
  https://checkout.paystack.com).
- A: provider status map success->Succeeded; failed|abandoned|reversed->Failed
  (no throw); pending|processing|queued|ongoing->Pending. Transport/HTTP/JSON
  verify errors -> Pending (NEVER paid), order stays retryable.
- B: ownership-first: unknown/foreign reference -> NotFound BEFORE any gateway
  call (zero provider traffic for probes).
- C (refund boundary): acceptance-based Refunded + ReverseSpending(GrandTotal,
  floor 0). Paystack refund acceptance != settlement; NO refund webhooks and NO
  PaymentRefundedEvent exist (any earlier text implying one is superseded —
  the only payment domain event is PaymentFailedEvent). Admin copy + activity
  entry say "final settlement is not confirmed here".
- D: MarkFailed guard — Succeeded is terminal except Refunded.
- E: InitializePayment rejects customerPayable<=0 (Conflict) and paid orders
  ("already being paid" message).
- F: Paystack:Channels passthrough (empty = all channels enabled on the
  Paystack account; valid values per docs: card, bank, apple_pay, ussd, qr,
  mobile_money, bank_transfer, eft, capitec_pay, payattitude). Complete.cshtml
  copy names card/bank transfer/USSD/mobile money + "any other option Paystack
  enables for this store".
- G: webhook currency check (null->skip): foreign-currency events are ignored;
  amount+currency validated SERVER-SIDE against the order (mismatch -> Failed,
  "Amount/Currency mismatch").

E2E (e2e/payments/{paystack_stub.py,workflow.py}): 62/62 checks over 5 orders
+ refund — declined->retry->success on the same order (fresh PAY- reference per
attempt), webhook-only confirm (browser closed), duplicate callbacks/webhooks/
late second-attempt verification idempotent (one receipt, spending once),
forged signature confirms nothing, async-channel pending -> settle (distinct
pending UI: "you have not been marked paid yet … not charged twice"), abandoned
never paid, unknown + foreign references safe (shot 18 shows the not-found
page), amount+currency mismatch never paid, paid order rejects a new payment,
refund + spending reversal (spending 3x9500-9500), second refund refused with
"Only a paid order can be refunded" (the action redirects with the message —
assert the rendered text, not a status code), exactly one gateway refund
request, and the admin Business activity log records payment.initiated /
payment.verification failed / payment.succeeded / the refund correctly.
NOT TESTABLE in-sandbox (no external network): real Paystack initialize/
verify/refund traffic, 3DS/bank-USSD sessions, actual refund settlement,
Paystack's webhook retry cadence, live fee rounding.

TESTS: 650/650 (350 unit + 300 integration, incl.
PaymentLifecycleTests.Currency_mismatch_is_never_confirmed_on_verify_or_webhook).

EVIDENCE: evidence/ = 01-23 ordered screenshots + final-payments-e2e-log.txt
+ final-test-log.txt, from the ONE clean final run against the committed code
(superseded shots/logs removed). Screenshot ground truth was double-checked via
pixel signatures (sidebar active-nav markers) after image-preview tooling
misassociated files — the PNGs themselves are correct.

2026-09-23 — SERVICE FEE (owner directive)
================================================================================
WHAT: service fee = Y% of the Paystack transaction fee charged to the customer
on top of the order amount. Y = ApplicationSettings key "service_fee.percent"
(default 100 = legacy full pass-through), master switch
Paystack:CustomerBorneCharge. Admin -> Settings -> Service Fee with the brief's
two-step confirmed flow (preview dialog "Change Service Fee?" -> explicit
confirm; stale confirmation never overwrites a newer change; generic settings
save cannot touch the key). Parsing/formatting in
Application/Common/ServiceFeeSettings.cs (0–100, 2 dp max, rejects NaN/Inf).
FORMULA (Infrastructure/Payments/PaystackChargeCalculator.cs):
ComputeCustomerPayable: T = (O + y*100)/(1 − y*0.015)  [cap zone: T = O + y*2000]
ServiceFee S = T − O  ==  y * fee(T),  fee(T) = 1.5%*T + 100  (flat waived below
a 2,500 charge, fee cap 2,000 local). Compute(y=100) ==
ComputeCustomerBorneCharge/ComputeCustomerPayable (legacy identical).
Golden: 9500@100 -> 9746.20 (S=246.20); 9500@50 -> 9622.17 (122.17);
9500@25 -> 9560.85 (60.85); 12000@25 -> 12070.27 (70.27); 125900@100 ->
127900.00 (cap gap 2,000); 1000@100 -> 1015.23 (flat waived).
Snapshots on Payment/Receipt: OrderAmount/ServiceFeePercent/ServiceFeeAmount/
PaystackFeeExpected/PaystackFeeActual (migration
20260924002228_AddServiceFeeSnapshot + history backfill to 100%).
POLISH (same day): Payment's money/percent scalars (CustomerBorneCharge,
OrderAmount, ServiceFeePercent, PaystackFeeExpected, PaystackFeeActual) now
carry explicit HasColumnType("decimal(18,2)") — EF's "no store type for
decimal" warnings are gone. Order.Kind gained .HasSentinel((OrderKind)255):
'Direct' is the CLR default and, without a sentinel, EF omitted the column on
insert so the database default (Custom) could silently win on SQL Server —
direct orders must never be recorded as custom. Migration renamed/re-authored
20260923230818 -> 20260924002228 (same schema + backfill SQL). Verified:
0 model-build warnings of either class, 724/724 tests re-run afterwards.
COPY: customer sees "Service fee (added to your payment)" and "You will pay" /
"incl. … service fee (Y%)" — never "Paystack processing fee"; admin sees
"Paystack fee (expected / charged)". Receipts show Order total / Service fee /
Amount paid. Audit: "Service fee configuration changed" + "from X% to Y%" +
"Applies to future transactions" (BusinessActivityCapture interceptor;
AuditLog.Summary holds the detail). Payment snapshots render in AdminOrders
payment card, _ReceiptDocument (incl. /i/{token} public receipt) and
PortalPayments. Retries RE-PRICE at the current config (never snapshot the
first attempt). Refunds = full amount incl. fee as one charge reversal;
lifetime spending counts ORDER values net of refunds; receipts keep the
applied split forever (historical immutability).
CAUGHT + FIXED during E2E: StoreControllerBase.PublicKeys whitelisted the
storefront settings keys but NOT service_fee.percent -> checkout silently
priced at the 100% default while the admin page (direct DB read) showed the
configured value. service_fee.percent is now whitelisted there.
TESTS: 724/724 (394 unit + 330 integration). New: ServiceFeeSettingsTests,
ServiceFeeChargeCalculatorTests (golden values + OracleFee invariant ±0.02),
ServiceFeeFeatureTests (config group + transaction group). FakePaymentGateway
gained VerifyFees (6th ctor arg of PaymentVerificationResult).
E2E: e2e/service-fee/workflow.py -> 54/54 checks; evidence/ = 17 numbered
shots (final-service-fee-e2e-log.txt at repo root). Task-4 Direct Checkout
evidence preserved at e2e/direct-checkout/evidence/.

================================================================================
90) update-database FAILS: "Column name 'ActivityType' in table 'AuditLogs' is
    specified more than once" (migration 20260923120646_updatedMigration) [2026-09-25]
================================================================================
SYMPTOM: `update-database` against SQL Server stops at
   20260923120646_updatedMigration with SqlException 2705 on
   ALTER TABLE [AuditLogs] ADD [ActivityType] nvarchar(80) NULL.
ROOT CAUSE: 20260923120646_updatedMigration is a byte-for-byte duplicate of
   20260922040450_AddBusinessActivityAudit's schema part (same 15 AuditLogs
   columns + 8 indexes, minus the backfill SQL). It was scaffolded from a model
   snapshot that did not yet contain the audit migration. EF applies pending
   migrations in id order, so on any database where AddBusinessActivityAudit
   (or database/20260922-BusinessActivity-Upgrade.sql) already ran, the
   duplicate ALTER TABLE fails. A brand-new database failed the same way.
FIX (in code, this commit):
   - Both migrations' Up() now use guarded T-SQL:
       IF COL_LENGTH(N'[AuditLogs]', N'<col>') IS NULL ALTER TABLE ... ADD ...
       IF NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM sys.indexes WHERE name=... ) CREATE INDEX ...
     so whichever of the two runs second is a no-op. The backfill/permission SQL
     in AddBusinessActivityAudit is unchanged.
   - updatedMigration.Down() is now empty (the objects are owned by
     AddBusinessActivityAudit, whose Down() drops them). Designer files / model
     snapshot untouched, so `dotnet ef migrations has-pending-model-changes`
     still reports none.
   - The migration was NOT deleted: databases that already recorded
     20260923120646_updatedMigration in __EFMigrationsHistory stay valid.
WHAT THE OWNER DOES: pull, rebuild, run `update-database` again — it completes
   (the two guarded migrations skip existing objects, then AddOrderKind and
   AddServiceFeeSnapshot apply). No manual SQL needed. Optional manual unblock
   without rebuilding: database/20260925-FixDuplicateAuditMigration.sql
   (idempotent; adds missing objects and records both migration ids).
VERIFIED HERE: solution builds; `dotnet ef migrations script` output for
   20260922040450 and 20260923120646 inspected (all guarded); full test suite
   green. No SQL Server instance exists in this sandbox, so the actual
   `update-database` against SQL Server could not be executed here — the
   guarded statements are plain T-SQL patterns identical to the ones in the
   already-proven database/20260922-BusinessActivity-Upgrade.sql.

§92 DEVELOPER PORTAL (2026-09-26)
================================================================================
A) WHAT: a third portal at /dev (separate from /portal and /admin) for technical
   monitoring, diagnostics, safe maintenance and service-fee reconciliation.
   Assessment (read first): DEV_PORTAL_ARCHITECTURE_ASSESSMENT.md.
   Report: DEV_PORTAL_REPORT.md.
B) ACCESS: five permissions dev.portal.view / dev.logs.view / dev.payments.view /
   dev.servicefees.view / dev.maintenance.run (Shared/Constants/Permissions.cs,
   Permissions.Developer). They are NOT in Permissions.All(), so the Owner does not
   inherit them; PermissionPolicyProvider accepts them via Permissions.IsKnown().
   The Developer role (RoleNames.Developer) holds only these. The Owner can grant
   it via users.assignroles. The role-permission editor hides dev.* ids and
   UpdateRolePermissionsHandler preserves them. DevControllerBase = policy + live
   DB re-check + [DevPermission].
   Seed: SeedOptions.DeveloperEmail/DeveloperPassword; idempotent by email; never
   takes over a non-developer account. Sandbox: developer@dechimexprinthub.local /
   DevPortal#2026! (appsettings.Development.json). Production: set
   Seed__DeveloperEmail and Seed__DeveloperPassword via env/secrets (base
   appsettings ships them blank). Outside Development a password change is forced
   at first login.
C) DATA: migration 20260925232100_AddDeveloperPortalDiagnostics (additive) adds
   TechnicalEvents, IntegrationCalls and RequestMetricBuckets.
   Write path: TechnicalEventSink (Serilog) / IntegrationCallRecorder /
   RequestMetrics -> TechnicalEventRecorder (redaction via SensitiveDataRedactor:
   value patterns + sensitive key names + Luhn card numbers) -> DiagnosticsQueue
   (bounded) -> DiagnosticsWriterService (2 s batches). Retention 30 d, applied
   hourly (DiagnosticsRetention). The Payments table IS the fee ledger
   (ServiceFeeReportService).
D) FIXES FOUND BY THE E2E: (1) Invoice/Receipt shared Money (EF owned type)
   instances with Order/Payment, causing an EF "same entity tracked as different
   entity types" warning on every save. Fixed with Money.Copy(). (2) The sink now
   ignores application/domain rule exceptions and OperationCanceledException that
   come from the MediatR LoggingBehavior (expected outcomes, not faults).
   (3) Card-number redaction requires Luhn and skips yyyyMMdd timestamps, so
   PAY-2026… references stay searchable. (4) The dev portal shows an external
   check's connectivity failure as Unavailable; the public /health mapping is
   unchanged (Degraded). (5) TechnicalEventRecorder read the ambient HttpContext; work
   that outlives its request (email queue Task.Run) hit a recycled context, which
   threw, and the silent catch lost the event (seen once: the Brevo outage error).
   Now RequestSnapshot() treats a recycled context as "no request", and recorder
   failures are counted (DiagnosticsQueue.CaptureFailures, shown on /dev/health).
   Test: TechnicalEventRecorderTests.
E) VERIFICATION (final clean run "devfinal", e2e/dev-portal/run_final.sh):
   tests 829/829 (454 unit + 375 integration; 748 before this task); public
   /health Healthy; smoke 29/29; checkout regression 60/60; dev-portal journey
   107/107; 0 app error lines; 0 EF shared-owned-entity warnings; diagnostics
   pipeline 0 dropped / 0 failed writes / 0 capture failures; leak sweep clean.
   evidence/ = 30 ordered shots + final-*-log.txt + final-service-fee-export.csv
   + README.md (index of shots and logs).
   The previous (keys) evidence was moved to e2e/provider-keys/evidence-final-run/.
   The Brevo stub gained an opt-in POST /control/mode {"fail":true} outage switch
   (default off). GET /control/mode returns {"fail","failed"}; "failed" counts
   refused sends. The E2E holds the outage until failed>=1 (deterministic).

§95 DEV PORTAL SIGN-IN "OperationCanceledException" + BLANK 404/400 (2026-09-26)
================================================================================
A) REPORT: VS broke with "Exception User-Unhandled System.OperationCanceledException" in
   ServiceFeeReportService.OverviewAsync (dashboard) while signing in to /dev locally.
   CAUSE: browser dropped the in-flight request (double Sign-in click, reload, nav, 30s status
   poll) -> RequestAborted -> EF throws OCE. Middleware already mapped it to 499, but the
   exception left user code through MVC -> VS Just-My-Code "user-unhandled" break + WRN log.
B) FIX: DevControllerBase.UnlessClientAborted(Func<Task<IActionResult>>) catches OCE ONLY
   when HttpContext.RequestAborted.IsCancellationRequested -> StatusCodeResult(499), and
   disables IStatusCodePagesFeature for that response. All 17 async [HttpGet] dev actions
   are "=> UnlessClientAborted(async () => {...})"; the live permission re-check in
   OnActionExecutionAsync has the same guard. POST /dev/maintenance untouched.
   NEW dev GET actions with a CancellationToken should use the same wrapper.
C) SECOND BUG (Program.cs hand-rolled UseStatusCodePages re-execute): the endpoint from pass 1
   was not cleared, so UseRouting re-ran the ORIGINAL action -> blank 404 for NotFound()
   actions (/admin/customers/{missing}) and blank 400 for anti-forgery failures. Now:
   http.SetEndpoint(null); Request.RouteValues.Clear(); Request.Method = GET (ErrorController
   is GET-only and re-applies the status code). Three existing CSRF tests (expect 400) guard it.
D) TESTS: DevPortal/DevPortalClientAbortTests.cs (8; red on old code), DevPortal/
   StatusCodeReExecuteTests.cs (4). Suite 879/879 (459 unit + 420 integration).
E) E2E: e2e/dev-abort/dev_abort_e2e.py (37 checks; env APP_LOG, E2E_SHOTS_DIR). The abort
   storm uses raw sockets with SO_LINGER 0 (RST) and asserts via the app log (499 on the dev
   path, 0 "Handled OperationCanceledException", 0 OCE stack traces, 0 /Error?code=499).
   Pipeline: e2e/fixes/run_final.sh gained PRIMARY=devabort (stage 8b) via
   e2e/dev-abort/run_final.sh; the brand journey now ALWAYS runs (shots to /tmp unless
   PRIMARY=brand); fixes shots only replace e2e/fixes/evidence-final-run when PRIMARY=brand;
   a brand-run evidence/ is moved to e2e/brand/evidence-final-run before a new primary runs.
F) FINAL RUN: tests 879/879; /health Healthy; smoke 29/29; checkout 60/60; brand 245/245;
   design-review 36/36; fixes 39/39; dev-portal 107/107; dev-abort 37/37; unhandled 0; leaks
   clean. Evidence: evidence/ (10 shots). Report: DEV_SIGNIN_ABORT_REPORT.md.

C) ART TOOL: DeChimexPrints/tools/hero-art/extract_hero_alpha.py <src> <out_base> [--sizes 1536x1024,768x512]
   [--no-hint] -> <out_base>.png/.webp (+ -<w> variants); see tools/hero-art/README.md. The hint is the
   rembg isnet-general-use mask, computed in-process (U2NET_HOME=/var/tmp/u2net). It is used ONLY to flip
   enclosed white regions (ring interior, handle holes) to background, and only when the region is also
   flat pure white (mean min channel >= 250, std <= 3, hint < 0.2). Alpha: border-connected near-white
   (denoised min channel >= 244) is background, with a soft luma ramp (L >= 252 -> 0, <= 244 -> 1; covers
   edges and contact shadows); everything else is opaque. Partial pixels are un-premultiplied against white.
   Art rebuilt 2026-09-28 after a sandbox reset: hero 51.5/3.5/45.0 % (opaque/partial/transparent),
   hero-mobile 61.7/2.8/35.5 %, lavender kept (84.6k / 52.1k px).
   Tried, not kept: an isnet>0.9 "restore flooded subject" pass; it restored 0 px on the portrait art.
   DEAD ENDS:
   - rembg/u2net/isnet as the alpha itself: they drop the white pedestals and the white mug;
   - birefnet: OOM;
   - pure colour-to-alpha: the whites become see-through;
   - border flood only: enclosed whites stay opaque.

A) WHAT WAS FOUND (before):
   - ONE customer shell, _CustomerLayout, served every customer-facing page: public
     storefront AND the signed-in portal. Its markup ended with <footer class="cs-foot">
     (brand, Shop/Help/Talk-to-us columns, socials, WhatsApp, studio/pickup addresses,
     legal lines). The footer therefore rendered on the customer dashboard, orders,
     tracking, workspace, profile, addresses, wishlist, cart, checkout, order
     confirmation and payment return too.
   - _PublicLayout and _PortalLayout were pass-throughs to _CustomerLayout, so declaring
     either changed nothing. _AdminLayout (68 views), _AuthLayout (6 views), _DevLayout
     and _Layout rendered no footer at all: admin/auth already behaved correctly.
   - Verified live: a before-audit journey (e2e/footer/footer_e2e.py, FOOTER_MODE=audit)
     recorded "full" on all 30 customer-facing page groups and "none" on admin/auth.
B) WHAT CHANGED (layout architecture only):
   - _CustomerLayout.cshtml: footer is now selected by ViewData["Footer"]
     ("full" default = storefront footer, unchanged markup; "slim" = new one-line legal
     bar with copyright, Privacy/Terms/Contact and the payment line; anything else =
     no footer). Body classes, header, bottom nav, scripts and sections are untouched.

     TRANSACTIONAL shell: Layout = "_CustomerLayout", ViewData["Footer"] = "slim", and
     the active-nav inference it already had. 18 views now declare it instead of
     _CustomerLayout: PortalDashboard/Index, PortalOrders/{Index,Details,Track,Workspace},
     PortalProfile/{Index,ChangePassword}, PortalAddresses/{Index,Add},
     PortalWishlist/Index, PortalPayments/Index, PortalInvoices/Index,
     PortalShop/OrderForm, StoreCart/Index, Checkout/{Index,Complete,NoProfile,Return}.
   - customer.css: .cs-foot-slim styles appended next to the existing .cs-foot block.
     The slim bar sits above the fixed bottom nav (body padding already reserves it),
     wraps on phones, and adds no margin-top, so footer-less pages keep correct spacing
     and short pages have no empty band.
   - UNCHANGED: the storefront footer markup/content/links/classes, the header, the
     bottom nav, admin/auth/dev layouts, _Layout pages (unsubscribe, public invoice,
     MVC skeleton home), all controllers, business logic and data.
   - Placement result: home/shop/products/product/services/gallery/about/contact/faq/

     all /portal/** workspace pages, custom-order form, /cart (guest and signed-in),
     /checkout, /checkout/complete/{id}, /checkout/return -> slim bar; /admin/** and
     /Account/** (login, register, forgot/reset password, access denied, verify) and
     /dev/** -> no footer.

TASK: audit and redesign the whole email UI so emails feel like a natural extension of the

     17 templates + RenderMarketing) already had a component library and a shell, but its
     tokens were an EMAIL-ONLY palette: violet #7C3AED CTA, pink/orange gradient strip
     (#7C3AED -> #EC4899 -> #F97316), lavender page #F6F5FC, purple-grey text #3B3752,
     #1E1B2E ink, #6B6880 muted, #ECEAF5 lines, Segoe UI stack. None of it matched the app.
   - Three more places carried the old language: the admin marketing PREVIEW
     (Views/AdminMarketing/Compose.cshtml), the unsubscribe landing page
     (Views/Unsubscribe/Unsubscribe.cshtml) and the staff reply greeting
     (ContactMessageCommands.cs). Two dormant templates (Otp, DesignProofApproved) have no
     send site (unchanged: no new triggers, no new features).
   - The app's real system (app.css :root + customer.css): warm canvas #F7F6F2, white cards
     with the warm hairline #E7E4DC, ink #17161C, slate text, indigo primary #4338CA
     (#312E81 deep, #EEF2FF soft), status tones from .cs-status, alert tones from .flash,
     Inter stack, radii 8/10/14/18, one restrained --brand-grad, and the .cs-logo lockup
     (indigo tile + real /img/logo.png + DE-CHIMEX / PRINT HUB wordmark).
B) WHAT CHANGED (presentation only; triggers, recipients, routes, queueing untouched):
   - EmailTemplateRenderer.cs rewritten as the app's system in email-safe form: tokens
     mirrored 1:1 (each carries its app CSS variable in the comment), shell = hidden
     preheader -> top bar lockup (40px #312E81 tile + /img/logo.png + wordmark) -> 600px
     white card (r18, 1px --border) on the warm canvas -> body cell (class 'dc-pad dc-body')
     -> slim footer strip (#FCFBF9: "Creativity with Ease." line, Privacy/Terms/Contact/
     support email, copyright, Paystack/NGN line; marketing adds the server-side
     unsubscribe). Components mirror app components: Title=.cs-page-head h1, Lead, Note,
     Rows/.ws-page .table, Total=.ck-sum-row.is-pay, Badge=.cs-status (text + dot),
     Panel=.cs-callout, Message=.chat-bubble.theirs, Code=.cs-body .input token field,
     Alert=.flash/alert tones with labels, Signature=.card-head divider,
     Button=.btn-primary large (bgcolor #4338CA, r12, full width <=620px, URL fallback).
     Contract preserved: {{Key}} encoded unless key starts Raw, {{#Key}} blocks, subjects
     substituted unencoded, same model keys at every call site (a new placeholder a caller
     does not send was caught and removed). Plain text now keeps each action URL inline.
   - Contact intake/reply: no inline palette colours left (the shell owns them);
     Compose.cshtml preview rebuilt to mirror the real email (canvas, lockup, r18 card,
     slim footer with unsubscribe); Unsubscribe.cshtml uses the app lockup with app tokens.
   - Tests: EmailTemplateRendererTests locks updated (app primary, "Creativity with Ease.")
     plus new locks (plain-text link reachability, no em/en dashes, previews write .txt);
     new EmailDesignSystemTests re-reads app.css/customer.css and fails on any token that is
     not the app's, on the retired palette, or on a non-Inter stack.
   - Other harnesses: the shared verification-code extractor
     (ws_designreview_e2e.py + the proof/placeholder/PWA copies, verification_code()) no
     longer assumes the FIRST 6-digit run is the code, because palette tokens such as
     #475569 are all digits; it prefers the renderer's code block and falls back to the first
     6-digit run that is not part of a hex colour. (This kept checkout/design-review/PWA
     journeys working against the new HTML.)
   - Pipeline: stage 8h added to e2e/fixes/run_final.sh (PRIMARY=emailux, new
     e2e/email-ux/run_final.sh); the evidence archive guards were rewritten to identify a
     previous run by a SHOT name unique to its primary journey (stage log names are not
     unique: every full run writes every regression log), with a new guard for the email run.

§103 VISUAL MARKETING CAMPAIGN BUILDER [2026-10-06]
================================================================================
REQUEST: add an Owner/Manager campaign builder to the DeChimex Hub, reusing its
provider, customer/consent data, send-job, audit, and UI conventions. Keep the
campaign editor separate from transactional email and do not expose raw markup,
CSS, JSON, API settings, or provider-specific controls to staff. First provide
an architecture assessment, then implement, verify, capture final evidence,
archive the old evidence, package, and commit.

A) ARCHITECTURE ASSESSMENT (shared before implementation)
- Existing reusable foundations: role/permission policies, customer records and
  marketing-consent/unsubscribe state, audit logging, the email renderer and
  provider abstraction, transaction/outbox/job processing, and Hub admin
  layout/conventions. Transactional email remains on its existing distinct path.
- Existing provider integration reports acceptance of a send request, not
  delivery, open, or click events. UI statistics therefore use “Accepted by
  email service” and do not imply downstream delivery/engagement.
- New work was scoped to campaign draft/recipient persistence, consent-safe
  audience segments, controlled block composition and starter templates,
  preview/test-send/confirmation/scheduling workflow, background delivery,
  unsubscribe handling, campaign views, and tests. No Brevo-specific UI or
  unrestricted HTML/CSS authoring was added.

B) IMPLEMENTATION
- Added/extended marketing application commands and queries, campaign persistence,
  audience eligibility, consent rechecks at confirmation/delivery, provider-backed
  test sends and campaign sends, explicit send confirmation, scheduling, outcome
  reporting, and tokenized unsubscribe flow.
- The staff editor offers supported segments only (all subscribed, previous
  purchasers, and no paid purchase in 90 days), current eligible counts, four
  starter templates, and reorderable heading/text/image/button/divider blocks.
  Personalization safely falls back when a first name is unavailable. Raw HTML,
  CSS, JSON, provider/API settings and unsupported audience criteria are not
  editor controls.
- Desktop/mobile previews use the delivery renderer; drafts persist and can be
  edited; test sends are isolated from campaign recipients; sends require an
  explicit audience confirmation and can be queued immediately or scheduled.
  Consent is checked again when sending starts and before recipients are sent.
- Main implementation areas: `src/DeChimex.Application/Features/Marketing/`,
  `src/DeChimex.Host/Controllers/Mvc/Admin/AdminMarketingController.cs`,
  `src/DeChimex.Host/Views/AdminMarketing/`, marketing infrastructure and
  persistence, `tests/DeChimex.UnitTests/`,
  `tests/DeChimex.IntegrationTests/Controllers/MarketingBroadcastTests.cs`, and
  `e2e/marketing-builder/`.

C) FINAL-REVIEW FIXES
- The Details-page preview initially embedded an authenticated network route,
  which the app's global `X-Frame-Options: DENY` correctly prevents from loading
  in an iframe. Changed the details view to receive rendered email HTML from the
  same delivery preview query and place it in an attribute-encoded `srcdoc`
  iframe with the restrictive sandbox (no script permission). This retains the
  global anti-framing header. Integration and browser E2E checks verify the
  rendered campaign appears there and block text stays encoded.
- Browser scheduling uses minute-precision `datetime-local` input. The E2E now
  rounds above the one-minute minimum and selects a minute-precision value,
  avoiding Chromium's rejection of second-bearing fill values.

D) FINAL VALIDATION (after code and test fixes)
- Solution build succeeded with 0 errors.
- Full suite passed: 480 unit + 441 integration = 921, zero failed/skipped.
- Fresh browser run `marketingfinal`: 35/35 checks. Covered Owner access,

## Notification read-state audit and verification — 2026-10-06

### Original behavior observed
- `Notification.IsRead` was already the persisted per-row state; creation defaulted new notifications to unread, and the existing notification query/mark-read path already scoped customer rows by recipient and staff rows by role permission.
- The customer bell fetched recent rows without marking them read merely by opening it, but selecting a row sent a fire-and-forget read request, so navigation could beat persistence. There was no full customer history page or explicit bulk-read action.
- The staff history exposed a separate Mark read action, but lacked an Open action that persisted one row before following its existing link; there was no explicit bulk-read action.
- SignalR delivery incremented local badge state without row-ID deduplication/reconciliation on each event. Web Push stored the destination URL but did not carry the persisted notification ID back to the server on click.

### Focused changes
- Added a portal notification history page and explicit, anti-forgery-protected mark-all actions for portal and staff. Bulk updates use the existing `IsRead` field, only affect rows visible to the authenticated user/permissions, and keep rows in history.
- Individual portal/staff Open paths now persist just the selected unread row before navigating. Already-read rows remain idempotent. Dropdown/history reads remain read-only; unread counts are reconciled against persisted server counts.
- Added persisted notification IDs to Web Push click data so the service worker can keep the original deep link and request the same idempotent read update. SignalR events remain unread, are deduplicated by ID, and trigger count reconciliation.
- Added/extended unit, MVC integration, and focused browser coverage. No separate “seen” state, table, duplicate API, role change, schema migration, or business-event rewrite was introduced.

### Final behavior and validation
- Opening dropdown/full history does not change read state; one-item selection decrements count by one and preserves history; already-read selection does not write again; explicit mark-all is scoped and retains history.
- Tests: solution build succeeded with 0 errors; unit suite **486/486 passed**; integration suite **443/443 passed**; final fresh browser run `notifrunfi` **48/48 passed**. Ordered screenshots and the same-run log are in root `evidence/`.
- The final sandbox log contained CORS-policy warning entries for the local `127.0.0.1` origin, but notification SignalR connections upgraded successfully and the live-delivery assertions passed. Check production origin allowlists separately if the deployment host differs.

### Existing model limitation
Staff-audience notifications without a direct recipient retain one shared row-level `IsRead` bit. Staff members who can see the same broadcast therefore share that read state; a per-user receipt model would require architecture the task explicitly excluded. Provider credentials in both appsettings files were left in place for packaging; none were copied into logs or this handoff.

### Isolated committed-tree verification
A separate clone of commit `7e6f905` built successfully (0 errors, 16 repository warnings); its unit suite passed 473/473 and the notification-filtered integration suite passed 23/23. The full integration suite in that isolated clone hit one unrelated `DevPortalDiagnosticsTests` Paystack-health assertion (`Healthy` expected, `Unavailable` observed) and the test host then aborted. The full workspace integration run with the existing local test configuration passed 443/443; the notification-filtered clean-clone run had no failures. No unrelated diagnostics behavior was changed.
